`collectors/behavior-probe/src/main/kotlin/com/detectany/collectors/behavior/BehaviorCollector.kt`:

```kt
package com.detectany.collectors.behavior

import com.detectany.core.model.Collector
import com.detectany.core.model.CollectorId
import com.detectany.core.model.CollectorResult
import com.detectany.core.model.Confidence
import com.detectany.core.model.Finding
import com.detectany.core.model.ScanInput
import com.detectany.core.nativeplatform.JniSyscallBackend
import com.detectany.core.nativeplatform.ProbeJni
import com.detectany.core.nativeplatform.TriplePathExecutor

/**
 * C8 接口行为（任务 3.8）：KSU reboot supercall / APatch syscall45 / 旧 prctl /
 * SELinux errno 语义 / 三路 diff / NativeBridgeError / ptrace 注入残留。
 * 全部 magic/参数可经特征数据下发（Phase 1 用内置默认值）。
 */
class BehaviorCollector(
    private val ksuMagic1: Long = KSU_MAGIC1,
    private val ksuMagic2: Long = KSU_MAGIC2,
    private val apatchSuperkey: String = "su",
    private val apatchHelloCmd: Long = APATCH_HELLO_CMD,
    private val legacyPrctlOption: Long = LEGACY_KSU_OPTION,
    private val tripleExecutor: TriplePathExecutor = TriplePathExecutor(JniSyscallBackend),
) : Collector {

    /** 特征数据装配工厂（specs：magic MUST 来自特征数据，MUST NOT 编译期写死）。 */
    constructor(
        supercalls: List<com.detectany.core.model.SupercallSpec>,
        tripleExecutor: TriplePathExecutor = TriplePathExecutor(JniSyscallBackend),
    ) : this(
        ksuMagic1 = supercalls.firstOrNull { it.family == "ksu_reboot" }?.magic1 ?: KSU_MAGIC1,
        ksuMagic2 = supercalls.firstOrNull { it.family == "ksu_reboot" }?.magic2 ?: KSU_MAGIC2,
        apatchSuperkey = supercalls.firstOrNull { it.family == "apatch_supercall" }?.superkey ?: "su",
        apatchHelloCmd = supercalls.firstOrNull { it.family == "apatch_supercall" }?.helloCmd ?: APATCH_HELLO_CMD,
        legacyPrctlOption = supercalls.firstOrNull { it.family == "legacy_ksu_prctl" }?.magic1 ?: LEGACY_KSU_OPTION,
        tripleExecutor = tripleExecutor,
    )

    override val id: CollectorId = CollectorId.C8_BEHAVIOR

    override fun collect(input: ScanInput): CollectorResult = try {
        val findings = mutableListOf<Finding>()
        findings += BehaviorProbes.ksuSupercall(ksuMagic1, ksuMagic2)
        findings += BehaviorProbes.apatchSupercall(apatchSuperkey, apatchHelloCmd)
        findings += BehaviorProbes.legacyPrctl(legacyPrctlOption)
        findings += BehaviorProbes.selinuxLabelSemantics()
        findings += BehaviorProbes.nativebridgeSticky()
        findings += BehaviorProbes.ptraceResidue(tripleExecutor)
        CollectorResult(
            collector = id,
            status = CollectorResult.Status.OK,
            findings = findings,
            meta = BehaviorProbes.lastMeta,
        )
    } catch (e: Throwable) {
        CollectorResult(collector = id, status = CollectorResult.Status.BLOCKED, meta = mapOf("error" to (e.message ?: e.javaClass.simpleName)))
    }

    companion object {
        const val KSU_MAGIC1 = 0xDEADBEEFL
        const val KSU_MAGIC2 = 0xCAFEBABEL

        /** APatch ver_and_cmd：(KP 版本 << 32) | (0x1158 << 16) | SUPERCALL_HELLO(0x1000) */
        const val APATCH_HELLO_CMD = (0x1158L shl 16) or 0x1000L

        /** 旧版 KSU prctl option（v1.0.5 及以前） */
        const val LEGACY_KSU_OPTION = 0xDEADBEEFL

        /** SELinux 探测 label 集（PayPal 2026 向量） */
        val SELINUX_PROBE_LABELS = mapOf(
            "magisk" to "u:r:magisk:s0",
            "ksu_file" to "u:r:ksu_file:s0",
            "lsposed" to "u:r:lsposed:s0",
        )
        const val SELINUX_CONTROL_LABEL = "u:r:detectany_nonexistent_control:s0"
    }
}

/** 行为探针（采集层薄封装；判定常量进 Finding，规则层可复核）。 */
object BehaviorProbes {

    internal var lastMeta: Map<String, String> = emptyMap()

    /** KSU 系：reboot 双魔数 → fd 表新增 anon_inode（未授权命令 -EPERM 可区分）。 */
    fun ksuSupercall(magic1: Long, magic2: Long): List<Finding> {
        val out = ProbeJni.ksuProbe(magic1, magic2, 0L, 0L)
        val head = out.firstOrNull() ?: return emptyList()
        val ret = Regex("ret=(-?\\d+)").find(head)?.groupValues?.get(1)?.toLongOrNull()
        val errno = Regex("errno=(-?\\d+)").find(head)?.groupValues?.get(1)?.toIntOrNull()
        val newFds = out.drop(1)
        lastMeta = lastMeta + ("ksuRet" to head)
        return buildList {
            newFds.filter { it.contains("ksu_driver") }.forEach { fd ->
                add(
                    Finding(
                        ruleId = "behavior.ksu_supercall",
                        collector = CollectorId.C8_BEHAVIOR,
                        confidence = Confidence.B,
                        category = Finding.Category.ROOT_KERNEL,
                        framework = "ksu",
                        detail = "reboot 双魔数探针获得 fd: $fd（KSU v2.0+ supercall 安装）",
                        raw = mapOf("fd" to fd, "ret" to (ret?.toString() ?: "?"), "errno" to (errno?.toString() ?: "?")),
                    ),
                )
            }
        }
    }

    /** APatch：syscall 45 + 0x1158 布局；SUPERCALL_HELLO 返回 0x11581158 为强命中。 */
    fun apatchSupercall(superkey: String, helloCmd: Long = BehaviorCollector.APATCH_HELLO_CMD): List<Finding> {
        val r = ProbeJni.apatchProbe(superkey, helloCmd)
        val ret = r[0]
        lastMeta = lastMeta + mapOf("apatchRet" to ret.toString(), "apatchErrno" to r[1].toString(), "apatchNs" to r[2].toString())
        return if (ret == APATCH_HELLO_MAGIC) {
            listOf(
                Finding(
                    ruleId = "behavior.apatch_supercall",
                    collector = CollectorId.C8_BEHAVIOR,
                    confidence = Confidence.B,
                    category = Finding.Category.ROOT_KERNEL,
                    framework = "apatch",
                    detail = "APatch supercall HELLO 返回 0x11581158",
                    raw = mapOf("ret" to ret.toString(16)),
                ),
            )
        } else {
            emptyList() // 无 superkey 时退化为 truncate 失败；计时侧信道进 meta 供规则层统计
        }
    }

    private const val APATCH_HELLO_MAGIC = 0x11581158L

    /**
     * 旧版 KSU（存量老内核）：prctl(0xDEADBEEF)。
     * 原厂内核的裸 svc 返回 -EINVAL 本体（-22，errno 不置位）——API 35 实测；
     * 返回其他值（0/正数/版本号）即为被 hook 的旧版 KSU 存量特征。
     */
    fun legacyPrctl(option: Long = BehaviorCollector.LEGACY_KSU_OPTION): List<Finding> {
        val r = ProbeJni.prctlProbe(option, 2L, 0L, 0L, 0L)
        val ret = r[0]
        lastMeta = lastMeta + mapOf("legacyPrctlRet" to ret.toString(), "legacyPrctlErrno" to r[1].toString())
        return if (ret != -EINVAL.toLong()) {
            listOf(
                Finding(
                    ruleId = "behavior.legacy_prctl",
                    collector = CollectorId.C8_BEHAVIOR,
                    confidence = Confidence.C,
                    category = Finding.Category.ROOT_KERNEL,
                    framework = "ksu",
                    detail = "prctl(0xDEADBEEF) 返回 $ret errno=${r[1]}（原厂应为 -EINVAL；旧版 KSU 存量特征）",
                    raw = mapOf("ret" to ret.toString(), "errno" to r[1].toString()),
                ),
            )
        } else {
            emptyList()
        }
    }

    private const val EINVAL = 22

    /**
     * SELinux errno 语义：EINVAL=label 不存在 / EPERM=label 存在但禁止。
     * 对照 label 必须返回 EINVAL 才认为机制可用（部分 ROM 对 attr 写入有别的行为）。
     */
    fun selinuxLabelSemantics(): List<Finding> {
        val control = ProbeJni.selinuxLabelProbe(BehaviorCollector.SELINUX_CONTROL_LABEL)
        if (control[0] < 0L || control[1].toInt() != EINVAL) return emptyList() // 机制不可用，不采信
        return buildList {
            for ((name, label) in BehaviorCollector.SELINUX_PROBE_LABELS) {
                val r = ProbeJni.selinuxLabelProbe(label)
                if (r[0] < 0L) continue
                if (r[1].toInt() == EPERM) {
                    add(
                        Finding(
                            ruleId = "behavior.selinux_label.$name",
                            collector = CollectorId.C8_BEHAVIOR,
                            confidence = Confidence.B,
                            category = when (name) {
                                "lsposed" -> Finding.Category.HOOK
                                else -> Finding.Category.ROOT_KERNEL
                            },
                            framework = name,
                            detail = "SELinux 域存在: $label（EPERM vs 对照 EINVAL）",
                            raw = mapOf("label" to label, "errno" to "1"),
                        ),
                    )
                }
            }
        }
    }

    private const val EPERM = 1

    /** NativeBridgeError() 粘滞标志（ZN/ReZygisk）。 */
    fun nativebridgeSticky(): List<Finding> {
        val r = ProbeJni.nativebridgeError()
        lastMeta = lastMeta + ("nativebridgeError" to r.toString())
        return if (r == 1) {
            listOf(
                Finding(
                    ruleId = "behavior.nativebridge_sticky",
                    collector = CollectorId.C8_BEHAVIOR,
                    confidence = Confidence.B,
                    category = Finding.Category.ZYGISK,
                    framework = null,
                    detail = "NativeBridgeError()=true（曾尝试加载 NativeBridge，属性还原后仍暴露）",
                    raw = mapOf("hadError" to "true"),
                ),
            )
        } else {
            emptyList()
        }
    }

    /** ptrace 残留 + 三路 diff（父进程 = zygote，eventmsg==zygote pid 即注入）。 */
    fun ptraceResidue(executor: TriplePathExecutor): List<Finding> {
        val findings = mutableListOf<Finding>()
        runCatching {
            val msg = ProbeJni.ptraceParentEventmsg()
            val ppid = readPpid()
            lastMeta = lastMeta + mapOf("ptraceMsg" to msg.toString(), "ppid" to ppid.toString())
            if (msg >= 0 && ppid > 0 && msg == ppid) {
                findings.add(
                    Finding(
                        ruleId = "behavior.ptrace_residue", // A4: NeoZygisk ≥10-30 清除残留，对该分支即将失效；ReZygisk 仍 A 级
                        collector = CollectorId.C8_BEHAVIOR,
                        confidence = Confidence.A,
                        category = Finding.Category.ZYGISK,
                        framework = null,
                        detail = "父进程(zygote=$ppid)存在注入残留 eventmsg（ZN/ReZygisk/NeoZygisk 形态）",
                        raw = mapOf("msg" to msg.toString(), "ppid" to ppid.toString()),
                    ),
                )
            }
        }
        // 三路 diff（接口行为的一致性证据）
        for (path in listOf("/system/lib64/libc.so", "/system/bin/sh", "/data/local/tmp")) {
            runCatching {
                val diff = executor.probe(path)
                diff.svcVsLibc?.let { m ->
                    findings.add(
                        Finding(
                            ruleId = "behavior.triple_diff",
                            collector = CollectorId.C8_BEHAVIOR,
                            confidence = Confidence.A,
                            category = Finding.Category.HOOK,
                            framework = null,
                            detail = "三路 diff 不一致 @ $path: ${m.kind} ${m.detail}",
                            raw = mapOf("path" to path, "kind" to m.kind.name),
                        ),
                    )
                }
            }
        }
        return findings
    }

    private fun readPpid(): Long {
        val stat = runCatching { java.io.File("/proc/self/stat").readText() }.getOrNull() ?: return -1
        // 格式：pid (comm) state ppid ...；comm 可能含空格，取最右括号后第 2 个字段
        val after = stat.substringAfterLast(')')
        return after.trim().split(Regex("\\s+")).getOrNull(1)?.toLongOrNull() ?: -1
    }

    /**
     * 时序侧信道：syscall 45 (truncate) 被 APatch supercall handler hook，
     * 每次调用都做 superkey 字符串比对后才 fall through → 额外开销。
     * 对比 syscall 46 (ftruncate, 未被 hook)：差值 = supercall handler 处理时间。
     *
     * 原厂内核：45 ≈ 46（同为文件系统调用，成本相近）
     * APatch 内核：45 > 46（多出 key 比对开销，典型 100-500ns）
     */
    fun supercallTimingSideChannel(): List<Finding> {
        // ⛔ 已禁用（2026-11-09 差分测量证伪）
        // 差分对照组（newfstatat/fstat）证明：truncate vs ftruncate 的差异
        // 是路径解析成本（~2000ns），不是 supercall 开销。
        // APatch supercall handler 的净开销 < 噪声底（差分结果 -202ns ≈ 0）。
        // 之前的 200ns 阈值把路径解析天然差误判为 supercall → 假阳性。
        // 保留方法体用于未来改进（如纳秒级 TSC 访问），当前不产出 Finding。
        return emptyList()
        /*
        // 模拟器/QEMU 上跳过——虚拟化使所有 syscall 时序不可靠（2026-11-09 模拟器误报实证）
        val isQemu = (System.getProperty("ro.kernel.qemu") == "1") ||
            android.os.Build.FINGERPRINT.contains("generic", true) ||
            android.os.Build.HARDWARE.contains("goldfish", true) ||
            android.os.Build.HARDWARE.contains("ranchu", true) ||
            android.os.Build.PRODUCT.contains("sdk_gphone", true)
        if (isQemu) {
            lastMeta = lastMeta + mapOf("timingSkipped" to "emulator")
            return emptyList()
        }

        val timing = runCatching { ProbeJni.supercallTiming(2000) }.getOrNull()
            ?: return emptyList()

        val truncateNs = timing[0]   // syscall 45 (hooked)
        val ftruncateNs = timing[1]  // syscall 46 (not hooked)
        val getpidNs = timing[2]     // baseline
        val overheadNs = truncateNs - ftruncateNs
        lastMeta = lastMeta + mapOf(
            "timingTruncate" to truncateNs.toString(),
            "timingFtruncate" to ftruncateNs.toString(),
            "timingGetpid" to getpidNs.toString(),
            "timingOverhead" to overheadNs.toString(),
        )

        // APatch 的 superkey 比对至少要做一次字符串比较 + 分支判断
        // 阈值：overhead > 200ns 且 > getpid 的 2 倍（排除调度噪声）
        // 这不需要 manager 在装——内核里的 hook 每次调用都在
        val thresholdNs = 200L
        val isSignificant = overheadNs > thresholdNs && overheadNs > getpidNs * 2

        return if (isSignificant) {
            listOf(
                Finding(
                    ruleId = "behavior.supercall_timing",
                    collector = CollectorId.C8_BEHAVIOR,
                    confidence = Confidence.B,
                    category = Finding.Category.ROOT_KERNEL,
                    framework = "apatch",
                    detail = "syscall 45 比 46 慢 ${overheadNs}ns（阈值 ${thresholdNs}ns）",
                    raw = mapOf(
                        "truncateNs" to truncateNs.toString(),
                        "ftruncateNs" to ftruncateNs.toString(),
                        "getpidNs" to getpidNs.toString(),
                        "overheadNs" to overheadNs.toString(),
                    ),
                ),
            )
        } else emptyList()
        */
    }

}
```

`collectors/behavior-probe/src/test/kotlin/com/detectany/collectors/behavior/BehaviorLogicTest.kt`:

```kt
package com.detectany.collectors.behavior

import kotlin.test.Test
import kotlin.test.assertEquals

/** 任务 3.8 主机侧验证：/proc/self/stat 的 ppid 解析（ptrace 残留比对的前置逻辑）。 */
class BehaviorLogicTest {

    @Test
    fun parsesPpidWithSpacedComm() {
        // comm 可含空格与括号 —— 必须取最后一个 ')' 之后；字段序：state ppid pgrp...
        val stat = "4728 (Some Process) S 731 4728 710 0 -1 1077952832"
        val ppidField = stat.substringAfterLast(')').trim().split(Regex("\\s+"))[1]
        assertEquals("731", ppidField)
    }

    @Test
    fun apatchHelloCmdLayout() {
        // ver_and_cmd = (0x1158 << 16) | 0x1000 —— 与 KernelPatch scdefs 布局一致
        val cmd = BehaviorCollector.APATCH_HELLO_CMD
        assertEquals(0x1158L shl 16 or 0x1000L, cmd)
        assertEquals(0x11581000L, cmd)
    }

    @Test
    fun ksuMagics() {
        assertEquals(0xDEADBEEFL, BehaviorCollector.KSU_MAGIC1)
        assertEquals(0xCAFEBABEL, BehaviorCollector.KSU_MAGIC2)
    }
}
```

`collectors/code-integrity/src/main/kotlin/com/detectany/collectors/codeintegrity/CodeIntegrityCollector.kt`:

```kt
package com.detectany.collectors.codeintegrity

import com.detectany.core.model.Collector
import com.detectany.core.model.CollectorId
import com.detectany.core.model.CollectorResult
import com.detectany.core.model.Confidence
import com.detectany.core.model.Finding
import android.content.Context
import com.detectany.core.model.ScanInput
import com.detectany.core.nativeplatform.ElfJni
import com.detectany.core.nativeplatform.NativePlatform
import com.detectany.core.nativeplatform.ProbeJni
import com.detectany.collectors.memmap.MapsParser
import java.util.zip.ZipFile

/**
 * C7 指令完整性（任务 3.7）：
 * 1) 自身 native 库全部导出入口 prologue 的磁盘 vs 内存字节比对（校验清单=采集器入口）；
 * 2) 自身 GOT 槽位解析地址 vs 目标库磁盘 st_value+基址（无 dlsym 依赖）。
 */
class CodeIntegrityCollector(
    private val ownLib: () -> OwnLibPath? = { locateOwnLib() },
    private val mapsReader: () -> List<String> = { java.io.File("/proc/self/maps").readLines() },
) : Collector {

    /** 自身库定位：磁盘路径（ELF 解析用）+ 进程内加载基址。 */
    data class OwnLibPath(val diskPath: String, val base: Long)

    override val id: CollectorId = CollectorId.C7_CODE_INTEGRITY

    override fun collect(input: ScanInput): CollectorResult {
        return try {
            val loc = ownLib()
                ?: return CollectorResult(collector = id, status = CollectorResult.Status.DEGRADED, meta = mapOf("reason" to "own lib not located"))
            val maps = MapsParser.parse(mapsReader())
            val findings = IntegrityAnalyzer.checkPrologues(loc.diskPath, loc.base) +
                IntegrityAnalyzer.checkGot(loc.diskPath, loc.base, maps)
            CollectorResult(
                collector = id,
                status = CollectorResult.Status.OK,
                findings = findings,
                meta = mapOf("lib" to loc.diskPath, "base" to loc.base.toString()),
            )
        } catch (e: Throwable) {
            CollectorResult(collector = id, status = CollectorResult.Status.BLOCKED, meta = mapOf("error" to (e.message ?: e.javaClass.simpleName)))
        }
    }

    companion object {
        internal const val LIB_NAME = "libdetectany_native.so"

        /**
         * 定位自身库（两种打包形态）：
         * 1) extractNativeLibs=true：maps 中有 .../lib/arm64/libdetectany_native.so —— 磁盘=映射路径；
         * 2) extractNativeLibs=false（AGP 默认）：库直接从 APK 内加载，maps 只见 base.apk ——
         *    磁盘副本从 APK zip 抽出，**基址用自校验投票**：在 base.apk 的各映射段起点找 ELF 魔数，
         *    再以已知符号「磁盘 vs 内存」字节比对确认。
         *
         * 2026-11-06 Pixel 8 实测教训：dl_iterate_phdr 的 dlpi_addr 对 APK 内嵌库在主 app 进程
         * 返回错误 bias（androidTest 进程恰好正确，掩盖缺陷）—— memRead 落零页、prologue 全部
         * 假阳性（mem=全零 而非蹦床指令）。故 dlpi_addr 仅作最后兜底。
         */
        fun locateOwnLib(context: Context? = null): OwnLibPath? {
            NativePlatform.ensureLoaded()
            val maps = MapsParser.parse(java.io.File("/proc/self/maps").readLines())

            // 路径 1（最可靠）：maps 中直接有 libdetectany_native.so
            maps.firstOrNull { it.path?.endsWith(LIB_NAME) == true }?.let {
                return OwnLibPath(it.path!!, it.start)
            }

            // 路径 2：solist（linker 权威 load bias）+ 符号字节验证
            // 注：phdr 投票在模拟器和真机上均产生假阳性（2026-11-09），
            //     因为多个 APK 内嵌 so 可能通过魔数+phdr 双检查 → 已移除。
            //     solist 是 linker 维护的权威地址，用已知符号字节做最终验证。
            val disk = context?.let { extractFromApk(it) } ?: guessDiskPath(maps) ?: return null
            val diskBytes = runCatching { java.io.File(disk).readBytes() }.getOrNull() ?: return null

            val solistEntry = ProbeJni.solist().firstOrNull { it.substringBefore('\t').endsWith(LIB_NAME) }
            val solistBase = solistEntry?.substringAfter('\t')?.toLongOrNull()
            if (solistBase == null || solistBase <= 0) return null

            // 验证：读一个已知符号的 prologue 字节（disk vs memory）
            // 用 textRead（mprotect 方式，处理 execute-only 页）
            val verifySym = "Java_com_detectany_core_nativeplatform_SyscallJni_statSvc"
            val verifyVaddr = runCatching { ElfJni.exportValue(disk, verifySym) }.getOrDefault(0L)
            if (verifyVaddr == 0L) return null
            val verifyDisk = runCatching { ElfJni.readAtVaddr(disk, verifyVaddr, 16) }.getOrNull() ?: return null
            val verifyMem = runCatching { ProbeJni.textRead(solistBase + verifyVaddr, 16) }.getOrNull()

            return if (verifyMem != null && verifyMem.contentEquals(verifyDisk)) {
                OwnLibPath(disk, solistBase) // 字节匹配 = base 正确
            } else {
                android.util.Log.w("C7", "locateOwnLib 符号验证失败 disk=$disk solistBase=$solistBase memNull=${verifyMem == null}")
                null // 不匹配 = solist 有误或被 hook → 宁可不检出也不假阳性
            }
        }

        private fun leInt(b: ByteArray, off: Int): Long {
            var v = 0L
            for (i in 3 downTo 0) v = (v shl 8) or (b[off + i].toLong() and 0xFF)
            return v
        }

        private fun leLong(b: ByteArray, off: Int): Long {
            var v = 0L
            for (i in 7 downTo 0) v = (v shl 8) or (b[off + i].toLong() and 0xFF)
            return v
        }

        private fun guessDiskPath(maps: List<com.detectany.collectors.memmap.MapEntry>): String? =
            maps.firstNotNullOfOrNull { m -> m.path?.takeIf { it.endsWith("/base.apk") } }

        private fun extractFromApk(context: Context): String? = runCatching {
            val ai = context.applicationInfo
            val existing = java.io.File(ai.nativeLibraryDir, LIB_NAME)
            if (existing.isFile) return existing.absolutePath
            // 缓存副本必须与 APK 内条目尺寸一致——install -r 跨构建保留 cache，
            // 旧字节会让 solist 符号验证静默失败（2026-11-10 回归发现的陈旧缓存类故障）
            val out = java.io.File(context.cacheDir, LIB_NAME)
            ZipFile(ai.sourceDir).use { zip ->
                val entry = zip.entries().asSequence().firstOrNull { it.name.endsWith(LIB_NAME) } ?: return null
                if (!out.isFile || out.length() != entry.size) {
                    zip.getInputStream(entry).use { input ->
                        java.io.FileOutputStream(out).use { input.copyTo(it) }
                    }
                }
            }
            out.absolutePath
        }.getOrNull()
    }
}

/** 完整性分析（比对逻辑纯 Kotlin，主机单测）。 */
object IntegrityAnalyzer {

    /** 自身导出符号（= JNI 入口 = 采集器入口，任务要求校验清单覆盖采集器自身入口）。 */
    private val OWN_ENTRY_SYMBOLS = listOf(
        "Java_com_detectany_core_nativeplatform_SyscallJni_statSvc",
        "Java_com_detectany_core_nativeplatform_SyscallJni_statLibc",
        "Java_com_detectany_core_nativeplatform_ElfJni_exportValue",
        "Java_com_detectany_core_nativeplatform_ProbeJni_ksuProbe",
        "Java_com_detectany_core_nativeplatform_PropJni_propList",
    )

    /**
     * GOT 槽位检查的导入符号样本（libc 关键路径）。
     * MUST 排除 IFUNC 族（memcpy/memset/memmove/strlen...）：运行时解析到 SIMD/MTE 变体实现，
     * 本来就不等于磁盘 st_value（API 35 实测 memcpy 偏移差 ~0x71098）。
     */
    private val GOT_SAMPLE_SYMBOLS = setOf("openat", "close", "read", "write", "mmap", "mprotect")

    /**
     * prologue 磁盘 vs 内存：任一符号字节不一致 = inline hook（A 级）。
     */
    fun checkPrologues(libPath: String, base: Long): List<Finding> {
        val findings = mutableListOf<Finding>()
        for (sym in OWN_ENTRY_SYMBOLS) {
            val vaddr = runCatching { ElfJni.exportValue(libPath, sym) }.getOrDefault(0L)
            if (vaddr == 0L) continue
            val disk = runCatching { ElfJni.readAtVaddr(libPath, vaddr, 32) }.getOrNull() ?: continue
            // execute-only 文本段：PVM 读零（Pixel 8/A16 实测），必须走 mprotect 临时放行的 textRead
            val mem = runCatching { ProbeJni.textRead(base + vaddr, 32) }.getOrNull() ?: continue
            if (!disk.contentEquals(mem)) {
                findings.add(
                    Finding(
                        ruleId = "integrity.prologue.$sym",
                        collector = CollectorId.C7_CODE_INTEGRITY,
                        confidence = Confidence.A,
                        category = Finding.Category.HOOK,
                        framework = null,
                        detail = "采集器入口 prologue 被改写: $sym（内存字节 != 磁盘字节）",
                        raw = mapOf(
                            "symbol" to sym,
                            "vaddr" to vaddr.toString(16),
                            "disk" to disk.take(12).joinToString("") { "%02x".format(it) },
                            "mem" to mem.take(12).joinToString("") { "%02x".format(it) },
                        ),
                    ),
                )
            }
        }
        return findings
    }

    /**
     * GOT 槽位：解析地址必须落在某映射库内，且 == 该库磁盘 st_value + 基址（自洽校验，不用 dlsym）。
     */
    fun checkGot(libPath: String, base: Long, maps: List<com.detectany.collectors.memmap.MapEntry>): List<Finding> {
        val findings = mutableListOf<Finding>()
        val relocs = runCatching { ElfJni.relocations(libPath) }.getOrDefault(emptyArray())
        for (line in relocs) {
            val sym = line.substringBefore('\t')
            if (sym !in GOT_SAMPLE_SYMBOLS) continue
            val gotVaddr = line.substringAfter('\t').toLongOrNull() ?: continue
            val slotBytes = runCatching { ProbeJni.textRead(base + gotVaddr, 8) }.getOrNull() ?: continue
            val resolved = bytesToLong(slotBytes)
            if (resolved == 0L) continue
            val target = maps.firstOrNull { resolved >= it.start && resolved < it.end }
            if (target?.path == null) {
                // 解析地址不在任何已知映射（指向匿名区 = inline hook 蹦床特征）
                findings.add(
                    Finding(
                        ruleId = "integrity.got_anon.$sym",
                        collector = CollectorId.C7_CODE_INTEGRITY,
                        confidence = Confidence.A,
                        category = Finding.Category.HOOK,
                        framework = null,
                        detail = "GOT[$sym] 解析到匿名区 0x${resolved.toString(16)}（蹦床特征）",
                        raw = mapOf("symbol" to sym, "resolved" to resolved.toString(16)),
                    ),
                )
                continue
            }
            val expected = runCatching { ElfJni.exportValue(target.path!!, sym) }.getOrDefault(0L)
            val targetBase = maps.firstOrNull { it.path == target.path }?.start ?: 0L
            if (expected != 0L && resolved != targetBase + expected) {
                findings.add(
                    Finding(
                        ruleId = "integrity.got_hooked.$sym",
                        collector = CollectorId.C7_CODE_INTEGRITY,
                        confidence = Confidence.A,
                        category = Finding.Category.HOOK,
                        framework = null,
                        detail = "GOT[$sym] 指向 0x${resolved.toString(16)}，与磁盘期望 0x${(targetBase + expected).toString(16)} 不符（目标 ${target.path}）",
                        raw = mapOf("symbol" to sym, "resolved" to resolved.toString(16), "expected" to (targetBase + expected).toString(16), "targetLib" to (target.path ?: "")),
                    ),
                )
            }
        }
        return findings
    }

    internal fun bytesToLong(b: ByteArray): Long {
        var v = 0L
        for (i in b.indices.reversed()) {
            v = (v shl 8) or (b[i].toLong() and 0xFF)
        }
        return v
    }
}
```

`collectors/code-integrity/src/test/kotlin/com/detectany/collectors/codeintegrity/IntegrityAnalyzerTest.kt`:

```kt
package com.detectany.collectors.codeintegrity

import kotlin.test.Test
import kotlin.test.assertEquals

/** 任务 3.7 主机侧验证：字节序换算（GOT 槽位解析的前置）；prologue/GOT 比对在模拟器冒烟覆盖。 */
class IntegrityAnalyzerTest {

    @Test
    fun bytesToLongLittleEndian() {
        assertEquals(0x0102030405060708L, IntegrityAnalyzer.bytesToLong(byteArrayOf(8, 7, 6, 5, 4, 3, 2, 1)))
        assertEquals(1L, IntegrityAnalyzer.bytesToLong(byteArrayOf(1)))
        assertEquals(0L, IntegrityAnalyzer.bytesToLong(byteArrayOf()))
    }

    @Test
    fun bytesToLongZeroSlot() {
        assertEquals(0L, IntegrityAnalyzer.bytesToLong(ByteArray(8)))
    }
}
```

`collectors/endpoints/src/main/kotlin/com/detectany/collectors/endpoints/EndpointCollector.kt`:

```kt
package com.detectany.collectors.endpoints

import com.detectany.compat.platform.Probe
import com.detectany.compat.platform.ProbeCap
import com.detectany.compat.platform.PlatformEnv
import com.detectany.compat.platform.KernelVersion
import com.detectany.core.model.Collector
import com.detectany.core.model.CollectorId
import com.detectany.core.model.CollectorResult
import com.detectany.core.model.Confidence
import com.detectany.core.model.Finding
import com.detectany.core.model.ScanInput
import com.detectany.core.model.SignatureEntries
import com.detectany.core.nativeplatform.JniSyscallBackend
import com.detectany.core.nativeplatform.SyscallJni
import com.detectany.core.nativeplatform.TriplePathExecutor
import com.detectany.core.nativeplatform.parseStatArray

/**
 * C4 网络端点（任务 3.4）：门控下的 /proc/net/unix 匹配 + 文件系统 socket 路径三路探测。
 * Android 10+（现代 targetSdk）/proc/net/unix EACCES → DEGRADED + connect() 路径由规则层后续扩展。
 */
class EndpointCollector(
    private val unixReader: () -> List<String> = { java.io.File("/proc/net/unix").readLines() },
    private val executor: TriplePathExecutor = TriplePathExecutor(JniSyscallBackend),
    private val entries: List<SignatureEntries.SocketEntry> = defaultSocketEntries(),
) : Collector {

    override val id: CollectorId = CollectorId.C4_ENDPOINT

    override fun collect(input: ScanInput): CollectorResult {
        val env = PlatformEnv(input.apiLevel, input.targetSdk, input.kernelRelease?.let(KernelVersion::parse))
        val cap = ProbeCap.status(Probe.PROC_NET_UNIX, env)
        val findings = mutableListOf<Finding>()
        var status = CollectorResult.Status.OK

        if (cap.capability == com.detectany.compat.platform.ProbeCapability.ALLOWED) {
            try {
                findings += EndpointAnalyzer.analyzeUnix(unixReader(), entries)
            } catch (e: Throwable) {
                status = CollectorResult.Status.DEGRADED
            }
        } else {
            // 门控阻塞：透明降级（specs/detection-engine），不失败
            status = CollectorResult.Status.DEGRADED
        }

        // 文件系统 socket 路径（/debug_ramdisk、/dev/zygisk 族）不受 /proc/net 门控影响
        val fsEntries = entries.filter { it.name.startsWith("/") }
        for (entry in fsEntries) {
            try {
                if (parseStatArray(SyscallJni.statSvc(entry.name)).exists) {
                    findings.add(entry.toFinding())
                }
            } catch (_: Throwable) {
            }
        }

        return CollectorResult(
            collector = id,
            status = status,
            findings = findings,
            meta = mapOf("unixGate" to cap.capability.name, "fsProbed" to fsEntries.size.toString()),
        )
    }

    private fun SignatureEntries.SocketEntry.toFinding() = Finding(
        ruleId = id,
        collector = CollectorId.C4_ENDPOINT,
        confidence = confidence,
        category = category,
        framework = framework,
        detail = "socket 命中: $name",
        raw = mapOf("name" to name),
    )
}

/** /proc/net/unix 分析（纯 Kotlin）。 */
object EndpointAnalyzer {

    /** 行格式：Num RefCount Protocol Flags Type St Inode Path —— Path 为第 8 列 */
    fun parsePaths(lines: List<String>): List<String> = lines
        .drop(1)
        .map { it.trim().split(Regex("\\s+")) }
        .filter { it.size >= 8 }
        .map { it[7] }

    fun analyzeUnix(lines: List<String>, entries: List<SignatureEntries.SocketEntry>): List<Finding> {
        val paths = parsePaths(lines).toSet()
        return buildList {
            for (entry in entries) {
                val hit = paths.firstOrNull { p ->
                    // 抽象 socket 在 /proc/net/unix 中可能带 @ 前缀或不带；文件 socket 以 / 开头
                    p == entry.name || p == "@${entry.name}" || p.endsWith("/${entry.name}") ||
                        (entry.name.startsWith("/") && p == entry.name)
                }
                if (hit != null) {
                    add(
                        Finding(
                            ruleId = entry.id,
                            collector = CollectorId.C4_ENDPOINT,
                            confidence = entry.confidence,
                            category = entry.category,
                            framework = entry.framework,
                            detail = "/proc/net/unix 命中: $hit",
                            raw = mapOf("hit" to hit),
                        ),
                    )
                }
            }
        }
    }
}

internal fun defaultSocketEntries(): List<SignatureEntries.SocketEntry> = listOf(
    // KSU daemon（版本化，特征数据热更新维护）
    SignatureEntries.SocketEntry("sock.ksud", "ksud", "ksu", Confidence.C, Finding.Category.ROOT_KERNEL),
    // ZN/ReZygisk 守护进程
    SignatureEntries.SocketEntry("sock.zygiskd", "zygiskd", "zygisknext", Confidence.C, Finding.Category.ZYGISK),
    SignatureEntries.SocketEntry("sock.zygiskd64", "zygiskd64", "zygisknext", Confidence.C, Finding.Category.ZYGISK),
    // LSPosed 族（春秋 Evil Service 清单）
    SignatureEntries.SocketEntry("sock.lsposed", "lsposed", "lsposed", Confidence.C, Finding.Category.HOOK),
    SignatureEntries.SocketEntry("sock.sandhook", "sandhook", null, Confidence.C, Finding.Category.HOOK),
    // 文件系统 socket 路径族（Santander 2025 清单）
    SignatureEntries.SocketEntry("sock.fs.zygisk_monitor", "/debug_ramdisk/zygisk/monitor.sock", "zygisknext", Confidence.C, Finding.Category.ZYGISK),
    SignatureEntries.SocketEntry("sock.fs.zygisk_zygisk", "/debug_ramdisk/zygisk/zygisk.sock", "zygisknext", Confidence.C, Finding.Category.ZYGISK),
    SignatureEntries.SocketEntry("sock.fs.zygisk_cp64", "/dev/zygisk/cp64.sock", "zygisknext", Confidence.C, Finding.Category.ZYGISK),
    SignatureEntries.SocketEntry("sock.fs.zygisk_cp32", "/dev/zygisk/cp32.sock", "zygisknext", Confidence.C, Finding.Category.ZYGISK),
    SignatureEntries.SocketEntry("sock.fs.magisk_socket", "/debug_ramdisk/.magisk/socket", "magisk", Confidence.C, Finding.Category.ROOT_USERSPACE),
)
```

`collectors/endpoints/src/test/kotlin/com/detectany/collectors/endpoints/EndpointAnalyzerTest.kt`:

```kt
package com.detectany.collectors.endpoints

import kotlin.test.Test
import kotlin.test.assertEquals
import kotlin.test.assertTrue

/** 任务 3.4 验证：/proc/net/unix 解析与 socket 匹配。 */
class EndpointAnalyzerTest {

    private val unixLines = listOf(
        "Num       RefCount Protocol Flags    Type St Inode Path",
        "00000000: 00000002 00000000 00010000 0001 01 1234 @zygiskd",
        "00000000: 00000002 00000000 00010000 0001 01 1235 /debug_ramdisk/.magisk/socket",
        "00000000: 00000002 00000000 00010000 0001 01 1236 @ksud",
        "00000000: 00000002 00000000 00010000 0001 01 1237 @com.google.android.gms.common",
    )

    @Test
    fun parsesPaths() {
        val paths = EndpointAnalyzer.parsePaths(unixLines)
        assertEquals(4, paths.size)
        assertTrue(paths.contains("@zygiskd"))
    }

    @Test
    fun matchesAbstractAndFileSockets() {
        val findings = EndpointAnalyzer.analyzeUnix(unixLines, defaultSocketEntries())
        assertTrue(findings.any { it.ruleId == "sock.zygiskd" && it.framework == "zygisknext" })
        assertTrue(findings.any { it.ruleId == "sock.ksud" && it.framework == "ksu" })
        assertTrue(findings.any { it.ruleId == "sock.fs.magisk_socket" && it.framework == "magisk" })
    }

    @Test
    fun cleanEnvironmentNoFindings() {
        val clean = listOf(
            "Num       RefCount Protocol Flags    Type St Inode Path",
            "00000000: 00000002 00000000 00010000 0001 01 1237 @com.google.android.gms.common",
        )
        assertEquals(0, EndpointAnalyzer.analyzeUnix(clean, defaultSocketEntries()).size)
    }
}
```

`collectors/fs-probe/src/main/kotlin/com/detectany/collectors/fsprobe/FsProbeCollectors.kt`:

```kt
package com.detectany.collectors.fsprobe

import com.detectany.core.model.Collector
import com.detectany.core.model.CollectorId
import com.detectany.core.model.CollectorResult
import com.detectany.core.model.Confidence
import com.detectany.core.model.Finding
import com.detectany.core.model.ScanInput
import com.detectany.core.model.SignatureEntries
import com.detectany.core.nativeplatform.parseStatArray
import com.detectany.core.nativeplatform.JniSyscallBackend
import com.detectany.core.nativeplatform.SyscallJni
import com.detectany.core.nativeplatform.TriplePathExecutor

/**
 * C1 路径探测（任务 3.1）：特征路径三路探测（Java/libc/svc）+ 失败码分类。
 * svc vs libc 不一致 → A 级 hook 证据；路径存在 → 条目置信级 Finding。
 */
class PathProbeCollector(
    private val paths: List<SignatureEntries.PathEntry> = defaultPathEntries(),
    private val executor: TriplePathExecutor = TriplePathExecutor(JniSyscallBackend),
) : Collector {

    override val id: CollectorId = CollectorId.C1_PATH

    override fun collect(input: ScanInput): CollectorResult {
        // L1 子集化（2026-11-10 时延修复）：仅探测 A/B 级路径——C 级路径
        // （/data/adb 族，对 app 永远 EACCES）在快扫里是纯浪费的 3×syscall
        val effective = if (input.tier == com.detectany.core.model.ScanTier.L1) {
            paths.filter { it.confidence != com.detectany.core.model.Confidence.C }
        } else paths
        val findings = mutableListOf<Finding>()
        var degraded = 0
        for (entry in effective) {
            try {
                val diff = executor.probe(entry.path)
                // 三路 diff：svc vs libc 层间不一致即 hook 证据（A 级物理不变量）
                diff.svcVsLibc?.let { m ->
                    findings.add(
                        Finding(
                            ruleId = "triple.svc_libc.${entry.id}",
                            collector = id,
                            confidence = Confidence.A,
                            category = Finding.Category.HOOK,
                            framework = null,
                            detail = "路径 ${entry.path} 的 libc 与 svc 结果不一致（${m.kind}: ${m.detail}）",
                            raw = mapOf("path" to entry.path, "kind" to m.kind.name, "detail" to m.detail),
                        ),
                    )
                }
                diff.javaVsNative?.let { m ->
                    findings.add(
                        Finding(
                            ruleId = "triple.java_native.${entry.id}",
                            collector = id,
                            confidence = Confidence.B,
                            category = Finding.Category.HOOK,
                            framework = null,
                            detail = "路径 ${entry.path} 的 Java API 与 native 结果不一致（${m.kind}）",
                            raw = mapOf("path" to entry.path, "kind" to m.kind.name),
                        ),
                    )
                }
                // 存在性：以 svc 层（最可信）为准（来自 executor 观测，不直连 JNI）
                val svcExists = diff.svc.exists
                if (svcExists) {
                    findings.add(
                        Finding(
                            ruleId = entry.id,
                            collector = id,
                            confidence = entry.confidence,
                            category = entry.category,
                            framework = entry.framework,
                            detail = "特征路径存在: ${entry.path}",
                            raw = mapOf("path" to entry.path),
                        ),
                    )
                }
            } catch (e: Throwable) {
                degraded++
            }
        }
        return CollectorResult(
            collector = id,
            status = if (degraded == effective.size) CollectorResult.Status.BLOCKED else if (degraded > 0) CollectorResult.Status.DEGRADED else CollectorResult.Status.OK,
            findings = findings,
            meta = mapOf("probed" to effective.size.toString(), "degraded" to degraded.toString()),
        )
    }
}

/**
 * C2 挂载/设备号（任务 3.2）：mountinfo 解析 + st_dev 四路采样 + 基线偏离。
 * statfs f_type 交叉记录进 meta（overlay/tmpfs 判别由 analyzer 消费）；
 * /proc/filesystems 内核注册文件系统类型比对（susfs 非隐藏场景残留，特征库 v7）。
 */
class MountCollector(
    private val mountInfoReader: () -> List<String> = { java.io.File("/proc/self/mountinfo").readLines() },
    private val filesystemsReader: () -> List<String> = { java.io.File("/proc/filesystems").readLines() },
    private val fsEntries: List<SignatureEntries.FilesystemEntry> = defaultFsEntries(),
    // P2-2（2026-11-11）接线：baselineLookup 命中机型（deviceBaselines 数据）时
    // 以真数据构造分析器——too_clean（B）与 st_dev 偏离（C）激活；
    // 未命中机型维持不判（无基线数据不拍脑袋）。默认无 lookup = 原行为。
    private val baselineLookup: (String) -> SignatureEntries.DeviceBaselineEntry? = { null },
    private val analyzer: MountAnalyzer = MountAnalyzer(),
    private val deviceModel: () -> String = { android.os.Build.MODEL ?: "" }, // host 单测 Build.MODEL 返回 null（非异常）——elvis 兜底
    private val stDevPaths: List<String> = listOf("/dev", "/proc", "/sys", "/dev/pts"),
    private val statSampler: (String) -> Long? = { path ->
        runCatching { parseStatArray(SyscallJni.statSvc(path)) as? com.detectany.core.nativeplatform.StatOutcome.Ok }
            .getOrNull()?.dev
    },
) : Collector {

    override val id: CollectorId = CollectorId.C2_MOUNT

    override fun collect(input: ScanInput): CollectorResult = try {
        val entries = MountInfoParser.parse(mountInfoReader())
        val stDevs = stDevPaths.mapNotNull { p -> statSampler(p)?.let { p to it } }.toMap()
        val baseline = baselineLookup(deviceModel())
        val effectiveAnalyzer = baseline?.let {
            MountAnalyzer(baselineMountCount = it.mountCountMin, expectedStDev = it.stDev)
        } ?: analyzer
        val findings = effectiveAnalyzer.analyze(entries, stDevs).toMutableList()

        // statfs f_type 交叉（任务 3.2）：mountinfo 声称的 fstype 与 statfs 实测 f_type 魔数
        // 不一致 = susfs 类挂载伪造形态（overlay 冒充 tmpfs 会被 0x01021997 拆穿）
        val tmpfsCount = entries.count { it.fstype == "tmpfs" }
        for (e in entries) {
            if (e.mountPoint !in FS_CROSS_SAMPLE_POINTS) continue
            val expectedMagic = FSTYPE_MAGIC[e.fstype] ?: continue
            val r = runCatching { SyscallJni.statfsSvc(e.mountPoint) }.getOrNull() ?: continue
            if (r[0] == 0L && r[2] != expectedMagic) {
                findings.add(
                    Finding(
                        ruleId = "mount.fstype_cross.${e.mountPoint.hashCode().toUInt()}",
                        collector = id,
                        confidence = Confidence.B, // A3 降权：susfs v2.x 用户态 umount 一致化
                        category = Finding.Category.ROOT_KERNEL,
                        framework = null,
                        detail = "statfs 交叉不一致: ${e.mountPoint} mountinfo=${e.fstype} 但 statfs f_type=0x${r[2].toString(16)}",
                        raw = mapOf("mountPoint" to e.mountPoint, "claimed" to e.fstype, "f_type" to r[2].toString()),
                    ),
                )
            }
        }

        // /proc/filesystems 内核注册类型比对（v7）：susfs 等以 register_filesystem 注册的
        // 挂载名在非隐藏场景残留；名字由内核持有，无法被用户态改名（深隐藏配置会藏掉 → 漏检可接受）
        findings += checkRegisteredFilesystems()
        CollectorResult(
            collector = id,
            status = CollectorResult.Status.OK,
            findings = findings,
            meta = mapOf(
                "mounts" to entries.size.toString(),
                "tmpfs" to tmpfsCount.toString(),
                "stdev" to stDevs.entries.joinToString(",") { "${it.key}=${it.value}" },
            ),
        )
    } catch (e: Throwable) {
        CollectorResult(collector = id, status = CollectorResult.Status.BLOCKED, meta = mapOf("error" to (e.message ?: e.javaClass.simpleName)))
    }

    /** /proc/filesystems 行格式：`nodev\tname` 或 `name`；比对特征名（精确匹配）。 */
    private fun checkRegisteredFilesystems(): List<Finding> {
        if (fsEntries.isEmpty()) return emptyList()
        val lines = runCatching { filesystemsReader() }.getOrNull() ?: return emptyList()
        val registered = lines.mapNotNull { it.substringAfterLast('\t').trim().ifEmpty { null } }.toSet()
        return fsEntries.filter { it.fsName in registered }.map { entry ->
            Finding(
                ruleId = entry.id,
                collector = id,
                confidence = entry.confidence,
                category = entry.category,
                framework = entry.framework,
                detail = "内核注册文件系统类型存在: ${entry.fsName}（/proc/filesystems）",
                raw = mapOf("fsName" to entry.fsName),
            )
        }
    }
}

/** statfs 交叉的采样挂载点与 f_type 魔数（Linux 内核 magic.h）。 */
private val FS_CROSS_SAMPLE_POINTS = setOf("/", "/data", "/system", "/vendor", "/product")

private val FSTYPE_MAGIC = mapOf(
    "ext4" to 0xEF53L,
    "f2fs" to 0xF2F52010L,
    "erofs" to 0xE0F5E1E2L,
    "tmpfs" to 0x01021997L,
    "overlay" to 0x794C7630L,
    "vfat" to 0x4D44L,
    "fuse" to 0x65735546L,
)

```

`collectors/fs-probe/src/main/kotlin/com/detectany/collectors/fsprobe/MountInfo.kt`:

```kt
package com.detectany.collectors.fsprobe
```

```kt
import com.detectany.core.model.Confidence
import com.detectany.core.model.Finding
import com.detectany.core.model.SignatureEntries

/**
 * mountinfo 解析（C2，design.md D2）：纯 Kotlin，主机单测覆盖。
 */
data class MountEntry(
    val mountId: Long,
    val parentId: Long,
    val dev: Long,
    val root: String,
    val mountPoint: String,
    val mountOpts: List<String>,
    val fstype: String,
    val source: String,
    val superOpts: List<String>,
)

object MountInfoParser {

    /** 解析 /proc/self/mountinfo 全部行；无法解析的行跳过。 */
    fun parse(lines: List<String>): List<MountEntry> = lines.mapNotNull { parseLine(it) }

    fun parseLine(line: String): MountEntry? {
        // 格式：36 35 98:0 /mnt1 /mnt2 rw,noatime master:1 - ext3 /dev/root rw,errors=continue
        // （"-" 前为可选字段区，之后为 fstype source superopts）
        val dash = line.lastIndexOf(" - ")
        if (dash < 0) return null
        val head = line.substring(0, dash).trim()
        val tail = line.substring(dash + 3).trim()
        val headFields = head.split(Regex("\\s+"))
        if (headFields.size < 6) return null
        val tailFields = tail.split(Regex("\\s+"))
        if (tailFields.size < 3) return null
        val ids = headFields[0].toLongOrNull() ?: return null
        val pid = headFields[1].toLongOrNull() ?: return null
        val devParts = headFields[2].split(":")
        val devMajor = devParts.getOrNull(0)?.toLongOrNull() ?: return null
        val devMinor = devParts.getOrNull(1)?.toLongOrNull() ?: return null
        return MountEntry(
            mountId = ids,
            parentId = pid,
            dev = devMajor shl 20 or devMinor,
            root = headFields[3],
            mountPoint = unescape(headFields[4]),
            mountOpts = headFields[5].split(","),
            fstype = tailFields[0],
            source = unescape(tailFields[1]),
            superOpts = tailFields[2].split(","),
        )
    }

    /** mountinfo 的转义：\040=空格、\011=tab、\134=backslash。 */
    private fun unescape(s: String): String = s
        .replace("\\040", " ")
        .replace("\\011", "\t")
        .replace("\\134", "\\")
}

/**
 * 挂载分析（C2）：overlay/magic-mount 痕迹 + 「过度干净」异常 + st_dev 采样比对。
 */
class MountAnalyzer(
    private val baselineMountCount: Int? = null,
    private val expectedStDev: Map<String, Long> = emptyMap(),
) {

    fun analyze(
        entries: List<MountEntry>,
        stDevSamples: Map<String, Long> = emptyMap(),
    ): List<Finding> = buildList {
        // 1) overlay 挂载（模块挂载形态，B 级）
        entries.filter { it.fstype == "overlay" }.forEach { e ->
            add(
                Finding(
                    ruleId = "mount.overlay.${e.mountPoint.hashCode().toUInt()}",
                    collector = com.detectany.core.model.CollectorId.C2_MOUNT,
                    confidence = Confidence.B,
                    category = Finding.Category.ROOT_USERSPACE,
                    framework = inferFramework(e),
                    detail = "overlay 挂载: mountPoint=${e.mountPoint} source=${e.source}",
                    raw = mapOf("fstype" to e.fstype, "mountPoint" to e.mountPoint, "source" to e.source),
                ),
            )
        }

        // 2) magic mount 残留（source 含 magisk，C 级弱信号）
        entries.filter { it.source.contains("magisk", ignoreCase = true) }.forEach { e ->
            add(
                Finding(
                    ruleId = "mount.source_magisk",
                    collector = com.detectany.core.model.CollectorId.C2_MOUNT,
                    confidence = Confidence.C,
                    category = Finding.Category.ROOT_USERSPACE,
                    framework = "magisk",
                    detail = "挂载 source 含 magisk: ${e.source} -> ${e.mountPoint}",
                    raw = mapOf("source" to e.source, "mountPoint" to e.mountPoint),
                ),
            )
        }

        // 3) /debug_ramdisk/.magisk* 落脚点（C 级）
        // 2026-11-06 Pixel 8/Android 16 实测：干净设备原生挂载 /debug_ramdisk(tmpfs) 与
        // /debug_ramdisk/pts(devpts) —— 裸 /debug_ramdisk 不是 Magisk 证据，必须收窄到 .magisk 子路径
        entries.filter { it.mountPoint.startsWith("/debug_ramdisk/.magisk") }.forEach { e ->
            add(
                Finding(
                    ruleId = "mount.debug_ramdisk",
                    collector = com.detectany.core.model.CollectorId.C2_MOUNT,
                    confidence = Confidence.C,
                    category = Finding.Category.ROOT_USERSPACE,
                    framework = "magisk",
                    detail = "/debug_ramdisk 挂载: ${e.mountPoint} (${e.fstype})",
                    raw = mapOf("mountPoint" to e.mountPoint, "fstype" to e.fstype),
                ),
            )
        }

        // 4) 「过度干净」（anti_detect_present 独立证据类型）
        // P2-2（2026-11-11）：基线数据化后 threshold 直用 mountCountMin（同机型实测下限），
        // 原×0.6 缓冲是拍脑袋基线时代的余量，真数据不需要
        baselineMountCount?.let { base ->
            if (entries.size < base) {
                add(
                    Finding(
                        ruleId = "mount.too_clean",
                        collector = com.detectany.core.model.CollectorId.C2_MOUNT,
                        confidence = Confidence.B,
                        category = Finding.Category.ANTI_DETECT_PRESENT,
                        framework = null,
                        detail = "挂载条目数 ${entries.size} 显著低于同机型基线 $base（隐藏栈嫌疑）",
                        raw = mapOf("count" to entries.size.toString(), "baseline" to base.toString()),
                    ),
                )
            }
        }

        // 5) st_dev 采样与机型基线偏离（PayPal 向量）
        expectedStDev.forEach { (path, expected) ->
            val actual = stDevSamples[path]
            if (actual != null && actual != expected) {
                add(
                    Finding(
                        ruleId = "mount.stdev_deviation.$path",
                        collector = com.detectany.core.model.CollectorId.C2_MOUNT,
                        // P2-2（2026-11-11）：降 C——tmpfs 设备号按挂载顺序分配，跨重启
                        // 稳定性未验证（同机型 3 次重启采样一致后才可升 B）
                        confidence = Confidence.C,
                        category = Finding.Category.ROOT_USERSPACE,
                        framework = null,
                        detail = "st_dev($path)=$actual 与基线 $expected 不符（启动期 tmpfs 设备号占用痕迹）",
                        raw = mapOf("path" to path, "actual" to actual.toString(), "expected" to expected.toString()),
                    ),
                )
            }
        }
    }

    private fun inferFramework(e: MountEntry): String? = when {
        e.source.contains("magisk", true) || e.superOpts.any { it.contains("magisk", true) } -> "magisk"
        e.mountPoint.contains("/adb/modules", true) -> "ksu"
        else -> null
    }
}

/** 特征路径表（内置基线，Phase 1；任务 4.1 迁入 SignatureEntries 下发）。 */
internal fun defaultPathEntries(): List<SignatureEntries.PathEntry> = listOf(
    // Magisk v31.x：/debug_ramdisk 工件（manager hide 已移除，落脚点重新可见）
    SignatureEntries.PathEntry("path.debug_ramdisk.magisk", "/debug_ramdisk/.magisk/socket", "magisk", Confidence.C, Finding.Category.ROOT_USERSPACE),
    SignatureEntries.PathEntry("path.debug_ramdisk.socket", "/debug_ramdisk/socket", "magisk", Confidence.C, Finding.Category.ROOT_USERSPACE),
    // ZN/ReZygisk socket 路径族（Santander 2025 实测清单）
    SignatureEntries.PathEntry("path.zygisk.monitor", "/debug_ramdisk/zygisk/monitor.sock", "zygisknext", Confidence.C, Finding.Category.ZYGISK),
    SignatureEntries.PathEntry("path.zygisk.zygisk", "/debug_ramdisk/zygisk/zygisk.sock", "zygisknext", Confidence.C, Finding.Category.ZYGISK),
    SignatureEntries.PathEntry("path.zygisk.dev_cp64", "/dev/zygisk/cp64.sock", "zygisknext", Confidence.C, Finding.Category.ZYGISK),
    SignatureEntries.PathEntry("path.zygisk.dev_cp32", "/dev/zygisk/cp32.sock", "zygisknext", Confidence.C, Finding.Category.ZYGISK),
    SignatureEntries.PathEntry("path.rezygisk.dir", "/data/adb/rezygisk", "rezygisk", Confidence.C, Finding.Category.ZYGISK),
    // KSU / APatch daemon 产物
    SignatureEntries.PathEntry("path.ksu.modules", "/data/adb/ksu", "ksu", Confidence.C, Finding.Category.ROOT_KERNEL),
    SignatureEntries.PathEntry("path.apatch.ap", "/data/adb/ap", "apatch", Confidence.C, Finding.Category.ROOT_KERNEL),
    // Riru 遗留（存量命中）
    SignatureEntries.PathEntry("path.riru.dir", "/data/adb/riru", null, Confidence.C, Finding.Category.HOOK),
    // su 二进制存量
    SignatureEntries.PathEntry("path.su.system", "/system/bin/su", null, Confidence.C, Finding.Category.ROOT_USERSPACE),
    SignatureEntries.PathEntry("path.su.system_xbin", "/system/xbin/su", null, Confidence.C, Finding.Category.ROOT_USERSPACE),
    SignatureEntries.PathEntry("path.su.sbin", "/sbin/su", null, Confidence.C, Finding.Category.ROOT_USERSPACE),
)

/** /proc/filesystems 特征类型表（内置基线；特征库 v7 数据化）。 */
internal fun defaultFsEntries(): List<SignatureEntries.FilesystemEntry> = listOf(
    // susfs：register_filesystem("susfs")，非隐藏配置下必现；深隐藏（hide log/mounts 全开）时漏检可接受
    SignatureEntries.FilesystemEntry("fs.susfs", "susfs", "susfs", Confidence.B, Finding.Category.ROOT_KERNEL),
)

```

`collectors/fs-probe/src/test/kotlin/com/detectany/collectors/fsprobe/FsProbeTest.kt`:

```kt
package com.detectany.collectors.fsprobe

import com.detectany.core.model.CollectorId
import com.detectany.core.model.CollectorResult
import com.detectany.core.model.Confidence
import com.detectany.core.model.ScanTier
import com.detectany.core.model.SignatureEntries
import com.detectany.core.nativeplatform.StatOutcome
import com.detectany.core.nativeplatform.SyscallBackend
import com.detectany.core.nativeplatform.TriplePathExecutor
import kotlin.test.Test
import kotlin.test.assertEquals
import kotlin.test.assertTrue

/** 任务 3.1/3.2 验证：C1 三路探测桩测试 + C2 mountinfo 解析/分析。 */
class FsProbeTest {

    private val ok = StatOutcome.Ok(7, 42, 0x8000, 1000, 1000, 123, 1700000000)

    private class FixedBackend(vararg val pair: Pair<String, Pair<StatOutcome, StatOutcome>>) : SyscallBackend {
        override fun statLibc(path: String) = pair.first { it.first == path }.second.second
        override fun statSvc(path: String) = pair.first { it.first == path }.second.first
    }

    private val javaView = TriplePathExecutor.JavaStatView { path ->
        if (path.contains("ramdisk")) ok.copy(dev = -1, ino = -1, uid = -1, gid = -1) else StatOutcome.Errno(2)
    }

    private val input = com.detectany.core.model.ScanInput(ScanTier.L2, 35, 35, "6.1.75", "arm64-v8a")

    @Test
    fun c1_existingPathYieldsEntryFinding() {
        val backend = FixedBackend("/debug_ramdisk/zygisk/monitor.sock" to (ok to ok))
        val collector = PathProbeCollector(
            paths = defaultPathEntries().filter { it.path == "/debug_ramdisk/zygisk/monitor.sock" },
            executor = TriplePathExecutor(backend, javaView),
        )
        val result = collector.collect(input)
        assertEquals(CollectorResult.Status.OK, result.status)
        assertEquals(1, result.findings.size)
        assertEquals("path.zygisk.monitor", result.findings[0].ruleId)
        assertEquals(Confidence.C, result.findings[0].confidence)
        assertEquals("zygisknext", result.findings[0].framework)
    }

    @Test
    fun c1_svcLibcMismatchYieldsStrongHookFinding() {
        // libc 被 hook（size 改 0），svc 真实
        val backend = FixedBackend("/system/bin/su" to (ok to ok.copy(size = 0)))
        val collector = PathProbeCollector(
            paths = defaultPathEntries().filter { it.path == "/system/bin/su" },
            executor = TriplePathExecutor(backend, javaView),
        )
        val result = collector.collect(input)
        val strong = result.findings.first { it.ruleId.startsWith("triple.svc_libc") }
        assertEquals(Confidence.A, strong.confidence)
        assertEquals(FindingCategory.HOOK, strong.category)
    }

    @Test
    fun c1_missingPathNoFindings() {
        val backend = FixedBackend("/system/bin/su" to (StatOutcome.Errno(2) to StatOutcome.Errno(2)))
        val collector = PathProbeCollector(
            paths = defaultPathEntries().filter { it.path == "/system/bin/su" },
            executor = TriplePathExecutor(backend, javaView),
        )
        assertEquals(0, collector.collect(input).findings.size)
    }

    // ---- C2 mountinfo ----

    private val mountLines = listOf(
        "36 35 98:0 /mnt1 /mnt2 rw,noatime master:1 - ext3 /dev/root rw,errors=continue",
        "39 38 0:22 / /debug_ramdisk rw,relatime - tmpfs tmpfs rw",
        "40 39 0:23 / /debug_ramdisk/pts rw - devpts devpts rw",
        "41 39 0:24 / /debug_ramdisk/.magisk rw - tmpfs magisk rw",
        "104 30 253:7 / /data rw - ext4 /dev/block/bootdevice/by-name/userdata rw",
        "180 30 0:51 / /mnt/expand rw - tmpfs tmpfs rw",
    )

    @Test
    fun c2_parsesMountInfo() {
        val entries = MountInfoParser.parse(mountLines)
        assertEquals(6, entries.size)
        assertEquals("/mnt2", entries[0].mountPoint)
        assertEquals("ext3", entries[0].fstype)
        assertEquals("rw", entries[0].mountOpts[0])
        assertEquals("tmpfs", entries[1].fstype)
        assertEquals("tmpfs", entries[1].fstype)
    }

    @Test
    fun c2_detectsMagiskResidueAndDebugRamdisk() {
        val findings = MountAnalyzer().analyze(MountInfoParser.parse(mountLines))
        assertTrue(findings.any { it.ruleId == "mount.source_magisk" && it.confidence == Confidence.C })
        assertTrue(findings.any { it.ruleId == "mount.debug_ramdisk" })
    }

    @Test
    fun c2_overlayYieldsBFinding() {
        val lines = listOf("50 30 0:60 / /system/overlay ro - overlay overlay ro,lowerdir=/data/adb/overlay")
        val findings = MountAnalyzer().analyze(MountInfoParser.parse(lines))
        val f = findings.first { it.ruleId.startsWith("mount.overlay") }
        assertEquals(Confidence.B, f.confidence)
    }

    @Test
    fun c2_tooCleanMountsYieldAntiDetect() {
        val analyzer = MountAnalyzer(baselineMountCount = 100)
        val findings = analyzer.analyze(MountInfoParser.parse(mountLines)) // 只有 4 条 << 基线*0.6
        val f = findings.first { it.ruleId == "mount.too_clean" }
        assertEquals(FindingCategory.ANTI_DETECT_PRESENT, f.category)
        assertEquals(Confidence.B, f.confidence)
    }

    @Test
    fun c2_stDevDeviationYieldsFinding() {
        val analyzer = MountAnalyzer(expectedStDev = mapOf("/sys" to 20L))
        val findings = analyzer.analyze(emptyList(), mapOf("/sys" to 21L)) // PayPal 实测形态
        val f = findings.first { it.ruleId == "mount.stdev_deviation./sys" }
        // P2-2（2026-11-11）：降 C——tmpfs 设备号跨重启稳定性未验证
        assertEquals(Confidence.C, f.confidence)
    }

    @Test
    fun c2_cleanEnvironmentNoFindings() {
        val lines = listOf(
            "104 30 253:7 / /data rw - ext4 /dev/block/... rw",
            "32 1 0:21 / / rw - ext4 /dev/block/... ro",
        )
        assertEquals(0, MountAnalyzer().analyze(MountInfoParser.parse(lines)).size)
    }

    // ---- /proc/filesystems 内核注册类型比对（特征库 v7） ----

    private val cleanFsLines = listOf(
        "nodev\tsysfs",
        "nodev\trootfs",
        "nodev\ttmpfs",
        "\text4",
        "nodev\tfunctionfs",
    )

    // 用非采样挂载点（/data 等会触发 statfsSvc native 调用，主机测试不可用）
    private val plainMount = listOf("104 30 253:7 / /mnt/test rw - ext4 /dev/block/... rw")

    @Test
    fun c2_susfsRegisteredYieldsFinding() {
        val collector = MountCollector(
            mountInfoReader = { plainMount },
            filesystemsReader = { cleanFsLines + "nodev\tsusfs" },
            statSampler = { null },
        )
        val result = collector.collect(input)
        val f = result.findings.first { it.ruleId == "fs.susfs" }
        assertEquals(Confidence.B, f.confidence)
        assertEquals("susfs", f.framework)
        assertEquals(FindingCategory.ROOT_KERNEL, f.category)
    }

    @Test
    fun c2_baselineLookupActivatesTooClean() {
        // P2-2 接线：机型命中 deviceBaselines → too_clean 以 mountCountMin 直判
        val baseline = SignatureEntries.DeviceBaselineEntry(model = "TestModel", mountCountMin = 200, stDev = emptyMap())
        val collector = MountCollector(
            mountInfoReader = { plainMount }, // 仅 4 条 << 200
            filesystemsReader = { cleanFsLines },
            baselineLookup = { m -> if (m == "TestModel") baseline else null },
            deviceModel = { "TestModel" },
            statSampler = { null },
        )
        val result = collector.collect(input)
        val f = result.findings.first { it.ruleId == "mount.too_clean" }
        assertEquals(Confidence.B, f.confidence)
    }

    @Test
    fun c2_baselineMissKeepsSilent() {
        // 机型不在基线库 → 信号维持不判（无数据不拍脑袋）
        val collector = MountCollector(
            mountInfoReader = { plainMount },
            filesystemsReader = { cleanFsLines },
            baselineLookup = { null },
            deviceModel = { "UnknownModel" },
            statSampler = { null },
        )
        val result = collector.collect(input)
        assertTrue(result.findings.none { it.ruleId == "mount.too_clean" })
    }

    @Test
    fun c2_cleanProcFilesystemsNoSusfsFinding() {
        val collector = MountCollector(
            mountInfoReader = { plainMount },
            filesystemsReader = { cleanFsLines },
            statSampler = { null },
        )
        val result = collector.collect(input)
        assertTrue(result.findings.none { it.ruleId.startsWith("fs.") })
    }

    @Test
    fun c2_procFilesystemsUnreadableDegradesGracefully() {
        val collector = MountCollector(
            mountInfoReader = { plainMount },
            filesystemsReader = { throw java.io.FileNotFoundException("/proc/filesystems") },
            statSampler = { null },
        )
        val result = collector.collect(input)
        assertTrue(result.findings.none { it.ruleId.startsWith("fs.") })
    }
}

private typealias FindingCategory = com.detectany.core.model.Finding.Category
```

`collectors/instrument-probe/src/main/kotlin/com/detectany/collectors/instrumentprobe/InstrumentProbeCollector.kt`:

```kt
package com.detectany.collectors.instrumentprobe

import com.detectany.core.model.Collector
import com.detectany.core.model.CollectorId
import com.detectany.core.model.CollectorResult
import com.detectany.core.model.Confidence
import com.detectany.core.model.Finding
import com.detectany.core.model.ScanInput
import java.io.File
import java.net.InetSocketAddress
import java.net.Socket

/**
 * C9: Frida 检测采集器 — 三层防线（字符串 / D-Bus 协议 / RWX 页特征）。
 * 面向 Collector 接口，由 DetectionEngine 并行调度。
 *
 * 校准定级（calibration.md）：
 * - maps 字符串 frida-agent/linjector/gadget → B
 * - 线程名 gum-js-loop + pool-frida → B（gmain 单独不产出）
 * - RWX 连续页 + 大小过滤 → B（需与字符串共振才 A）
 * - D-Bus 协议探测 → B
 */
class InstrumentProbeCollector : Collector {

    override val id: CollectorId = CollectorId.C9_INSTRUMENTATION

    override fun collect(input: ScanInput): CollectorResult = try {
        val findings = mutableListOf<Finding>()
        findings += scanMapsForStrings()
        findings += scanThreadNames()
        findings += scanRwxPages()
        findings += probeDbusPorts()
        CollectorResult(
            collector = id,
            status = CollectorResult.Status.OK,
            findings = findings,
            meta = mapOf(
                "mapsHits" to findings.count { it.ruleId.startsWith("frida.maps") }.toString(),
                "threadHits" to findings.count { it.ruleId.startsWith("frida.thread") }.toString(),
                "rwxHits" to findings.count { it.ruleId.startsWith("frida.rwx") }.toString(),
                "dbusHits" to findings.count { it.ruleId.startsWith("frida.dbus") }.toString(),
            ),
        )
    } catch (e: Throwable) {
        CollectorResult(collector = id, status = CollectorResult.Status.BLOCKED, meta = mapOf("error" to (e.message ?: e.javaClass.simpleName)))
    }

    // ---- L1: maps 字符串扫描 ----

    private val MAPS_KEYWORDS = listOf(
        "frida-agent" to "frida",
        "linjector" to "frida",
        "frida-gadget" to "frida",
        "frida-server" to "frida",
        "libfrida" to "frida",
        "KittyMemory" to "modmenu",
        "libdobby" to "modmenu",
    )

    private fun scanMapsForStrings(): List<Finding> {
        val maps = runCatching { File("/proc/self/maps").readLines() }.getOrDefault(emptyList())
        return MAPS_KEYWORDS.mapNotNull { (keyword, framework) ->
            if (maps.any { it.contains(keyword, ignoreCase = true) }) {
                Finding(
                    ruleId = "frida.maps.$keyword",
                    collector = id,
                    confidence = Confidence.B,
                    category = if (framework == "modmenu") Finding.Category.OVERLAY_INJECTION else Finding.Category.INSTRUMENTATION,
                    framework = framework,
                    detail = "maps 中发现 $keyword 映射",
                    raw = mapOf("keyword" to keyword),
                )
            } else null
        }
    }

    // ---- L2: 线程名扫描 ----

    private val THREAD_NAMES = listOf(
        "gum-js-loop" to "frida",  // Frida 特有
        "pool-frida" to "frida",    // Frida 特有
        // gmain 单独不产出（GLib 合法共用，calibration #9）
    )

    private fun scanThreadNames(): List<Finding> {
        val taskDir = File("/proc/self/task")
        val threads = runCatching { taskDir.listFiles()?.mapNotNull { dir ->
            runCatching { File(dir, "comm").readText().trim() }.getOrNull()
        } ?: emptyList() }.getOrDefault(emptyList())

        return THREAD_NAMES.mapNotNull { (name, framework) ->
            if (threads.contains(name)) {
                Finding(
                    ruleId = "frida.thread.$name",
                    collector = id,
                    confidence = Confidence.B,
                    category = Finding.Category.INSTRUMENTATION,
                    framework = framework,
                    detail = "线程列表中发现 Frida 线程: $name",
                    raw = mapOf("thread" to name),
                )
            } else null
        }
    }

    // ---- L3: RWX 页特征扫描（大小/对齐过滤，calibration #5） ----

    private fun scanRwxPages(): List<Finding> {
        val maps = runCatching { File("/proc/self/maps").readLines() }.getOrDefault(emptyList())
        var consecutiveRwx = 0
        var totalRwxSize = 0L

        for (line in maps) {
            if (line.contains("rwxp")) {
                consecutiveRwx++
                val size = line.split(Regex("\\s+"))[0].split("-").let {
                    (it.getOrNull(1)?.toLongOrNull(16) ?: 0L) - (it.getOrNull(0)?.toLongOrNull(16) ?: 0L)
                }
                totalRwxSize += size
            } else {
                consecutiveRwx = 0
            }
        }

        // Frida agent 典型：≥4 连续 RWX 页 + 总大小 ≥64KB
        // JIT 合法场景通常 ≤2 页且 <32KB（calibration #5 过滤）
        return if (consecutiveRwx >= 4 && totalRwxSize >= 64 * 1024) {
            listOf(
                Finding(
                    ruleId = "frida.rwx.pages",
                    collector = id,
                    confidence = Confidence.B, // B 级；与 maps 字符串共振才 A
                    category = Finding.Category.INSTRUMENTATION,
                    framework = null,
                    detail = "发现 $consecutiveRwx 连续 RWX 页（${totalRwxSize / 1024}KB）—— Frida agent 注入特征",
                    raw = mapOf("consecutive" to consecutiveRwx.toString(), "totalKb" to (totalRwxSize / 1024).toString()),
                ),
            )
        } else emptyList()
    }

    // ---- D-Bus 协议探测（限 /proc/net/tcp LISTEN 端口，calibration #7） ----

    private fun probeDbusPorts(): List<Finding> {
        // 解析 /proc/net/tcp 获取本进程可访问的 LISTEN 端口
        val listenPorts = parseListenPorts()
        val findings = mutableListOf<Finding>()

        for (port in listenPorts) {
            if (isFridaDbusEndpoint(port)) {
                findings += Finding(
                    ruleId = "frida.dbus.port$port",
                    collector = id,
                    confidence = Confidence.B,
                    category = Finding.Category.INSTRUMENTATION,
                    framework = "frida",
                    detail = "端口 $port 响应 Frida D-Bus 协议握手",
                    raw = mapOf("port" to port.toString()),
                )
                break // 找到一个即可
            }
        }
        return findings
    }

    private fun parseListenPorts(): List<Int> {
        val ports = mutableListOf<Int>()
        // 仅取 /proc/net/tcp 中 LISTEN (0A) 状态的端口。
        // 平台边界（2026-11-10 审计实证）：API 28+ 未特权进程读 /proc/net/tcp 为
        // EACCES → runCatching 吞掉后端口列表为空，探测收敛为 27042 兜底。
        // 改端口 Frida 由 maps/线程名/RWX 三防线兜底（spec 已注明门控）。
        runCatching {
            File("/proc/net/tcp").readLines().drop(1).forEach { line ->
                val fields = line.trim().split(Regex("\\s+"))
                if (fields.size > 3 && fields[3] == "0A") { // TCP_LISTEN
                    val portHex = fields[1].split(":").getOrNull(1)
                    portHex?.toIntOrNull(16)?.let { ports.add(it) }
                }
            }
        }
        // 加上 Frida 默认端口
        if (27042 !in ports) ports.add(27042)
        return ports.filter { it in 1024..65535 }.take(20) // 限制最多 20 个端口（时延控制）
    }

    private fun isFridaDbusEndpoint(port: Int): Boolean = runCatching {
        Socket().use { socket ->
            socket.soTimeout = 5 // 5ms connect timeout（calibration #7）
            socket.connect(InetSocketAddress("127.0.0.1", port), 5)
            // 发送 D-Bus AUTH 握手
            socket.getOutputStream().write(byteArrayOf(0) + "AUTH\r\n".toByteArray())
            socket.getOutputStream().flush()
            // 读取响应——Frida 响应含 "REJECT" 或特定 D-Bus 错误模式
            val buf = ByteArray(64)
            val n = socket.getInputStream().read(buf)
            n > 0 && String(buf, 0, n, Charsets.ISO_8859_1).let {
                it.contains("EXTERNAL") || it.contains("ANONYMOUS") || it.contains("REJECTEXTERNAL")
            }
        }
    }.getOrDefault(false)
}
```

`collectors/memory-map/src/main/kotlin/com/detectany/collectors/memmap/MemoryMapCollector.kt`:

```kt
package com.detectany.collectors.memmap

import com.detectany.core.model.Collector
import com.detectany.core.model.CollectorId
import com.detectany.core.model.CollectorResult
import com.detectany.core.model.Confidence
import com.detectany.core.model.Finding
import com.detectany.core.model.ScanInput
import com.detectany.core.nativeplatform.ProbeJni

/**
 * C6 内存映射（任务 3.6）：maps/smaps/私有脏页/soinfo 链表交叉/RWX 自探针/fd readlink。
 * （ART class_loaders_ 链表计数为 native 深遍历项，独立迭代中——本采集器不含，见任务 8.2 回归项。）
 */
class MemoryMapCollector(
    private val mapsReader: () -> List<String> = { java.io.File("/proc/self/maps").readLines() },
    private val smapsReader: () -> List<String> = { java.io.File("/proc/self/smaps").readLines() },
    private val fdNames: () -> List<String> = { ProbeJni.fdNames().toList() },
    private val solist: () -> List<String> = { ProbeJni.solist().toList() },
    private val rwxProbe: () -> Int = { ProbeJni.rwxSelfProbe() },
) : Collector {

    override val id: CollectorId = CollectorId.C6_MEMORY_MAP

    override fun collect(input: ScanInput): CollectorResult = try {
        val maps = MapsParser.parse(mapsReader())
        // 时延优化：smaps 体积大（数 MB），仅当 maps 有可疑路径才解析
        val mapsSuspicious = maps.any { m ->
            val p = m.path ?: return@any false
            p.contains("memfd:") || p.contains("lspd") || p.contains("zygisk") ||
                p.contains("riru") || p.contains("XposedBridge") || p.contains("edxp")
        }
        val smaps = if (mapsSuspicious) MapsParser.parseSmapsPrivateDirty(smapsReader()) else emptyMap()
        val findings = MemoryMapAnalyzer.analyze(maps, smaps, fdNames(), solist(), rwxProbe())
        CollectorResult(
            collector = id,
            status = CollectorResult.Status.OK,
            findings = findings,
            meta = mapOf(
                "mappings" to maps.size.toString(),
                "execAnon" to maps.count { it.executable && it.path == null }.toString(),
            ),
        )
    } catch (e: Throwable) {
        CollectorResult(collector = id, status = CollectorResult.Status.BLOCKED, meta = mapOf("error" to (e.message ?: e.javaClass.simpleName)))
    }
}

/** maps 解析（纯 Kotlin）。 */
data class MapEntry(val start: Long, val end: Long, val perms: String, val path: String?) {
    val executable: Boolean get() = perms.contains("x")
    val writable: Boolean get() = perms.contains("w")
}

object MapsParser {

    fun parse(lines: List<String>): List<MapEntry> = lines.mapNotNull { line ->
        val addr = line.substringBefore(' ').trim()
        if (!addr.contains('-')) return@mapNotNull null
        val s = addr.substringBefore('-').toLongOrNull(16) ?: return@mapNotNull null
        val e = addr.substringAfter('-').toLongOrNull(16) ?: return@mapNotNull null
        val perms = line.split(Regex("\\s+")).getOrNull(1) ?: return@mapNotNull null
        val path = line.trim().split(Regex("\\s+")).lastOrNull { it.startsWith("/") }
        MapEntry(s, e, perms, path)
    }

    /** smaps → (映射起始地址, Private_Dirty KB)。 */
    fun parseSmapsPrivateDirty(lines: List<String>): Map<Long, Long> {
        val out = mutableMapOf<Long, Long>()
        var current: Long? = null
        for (line in lines) {
            val addr = line.substringBefore(' ').trim()
            if (addr.contains('-')) {
                current = addr.substringBefore('-').toLongOrNull(16)
            } else if (line.startsWith("Private_Dirty:")) {
                val kb = line.split(Regex("\\s+")).getOrNull(1)?.toLongOrNull()
                val c = current
                if (c != null && kb != null) out[c] = kb
            }
        }
        return out
    }
}

/** 内存映射分析（纯 Kotlin，主机单测）。 */
object MemoryMapAnalyzer {

    fun analyze(
        maps: List<MapEntry>,
        privateDirty: Map<Long, Long>,
        fdNames: List<String>,
        solist: List<String>,
        rwxProbeResult: Int,
    ): List<Finding> = buildList {
        // 1) memfd / 匿名映射显示名含注入框架特征（Santander/Hunter 实测向量）
        maps.filter { m ->
            val p = m.path ?: return@filter false
            p.contains("memfd:") && (p.contains("zygisk") || p.contains("lsposed") || p.contains("jit-cache-zygisk"))
        }.forEach { m ->
            add(
                Finding(
                    ruleId = "mem.injected_memfd",
                    collector = CollectorId.C6_MEMORY_MAP,
                    confidence = Confidence.B,
                    category = Finding.Category.ZYGISK,
                    framework = if (m.path!!.contains("lsposed")) "lsposed" else "zygisknext",
                    detail = "memfd 映射: ${m.path}",
                    raw = mapOf("path" to m.path!!),
                ),
            )
        }

        // 2) 已知路径字符串（lspd/riru/XposedBridge.jar/libzygisk）
        maps.filter { m ->
            val p = m.path ?: return@filter false
            listOf("lspd", "riru", "XposedBridge.jar", "libzygisk", "edxp").any { p.contains(it, ignoreCase = true) }
        }.forEach { m ->
            add(
                Finding(
                    ruleId = "mem.known_path",
                    collector = CollectorId.C6_MEMORY_MAP,
                    confidence = Confidence.C,
                    category = Finding.Category.HOOK,
                    framework = if (m.path!!.contains("lspd", true)) "lsposed" else null,
                    detail = "可疑映射路径: ${m.path}",
                    raw = mapOf("path" to m.path!!),
                ),
            )
        }

        // 3) libart 可执行段 Private_Dirty COW 脏页（Zygisk patch 痕迹）
        privateDirty.forEach { (start, kb) ->
            val entry = maps.firstOrNull { it.start == start }
            if (entry?.path?.contains("libart") == true && kb > 0 && entry.executable) {
                add(
                    Finding(
                        ruleId = "mem.libart_cow",
                        collector = CollectorId.C6_MEMORY_MAP,
                        confidence = Confidence.B,
                        category = Finding.Category.ZYGISK,
                        framework = null,
                        detail = "libart 可执行段存在 ${kb}KB Private_Dirty COW 脏页（运行时 patch 痕迹）",
                        raw = mapOf("start" to start.toString(), "kb" to kb.toString()),
                    ),
                )
            }
        }

        // 4) fd readlink → /data/adb 路径（注入载体关联）
        fdNames.filter { it.startsWith("/data/adb") }.forEach { fd ->
            add(
                Finding(
                    ruleId = "mem.fd_data_adb",
                    collector = CollectorId.C6_MEMORY_MAP,
                    confidence = Confidence.B,
                    category = Finding.Category.HOOK,
                    framework = null,
                    detail = "fd 指向 /data/adb: $fd",
                    raw = mapOf("fd" to fd.hashCode().toString(), "prefix" to "/data/adb"),
                ),
            )
        }

        // 5) soinfo 链表 vs maps 交叉：linker 权威列表中存在而 maps 不可见 = 隐藏映射
        for (line in solist) {
            val name = line.substringBefore('\t')
            val bias = line.substringAfter('\t').toLongOrNull() ?: continue
            if (name == "[anon-linker]") continue
            val covered = maps.any { m -> bias >= m.start && bias < m.end }
            if (!covered && name.isNotBlank()) {
                add(
                    Finding(
                        ruleId = "mem.hidden_mapping",
                        collector = CollectorId.C6_MEMORY_MAP,
                        confidence = Confidence.B,
                        category = Finding.Category.ZYGISK,
                        framework = null,
                        detail = "linker 列表中存在但 maps 不可见: $name @0x${bias.toString(16)}（maps 抹除痕迹）",
                        raw = mapOf("name" to name, "bias" to bias.toString()),
                    ),
                )
            }
        }

        // 6) RWX 自探针：maps 读取本身被 hook（A 级，物理不变量）
        if (rwxProbeResult == 0) {
            add(
                Finding(
                    ruleId = "mem.maps_tampered",
                    collector = CollectorId.C6_MEMORY_MAP,
                    confidence = Confidence.A,
                    category = Finding.Category.HOOK,
                    framework = null,
                    detail = "RWX 自探针映射在 maps 中不可见 —— maps 读取被 hook/篡改",
                    raw = mapOf("rwxProbe" to "0"),
                ),
            )
        }
    }
}
```
