`collectors/memory-map/src/test/kotlin/com/detectany/collectors/memmap/MemoryMapAnalyzerTest.kt`:

```kt
package com.detectany.collectors.memmap

import com.detectany.core.model.Confidence
import com.detectany.core.model.Finding
import kotlin.test.Test
import kotlin.test.assertEquals
import kotlin.test.assertTrue

/** 任务 3.6 验证：maps/smaps/soinfo 交叉/RWX 自探针分析。 */
class MemoryMapAnalyzerTest {

    @Test
    fun detectsInjectedMemfd() {
        val maps = listOf(
            MapEntry(0x1000, 0x2000, "r-xp", null),
            MapEntry(0x3000, 0x4000, "r-xp", "/memfd:jit-cache-zygisk_lsposed (deleted)"),
        )
        val f = MemoryMapAnalyzer.analyze(maps, emptyMap(), emptyList(), emptyList(), 1)
        assertEquals(1, f.size)
        assertEquals(Confidence.B, f[0].confidence)
        assertEquals(Finding.Category.ZYGISK, f[0].category)
    }

    @Test
    fun detectsKnownHookPaths() {
        val maps = listOf(MapEntry(0x1000, 0x2000, "r-xp", "/data/adb/lspd/liblspd.so"))
        val f = MemoryMapAnalyzer.analyze(maps, emptyMap(), emptyList(), emptyList(), 1)
        assertTrue(f.any { it.ruleId == "mem.known_path" && it.framework == "lsposed" })
    }

    @Test
    fun detectsLibartCowDirty() {
        val maps = listOf(MapEntry(0x5000, 0x6000, "r-xp", "/apex/com.android.art/lib64/libart.so"))
        val f = MemoryMapAnalyzer.analyze(maps, mapOf(0x5000L to 4L), emptyList(), emptyList(), 1)
        assertTrue(f.any { it.ruleId == "mem.libart_cow" && it.detail.contains("4KB") })
    }

    @Test
    fun detectsFdPointingToDataAdb() {
        val f = MemoryMapAnalyzer.analyze(emptyList(), emptyMap(), listOf("/data/adb/modules/zygisksu/x.so"), emptyList(), 1)
        assertEquals("mem.fd_data_adb", f.single().ruleId)
    }

    @Test
    fun detectsHiddenMappingSolistVsMaps() {
        // linker 链表有 libx.so @0x9000，但 maps 中无对应区间
        val maps = listOf(MapEntry(0x1000, 0x2000, "r-xp", "/system/lib64/libc.so"))
        val solist = listOf("/system/lib64/libc.so\t4096", "/data/local/tmp/libx.so\t36864")
        val f = MemoryMapAnalyzer.analyze(maps, emptyMap(), emptyList(), solist, 1)
        assertTrue(f.any { it.ruleId == "mem.hidden_mapping" && it.detail.contains("libx.so") })
    }

    @Test
    fun rwxSelfProbeInvisibleIsStrongFinding() {
        val f = MemoryMapAnalyzer.analyze(emptyList(), emptyMap(), emptyList(), emptyList(), 0)
        val strong = f.single()
        assertEquals("mem.maps_tampered", strong.ruleId)
        assertEquals(Confidence.A, strong.confidence)
    }

    @Test
    fun cleanEnvironmentNoFindings() {
        val maps = listOf(
            MapEntry(0x1000, 0x2000, "r-xp", "/system/lib64/libc.so"),
            MapEntry(0x3000, 0x3400, "rw-p", "[anon:.bss]"),
        )
        val solist = listOf("/system/lib64/libc.so\t4096")
        assertEquals(0, MemoryMapAnalyzer.analyze(maps, emptyMap(), emptyList(), solist, 1).size)
    }
}
```

`collectors/properties/src/main/kotlin/com/detectany/collectors/props/PropertyCollector.kt`:

```kt
package com.detectany.collectors.props

import com.detectany.core.model.Collector
import com.detectany.core.model.CollectorId
import com.detectany.core.model.CollectorResult
import com.detectany.core.model.Confidence
import com.detectany.core.model.Finding
import com.detectany.core.model.ScanInput
import com.detectany.core.model.SignatureEntries
import com.detectany.core.nativeplatform.SystemPropertiesBridge

/**
 * C3 属性快照（任务 3.3）：全量 KV + 特征前缀匹配 + 属性区空洞扫描。
 */
class PropertyCollector(
    private val props: () -> Map<String, String> = { SystemPropertiesBridge().list() },
    private val areaScanner: () -> List<PropertyAreaFile> = { scanPropertyArea() },
    private val signatureEntries: List<SignatureEntries.PropertyEntry> = defaultPropertyEntries(),
) : Collector {

    override val id: CollectorId = CollectorId.C3_PROPERTY

    /** 设备侧直读通道（host 测试注入 stub，不触达此路径）。 */
    private fun nativePropGet(key: String): String? =
        runCatching { com.detectany.core.nativeplatform.PropJni.propGet(key) }.getOrNull()

    /** /dev/__properties__/ 下文件观测（空洞扫描输入）。 */
    data class PropertyAreaFile(val name: String, val size: Long, val readable: Boolean, val ownerUid: Int?)

    override fun collect(input: ScanInput): CollectorResult = try {
        // L1 快路径（2026-11-10 时延修复）：免 800+ 全量枚举与属性区扫描，
        // 仅直读关键键（特征库 keyPrefix 的精确键 + 成对属性两侧）——P8 实测
        // L1 19-20ms 主因即全量枚举回调。L2/L3 维持全量语义。
        val map = if (input.tier == com.detectany.core.model.ScanTier.L1) {
            l1DirectReadMap()
        } else {
            props()
        }
        val areaFindings = if (input.tier == com.detectany.core.model.ScanTier.L1) {
            emptyList()
        } else {
            PropertyAnalyzer.analyzeArea(areaScanner())
        }
        val findings = PropertyAnalyzer.analyze(map, signatureEntries, directRead = ::nativePropGet) + areaFindings
        CollectorResult(
            collector = id,
            status = CollectorResult.Status.OK,
            findings = findings,
            meta = mapOf("count" to map.size.toString()),
        )
    } catch (e: Throwable) {
        CollectorResult(collector = id, status = CollectorResult.Status.BLOCKED, meta = mapOf("error" to (e.message ?: e.javaClass.simpleName)))
    }

    /** L1 直读键集：特征条目精确键 + 成对属性两侧 + verified boot 键。 */
    private fun l1DirectReadMap(): Map<String, String> = buildMap {
        val directKeys = mutableSetOf("ro.boot.verifiedbootstate")
        signatureEntries.forEach { e ->
            if (!e.keyPrefix.startsWith("init.svc.")) directKeys.add(e.keyPrefix)
        }
        PropertyAnalyzer.PIF_PAIRS.forEach { (_, main, mirror) ->
            directKeys.add(main); directKeys.add(mirror)
        }
        directKeys.forEach { k -> nativePropGet(k)?.let { put(k, it) } }
    }

    companion object {
        /** 属性区（resetprop 痕迹：春秋「Property Modified」向量）。ownerUid 预留（Phase 2 经 native 填充）。 */
        internal fun scanPropertyArea(): List<PropertyAreaFile> {
            val dir = java.io.File("/dev/__properties__")
            val files = dir.listFiles() ?: return emptyList()
            return files.map { f ->
                PropertyAreaFile(name = f.name, size = f.length(), readable = f.canRead(), ownerUid = null)
            }
        }
    }
}

/** 属性分析（纯 Kotlin，主机单测）。 */
object PropertyAnalyzer {

    /** 成对属性（Build 侧 vs 镜像侧，PIF/改机伪装残差）：Triple(id, main, mirror) + 置信级 */
    // 2026-11-11 干净华为真机回归实证：全部六对在 OEM 出厂态存在合法不一致——
    // 华为 TAS-AL00（未 root/locked/green）：bootimage.build.tags=dev-keys vs 主指纹
    // release-keys、bootimage fingerprint 为 generic_a15 模板、incremental 语义不同
    // （日期串 vs 版本号）、vendor.product.* 全空。老三对当年仅在 Pixel 验证维持 B
    // 属验证面不足。按准确性原则六对全部 C 级（weight-only），PIF 定性改由
    // verified_boot_lie（A）与 vendor 交叉族承担。
    internal val PIF_PAIRS: List<Triple<String, String, String>> = listOf(
        Triple("prop.build_pair.fingerprint", "ro.build.fingerprint", "ro.bootimage.build.fingerprint"),
        Triple("prop.build_pair.incremental", "ro.build.version.incremental", "ro.vendor.build.version.incremental"),
        Triple("prop.build_pair.tags", "ro.build.tags", "ro.bootimage.build.tags"),
        Triple("prop.build_pair.model", "ro.product.model", "ro.vendor.product.model"),
        Triple("prop.build_pair.device", "ro.product.device", "ro.vendor.product.device"),
        Triple("prop.build_pair.brand", "ro.product.brand", "ro.vendor.product.brand"),
    )

    /** C 级成对（OEM 出厂合法不一致实证，华为 2026-11-11）：全部降为 weight-only。 */
    internal val PIF_PAIRS_C_GRADE = setOf(
        "prop.build_pair.model", "prop.build_pair.device", "prop.build_pair.brand",
        "prop.build_pair.fingerprint", "prop.build_pair.incremental", "prop.build_pair.tags",
    )


    /**
     * @param directRead 直读通道（__system_property_find）：ro.boot.* 不在 foreach 枚举中，
     * 需按名直读。注入式设计保持分析器主机可测（v6 曾直接内联 PropJni 导致 5 个主机
     * 单测 UnsatisfiedLinkError——2026-11-10 审计修复）。
     */
    fun analyze(
        map: Map<String, String>,
        entries: List<SignatureEntries.PropertyEntry>,
        directRead: (String) -> String? = { null },
    ): List<Finding> = buildList {
        for (entry in entries) {
            // 前缀匹配（init.svc.magisk 命中 init.svc.magiskd / init.svc.magisk_p92d 等整族）+ 值条件
            val valueCond = entry.valueContains
            // valueContains=null → 属性存在即命中（如 ro.zygisk.denylists 残留检测）
            val hit = map.entries.firstOrNull {
                it.key.startsWith(entry.keyPrefix) &&
                    (valueCond == null || it.value.contains(valueCond))
            }
            // 对于 ro. 前缀的残留检测：值无关，存在即信号
            if (hit == null && entry.keyPrefix.startsWith("ro.") && valueCond == null) {
                // 精确匹配（非前缀）的 ro. 属性，存在即命中
                val exact = map[entry.keyPrefix]
                if (exact != null) {
                    add(
                        Finding(
                            ruleId = entry.id,
                            collector = CollectorId.C3_PROPERTY,
                            confidence = entry.confidence,
                            category = entry.category,
                            framework = entry.framework,
                            detail = "残留属性存在: ${entry.keyPrefix}=$exact（root 框架残留痕迹）",
                            raw = mapOf("key" to entry.keyPrefix.hashCode().toString()),
                        ),
                    )
                    continue
                }
            }
            if (hit != null) {
                add(
                    Finding(
                        ruleId = entry.id,
                        collector = CollectorId.C3_PROPERTY,
                        confidence = entry.confidence,
                        category = entry.category,
                        framework = entry.framework,
                        detail = "属性命中 ${hit.key}=${hit.value}",
                        raw = mapOf("key" to hit.key, "valueHash" to hit.value.hashCode().toString()),
                    ),
                )
            }
        }

        // PIF 系属性伪装（任务 8.1）：改 Build.* 但改不了 bootimage/vendor 侧镜像值
        // —— 成对属性不一致即基线偏离（PIF 只 hook ro.build.* 读取路径）
        for ((id, mainKey, mirrorKey) in PIF_PAIRS) {
            // 2026-11-11 华为真机审计修复：ro.bootimage.* 族不在 __system_property_foreach
            // 枚举结果中（与 ro.boot.* 同族盲区，shell 可读但 app 枚举拿不到）→
            // fingerprint/tags 两对的 bootimage 侧结构性缺失，生产从未生效。
            // mirror 侧枚举缺失时 directRead 兜底。
            val main = map[mainKey] ?: directRead(mainKey)
            val mirror = map[mirrorKey] ?: directRead(mirrorKey)
            if (!main.isNullOrEmpty() && !mirror.isNullOrEmpty() && main != mirror) {
                add(
                    Finding(
                        ruleId = id,
                        collector = CollectorId.C3_PROPERTY,
                        confidence = if (id in PIF_PAIRS_C_GRADE) Confidence.C else Confidence.B,
                        category = Finding.Category.ATTESTATION_SPOOF,
                        framework = "pif",
                        detail = "成对属性不一致: $mainKey != $mirrorKey（Build 伪装痕迹）",
                        raw = mapOf("mainHash" to main.hashCode().toString(), "mirrorHash" to mirror.hashCode().toString()),
                    ),
                )
            }
        }

        // verified boot 交叉：声称 green 但设备有任何 root 历史痕迹 = 矛盾
        // 用 propGet 直读（ro.boot.* 可能不在 foreach 枚举结果中）
        val vbs = directRead("ro.boot.verifiedbootstate") ?: map["ro.boot.verifiedbootstate"]
        if (vbs == "green") {
            val zygiskResidual = map.keys.any { it.startsWith("ro.zygisk.") }
            val magiskResidual = map.keys.any { it.startsWith("ro.magisk.") || it.startsWith("init.svc.magisk") }
            if (zygiskResidual || magiskResidual) {
                add(
                    Finding(
                        ruleId = "prop.verified_boot_lie",
                        collector = CollectorId.C3_PROPERTY,
                        confidence = Confidence.A, // 物理矛盾：verified boot green + root 残留 = 属性被伪造
                        category = Finding.Category.ANTI_DETECT_PRESENT,
                        framework = null,
                        detail = "verified boot 声称 green 但存在 root 框架残留属性——属性被伪造（深度隐藏特征）",
                        raw = mapOf("vbs" to vbs.hashCode().toString()),
                    ),
                )
            }
        }

        // 通用强信号：ro.debuggable / test-keys
        if (map["ro.debuggable"] == "1") {
            add(
                Finding(
                    ruleId = "prop.debuggable",
                    collector = CollectorId.C3_PROPERTY,
                    confidence = Confidence.C,
                    category = Finding.Category.ROOT_USERSPACE,
                    framework = null,
                    detail = "ro.debuggable=1（工程构建/调试 ROM 特征）",
                    raw = mapOf("key" to "ro.debuggable", "valueHash" to "1".hashCode().toString()),
                ),
            )
        }
        if ((map["ro.build.tags"] ?: "").contains("test-keys")) {
            add(
                Finding(
                    ruleId = "prop.test_keys",
                    collector = CollectorId.C3_PROPERTY,
                    confidence = Confidence.C,
                    category = Finding.Category.ROOT_USERSPACE,
                    framework = null,
                    detail = "ro.build.tags 含 test-keys（自编构建特征）",
                    raw = mapOf("key" to "ro.build.tags", "valueHash" to (map["ro.build.tags"] ?: "").hashCode().toString()),
                ),
            )
        }
    }

    /**
     * 属性区空洞（resetprop 改值后元数据留痕）：
     * - 文件存在但不可读（正常属性文件 0444 全局可读）
     * - size 为 0 的空洞文件
     */
    fun analyzeArea(files: List<PropertyCollector.PropertyAreaFile>): List<Finding> = buildList {
        files.filter { !it.readable || it.size == 0L }.forEach { f ->
            add(
                Finding(
                    ruleId = "prop.area_anomaly.${f.name.hashCode().toUInt()}",
                    collector = CollectorId.C3_PROPERTY,
                    confidence = Confidence.B,
                    category = Finding.Category.ANTI_DETECT_PRESENT,
                    framework = null,
                    detail = "属性区文件异常: ${f.name} readable=${f.readable} size=${f.size}（resetprop 痕迹形态）",
                    raw = mapOf("name" to f.name, "readable" to f.readable.toString(), "size" to f.size.toString()),
                ),
            )
        }
    }
}

internal fun defaultPropertyEntries(): List<SignatureEntries.PropertyEntry> = listOf(
    // Magisk 服务属性族（DroidGuard 2026 仍在探测）
    SignatureEntries.PropertyEntry("prop.init_svc_magisk", "init.svc.magisk", "magisk", Confidence.C, Finding.Category.ROOT_USERSPACE),
    SignatureEntries.PropertyEntry("prop.magisk_version", "magisk.version", "magisk", Confidence.C, Finding.Category.ROOT_USERSPACE),
    SignatureEntries.PropertyEntry("prop.persist_magisk_hide", "persist.magisk.hide", "magisk", Confidence.C, Finding.Category.ROOT_USERSPACE),
    // Riru 遗留（值必须含 loader 名——全机型默认值 "0" 不算命中）
    SignatureEntries.PropertyEntry("prop.riru_bridge", "ro.dalvik.vm.native.bridge", null, Confidence.C, Finding.Category.HOOK, valueContains = "libriruloader"),
)

```

`collectors/properties/src/test/kotlin/com/detectany/collectors/props/PifReplayTest.kt`:

```kt
package com.detectany.collectors.props

import com.detectany.core.rules.BundledRuleSet
import com.detectany.core.rules.RulesEngine
import kotlin.test.Test
import kotlin.test.assertEquals

/** 任务 8.1：PIF 成对属性偏离 → 分析器命中 → 规则回放。 */
class PifReplayTest {

    private val engine = RulesEngine(BundledRuleSet.ruleSet)

    @Test
    fun pifPairMismatchAnalyzerAndRule() {
        val map = mapOf(
            "ro.build.fingerprint" to "google/oriole/spoofed",
            "ro.bootimage.build.fingerprint" to "google/oriole/original",
        )
        val findings = PropertyAnalyzer.analyze(map, emptyList())
        assertEquals(1, findings.size)
        assertEquals("prop.build_pair.fingerprint", findings[0].ruleId)
        assertEquals("pif", findings[0].framework)

        val hits = engine.evaluate(findings)
        assertEquals("pif", hits.first { it.rule.id == "rule.pif" }.rule.framework)
    }

    @Test
    fun pifCleanPairsNoFalsePositive() {
        val map = mapOf(
            "ro.build.fingerprint" to "google/oriole/same",
            "ro.bootimage.build.fingerprint" to "google/oriole/same",
            "ro.build.version.incremental" to "x",
        )
        assertEquals(0, PropertyAnalyzer.analyze(map, emptyList()).filter { it.ruleId.startsWith("prop.build_pair") }.size)
    }
}
```

`collectors/properties/src/test/kotlin/com/detectany/collectors/props/PropertyAnalyzerTest.kt`:

```kt
package com.detectany.collectors.props

import kotlin.test.Test
import kotlin.test.assertEquals
import kotlin.test.assertTrue

/** 任务 3.3 验证：属性分析 + 属性区空洞。 */
class PropertyAnalyzerTest {

    @Test
    fun detectsInitSvcMagiskFamily() {
        val map = mapOf("ro.build.fingerprint" to "google/x", "init.svc.magiskd" to "running")
        val findings = PropertyAnalyzer.analyze(map, defaultPropertyEntries())
        val f = findings.first { it.ruleId == "prop.init_svc_magisk" }
        assertTrue(f.detail.contains("init.svc.magiskd"))
    }

    @Test
    fun detectsDebuggableAndTestKeys() {
        val map = mapOf("ro.debuggable" to "1", "ro.build.tags" to "release-keys")
        val findings = PropertyAnalyzer.analyze(map, emptyList())
        assertEquals(1, findings.size)
        assertEquals("prop.debuggable", findings[0].ruleId)

        val map2 = mapOf("ro.build.tags" to "test-keys")
        assertEquals("prop.test_keys", PropertyAnalyzer.analyze(map2, emptyList()).single().ruleId)
    }

    @Test
    fun cleanEnvironmentNoFindings() {
        val map = mapOf("ro.build.tags" to "release-keys", "ro.debuggable" to "0", "ro.build.fingerprint" to "google/pixel")
        assertEquals(0, PropertyAnalyzer.analyze(map, defaultPropertyEntries()).size)
    }

    @Test
    fun propertyAreaAnomalyDetected() {
        val files = listOf(
            PropertyCollector.PropertyAreaFile("uobject_r0-1234", 112L, readable = true, ownerUid = null),
            PropertyCollector.PropertyAreaFile("uobject_r0-5678", 0L, readable = true, ownerUid = null),     // 空洞
            PropertyCollector.PropertyAreaFile("uobject_r0-abcd", 96L, readable = false, ownerUid = null),   // 不可读
        )
        val findings = PropertyAnalyzer.analyzeArea(files)
        assertEquals(2, findings.size)
        assertTrue(findings.all { it.category == com.detectany.core.model.Finding.Category.ANTI_DETECT_PRESENT })
    }
}
```

`collectors/screen-share/src/main/kotlin/com/detectany/collectors/screenshare/ScreenRecordingMonitor.kt`:

```kt
package com.detectany.collectors.screenshare

import java.util.concurrent.Executor

/**
 * 系统级屏幕录制状态监控（G5，API 34+）。
 *
 * WindowManager.addScreenRecordingCallback(Executor, Consumer<Integer>) 上报
 * 「本应用画面是否正被系统级录制」（状态栏录屏指示器语义，OEM 录屏 + 设置了
 * 指示器的 MediaProjection 均覆盖）。需 Activity 可见期间注册（onStart/onStop）；
 * 扫描时读取最近状态。
 *
 * 降级行为：API <34 或未注册期间 stateVisible=null（未知），不产出 Finding。
 */
object ScreenRecordingMonitor {

    /** 最近一次系统上报的「应用可见于录制」状态；null=未知（未注册/未上报）。 */
    @Volatile
    var stateVisible: Boolean? = null
        private set

    /** 最近一次状态变更时间戳（elapsedRealtime），用于证据呈现。 */
    @Volatile
    var lastChangedAtMs: Long = 0L
        private set

    fun update(visible: Boolean) {
        stateVisible = visible
        lastChangedAtMs = android.os.SystemClock.elapsedRealtime()
    }

    /** 测试重置。 */
    fun reset() {
        stateVisible = null
        lastChangedAtMs = 0L
    }

    /**
     * 由 Activity onStart 调用（API 34+）。返回注销句柄（onStop 调用）。
     * API <34 返回 null（能力缺失，静默降级）。
     */
    fun register(activity: android.app.Activity, executor: Executor): AutoCloseable? {
        if (android.os.Build.VERSION.SDK_INT < 34) return null
        return runCatching {
            val cb = java.util.function.Consumer<Int> { state ->
                update(state == android.view.WindowManager.SCREEN_RECORDING_STATE_VISIBLE)
            }
            activity.windowManager.addScreenRecordingCallback(executor, cb)
            AutoCloseable { runCatching { activity.windowManager.removeScreenRecordingCallback(cb) } }
        }.getOrNull()
    }
}

```

`collectors/screen-share/src/main/kotlin/com/detectany/collectors/screenshare/ScreenShareCollector.kt`:

```kt
package com.detectany.collectors.screenshare

import android.content.Context
import android.hardware.input.InputManager
import android.os.BatteryManager
import android.provider.Settings
import com.detectany.core.model.Collector
import com.detectany.core.model.CollectorId
import com.detectany.core.model.CollectorResult
import com.detectany.core.model.Confidence
import com.detectany.core.model.Finding
import com.detectany.core.model.ScanInput
import java.io.File

/**
 * C12: 屏幕共享/远程控制检测 — scrcpy / ADB / 远程桌面 / overlay。
 * 面向 Collector 接口，由 DetectionEngine 并行调度。
 *
 * 校准定级（calibration.md）：
 * - scrcpy unix socket → A（专属特征）
 * - InputDevice 名含 scrcpy → A（专属）
 * - ADB + USB + 应用前台 → B（三条件组合）
 * - ADB 单独 → C（开发者常用）
 * - 远程桌面 + 无障碍启用 → B；仅安装 → C
 * - overlay + canDrawOverlays → C（过滤器合法）；+ 触摸遮蔽共振 → B
 */
class ScreenShareCollector(
    private val context: Context,
) : Collector {

    override val id: CollectorId = CollectorId.C12_SCREEN_SHARE

    /** 已知远程桌面包名 */
    private val remoteDesktopPackages = mapOf(
        "com.anydesk.anydeskandroid" to "anydesk",
        "com.teamviewer.quicksupport.market" to "teamviewer",
        "com.teamviewer.host.market" to "teamviewer",
        "com.teamviewer.teamviewer.market.mobile" to "teamviewer",
        "com.sand.airdroid" to "airdroid",
        "com.sand.airmirror" to "airmirror",
        "com.koushikdutta.vysor" to "vysor",
        "com.google.chromeremotedesktop" to "chromeremote",
    )

    /** 已知过滤器/辅助工具 overlay 白名单（calibration #15） */
    private val overlayWhitelist = setOf(
        "com.urbandroid.lux", // Twilight
        "com.justgetflux.flux", // f.lux
        "net.dinglisch.android.taskerm", // Tasker (overlay)
    )

    override fun collect(input: ScanInput): CollectorResult = try {
        val findings = mutableListOf<Finding>()
        findings += checkScrcpySockets()
        findings += checkInputDevices()
        findings += checkAdbSession()
        findings += checkRemoteDesktop()
        findings += checkOverlay()
        findings += checkScreenRecordingState()
        CollectorResult(
            collector = id,
            status = CollectorResult.Status.OK,
            findings = findings,
            meta = mapOf(
                "scrcpySockets" to findings.count { it.ruleId.startsWith("screenshare.scrcpy") }.toString(),
                "adbEnabled" to isAdbEnabled().toString(),
                "remoteDesktopApps" to findRemoteDesktopApps().size.toString(),
                "screenRecording" to (ScreenRecordingMonitor.stateVisible?.toString() ?: "unknown"),
            ),
        )
    } catch (e: Throwable) {
        CollectorResult(collector = id, status = CollectorResult.Status.BLOCKED, meta = mapOf("error" to (e.message ?: e.javaClass.simpleName)))
    }

    // ---- 系统级录制状态（G5，API 34+；状态由 ScreenRecordingMonitor 持有） ----

    private fun checkScreenRecordingState(): List<Finding> {
        // null=未知（未注册/能力缺失）→ 不产出；true=系统确认本应用画面正被录制
        val visible = ScreenRecordingMonitor.stateVisible ?: return emptyList()
        return if (visible) {
            listOf(
                Finding(
                    ruleId = "screenshare.recording_active",
                    collector = id,
                    confidence = Confidence.B, // 系统上报的物理状态，非启发式
                    category = Finding.Category.SCREEN_SHARE,
                    framework = null,
                    detail = "系统录屏回调确认：本应用画面正在被录制（状态栏录屏指示器语义，API 34+）",
                    raw = mapOf(
                        "stateVisible" to "true",
                        "lastChangedAtMs" to ScreenRecordingMonitor.lastChangedAtMs.toString(),
                    ),
                ),
            )
        } else emptyList()
    }

    // ---- scrcpy unix socket 检测（A 级） ----

    private fun checkScrcpySockets(): List<Finding> {
        val unix = runCatching { File("/proc/net/unix").readLines() }.getOrDefault(emptyList())
        return if (unix.any { it.contains("scrcpy_", true) }) {
            listOf(
                Finding(
                    ruleId = "screenshare.scrcpy_socket",
                    collector = id,
                    confidence = Confidence.A,
                    category = Finding.Category.SCREEN_SHARE,
                    framework = "scrcpy",
                    detail = "发现 scrcpy 抽象套接字（屏幕镜像正在运行）",
                    raw = mapOf("socket" to "scrcpy_"),
                ),
            )
        } else emptyList()
    }

    // ---- InputDevice 枚举（scrcpy HID / 虚拟设备） ----

    private fun checkInputDevices(): List<Finding> = buildList {
        runCatching {
            val im = context.getSystemService(Context.INPUT_SERVICE) as? InputManager ?: return@runCatching
            @Suppress("DEPRECATION")
            val ids = im.inputDeviceIds
            for (devId in ids) {
                val device = im.getInputDevice(devId) ?: continue
                val name = device.name ?: continue
                if (name.contains("scrcpy", true)) {
                    add(
                        Finding(
                            ruleId = "screenshare.scrcpy_hid",
                            collector = id,
                            confidence = Confidence.A,
                            category = Finding.Category.REMOTE_CONTROL,
                            framework = "scrcpy",
                            detail = "发现 scrcpy HID 虚拟输入设备: $name",
                            raw = mapOf("device" to name.hashCode().toString()),
                        ),
                    )
                }
                if (device.isVirtual && !name.contains("virtual", true)) {
                    add(
                        Finding(
                            ruleId = "screenshare.virtual_input",
                            collector = id,
                            confidence = Confidence.B,
                            category = Finding.Category.AUTOMATION,
                            framework = null,
                            detail = "虚拟输入设备（非物理硬件）: $name",
                            raw = mapOf("device" to name.hashCode().toString()),
                        ),
                    )
                }
            }
        }
    }

    // ---- ADB 会话检测（三条件分级） ----

    private fun isAdbEnabled(): Boolean = runCatching {
        Settings.Global.getInt(context.contentResolver, Settings.Global.ADB_ENABLED, 0) == 1
    }.getOrDefault(false)

    private fun isUsbPlugged(): Boolean = runCatching {
        val bm = context.getSystemService(Context.BATTERY_SERVICE) as? BatteryManager ?: return false
        bm.isCharging && bm.getIntProperty(BatteryManager.BATTERY_PROPERTY_STATUS) == BatteryManager.BATTERY_STATUS_CHARGING
    }.getOrDefault(false)

    private fun isAppInForeground(): Boolean = runCatching {
        val am = context.getSystemService(Context.ACTIVITY_SERVICE) as? android.app.ActivityManager
        val pid = android.os.Process.myPid()
        am?.runningAppProcesses?.firstOrNull { it.pid == pid }?.importance ==
            android.app.ActivityManager.RunningAppProcessInfo.IMPORTANCE_FOREGROUND
    }.getOrDefault(false)

    private fun checkAdbSession(): List<Finding> = buildList {
        val adb = isAdbEnabled()
        if (!adb) return@buildList

        val usb = isUsbPlugged()
        val fg = isAppInForeground()

        if (adb && usb && fg) {
            // B 级：三条件同时满足 = scrcpy/Vysor 会话指示
            add(
                Finding(
                    ruleId = "screenshare.adb_session",
                    collector = id,
                    confidence = Confidence.B,
                    category = Finding.Category.REMOTE_CONTROL,
                    framework = null,
                    detail = "ADB + USB + 应用前台运行（scrcpy/Vysor 会话特征）",
                    raw = mapOf("adb" to "1", "usb" to "1", "foreground" to "1"),
                ),
            )
        } else {
            // C 级：开发者常用
            add(
                Finding(
                    ruleId = "screenshare.adb_enabled",
                    collector = id,
                    confidence = Confidence.C,
                    category = Finding.Category.REMOTE_CONTROL,
                    framework = null,
                    detail = "ADB 调试已开启（开发者常用，非独占作弊信号）",
                    raw = mapOf("adb" to "1", "usb" to usb.toString()),
                ),
            )
        }
    }

    // ---- 远程桌面包指纹 ----

    private fun findRemoteDesktopApps(): List<Pair<String, String>> {
        return remoteDesktopPackages.mapNotNull { (pkg, name) ->
            runCatching {
                context.packageManager.getPackageInfo(pkg, 0)
                pkg to name
            }.getOrNull()
        }
    }

    private fun checkRemoteDesktop(): List<Finding> = buildList {
        val installed = findRemoteDesktopApps()
        if (installed.isEmpty()) return@buildList

        // 检查是否有对应的无障碍服务启用
        val enabledServices = runCatching {
            Settings.Secure.getString(context.contentResolver, Settings.Secure.ENABLED_ACCESSIBILITY_SERVICES) ?: ""
        }.getOrDefault("")

        for ((pkg, name) in installed) {
            val accessibilityActive = enabledServices.contains(pkg, true)
            add(
                Finding(
                    ruleId = "screenshare.remote_desktop.$name",
                    collector = id,
                    confidence = if (accessibilityActive) Confidence.B else Confidence.C,
                    category = Finding.Category.REMOTE_CONTROL,
                    framework = name,
                    detail = if (accessibilityActive) {
                        "$name 已安装且无障碍服务已启用（远程控制活跃）"
                    } else {
                        "$name 已安装（合法远程协助可能；无障碍未启用 = C 级）"
                    },
                    raw = mapOf("pkg" to pkg, "accessibility" to accessibilityActive.toString()),
                ),
            )
        }
    }

    // ---- Overlay 权限检测（白名单制） ----

    private fun checkOverlay(): List<Finding> = buildList {
        val canDraw = Settings.canDrawOverlays(context)
        if (!canDraw) return@buildList

        // 检查是否有白名单外的应用持有 overlay 权限
        // canDrawOverlays 只检查调用者自身——检测其他应用需枚举
        // 简化实现：如果本应用有 overlay 权限，检查是否在白名单
        val selfPkg = context.packageName
        val isWhitelisted = overlayWhitelist.any { selfPkg.startsWith(it) }

        if (!isWhitelisted) {
            add(
                Finding(
                    ruleId = "screenshare.overlay_permission",
                    collector = id,
                    confidence = Confidence.C, // C 级：过滤器/悬浮球合法（calibration #15）
                    category = Finding.Category.OVERLAY_INJECTION,
                    framework = null,
                    detail = "有应用持有悬浮窗权限（可能为 GameGuardian/Mod Menu，也可能为合法过滤器）",
                    raw = mapOf("canDrawOverlays" to "true"),
                ),
            )
        }
    }
}
```

`collectors/virtual-env/src/main/kotlin/com/detectany/collectors/virtualenv/VirtualEnvCollector.kt`:

```kt
package com.detectany.collectors.virtualenv

import android.content.Context
import android.content.pm.PackageManager
import android.hardware.SensorManager
import android.opengl.GLES20
import android.os.Build
import com.detectany.core.model.Collector
import com.detectany.core.model.CollectorId
import com.detectany.core.model.CollectorResult
import com.detectany.core.model.Confidence
import com.detectany.core.model.Finding
import com.detectany.core.model.ScanInput
import java.io.File

/**
 * C10: 虚拟环境/模拟器检测 — 四维交叉（构建属性 / 硬件痕迹 / 容器特征 / GL 渲染器）。
 * 面向 Collector 接口，由 DetectionEngine 并行调度。
 *
 * 校准定级（calibration.md）：
 * - ro.kernel.qemu=1 → A（不可伪装）
 * - /dev/qemu_pipe, /dev/vboxguest → A（硬件设备节点）
 * - Build.CPU_ABI=ARM + cpuinfo=x86 → A（架构声明不一致）
 * - Build.FINGERPRINT 含 generic/goldfish → B
 * - 传感器/基带缺失 → C（需共振，低端设备合法缺失）
 * - SwiftShader GL → C（Android Go 低端设备合法）
 * - 容器路径 user≥10 → 不产出（Work Profile 合法）
 */
class VirtualEnvCollector(
    private val context: Context,
) : Collector {

    override val id: CollectorId = CollectorId.C10_VIRTUAL_ENV

    override fun collect(input: ScanInput): CollectorResult = try {
        val findings = mutableListOf<Finding>()
        findings += checkBuildProperties()
        findings += checkHardwareArtifacts()
        findings += checkCpuArchitecture(input)
        findings += checkSensors()
        findings += checkContainer()
        findings += checkGlRenderer()
        CollectorResult(
            collector = id,
            status = CollectorResult.Status.OK,
            findings = findings,
            meta = mapOf(
                "fingerprint" to Build.FINGERPRINT.takeLast(30),
                "cpuAbi" to (Build.SUPPORTED_ABIS.firstOrNull() ?: "unknown"),
            ),
        )
    } catch (e: Throwable) {
        CollectorResult(collector = id, status = CollectorResult.Status.BLOCKED, meta = mapOf("error" to (e.message ?: e.javaClass.simpleName)))
    }

    // ---- 构建属性检测 ----

    private val EMULATOR_BUILD_PATTERNS = listOf(
        "generic" to "generic",
        "google_sdk" to "google_sdk",
        "sdk_gphone" to "sdk_gphone",
        "vbox86" to "vbox",
        "nox" to "nox",
        "ttVM" to "tencent",
        "ldplayer" to "ldplayer",
        "goldfish" to "goldfish",
        "ranchu" to "ranchu",
    )

    private fun checkBuildProperties(): List<Finding> = buildList {
        for ((pattern, name) in EMULATOR_BUILD_PATTERNS) {
            if (Build.FINGERPRINT.contains(pattern, true) || Build.MODEL.contains(pattern, true) ||
                Build.PRODUCT.contains(pattern, true) || Build.HARDWARE.contains(pattern, true)
            ) {
                add(
                    Finding(
                        ruleId = "emulator.build.$name",
                        collector = id,
                        confidence = Confidence.B,
                        category = Finding.Category.EMULATOR,
                        framework = name,
                        detail = "Build 属性含模拟器特征: $pattern",
                        raw = mapOf("pattern" to pattern),
                    ),
                )
            }
        }

        // A 级：ro.kernel.qemu = 1（不可伪装）
        val qemu = System.getProperty("ro.kernel.qemu") ?: runCatching {
            val pi = Class.forName("android.os.SystemProperties")
            val get = pi.getMethod("get", String::class.java)
            get.invoke(null, "ro.kernel.qemu") as? String
        }.getOrNull()
        if (qemu == "1") {
            add(
                Finding(
                    ruleId = "emulator.qemu",
                    collector = id,
                    confidence = Confidence.A,
                    category = Finding.Category.EMULATOR,
                    framework = "qemu",
                    detail = "ro.kernel.qemu=1（不可伪装的模拟器标志）",
                    raw = mapOf("qemu" to qemu),
                ),
            )
        }
    }

    // ---- 硬件痕迹检测 ----

    private fun checkHardwareArtifacts(): List<Finding> = buildList {
        val paths = listOf(
            "/dev/qemu_pipe" to "qemu",
            "/dev/socket/qemud" to "qemu",
            "/dev/vboxguest" to "vbox",
            "/proc/irq/9/vboxguest" to "vbox",
            "/data/bluestacks.prop" to "bluestacks",
            "/xbin/bstk" to "bluestacks",
        )
        for ((path, name) in paths) {
            if (File(path).exists()) {
                add(
                    Finding(
                        ruleId = "emulator.hw.$name",
                        collector = id,
                        confidence = Confidence.A, // 硬件设备节点不可软件伪造
                        category = Finding.Category.EMULATOR,
                        framework = name,
                        detail = "模拟器硬件痕迹: $path",
                        raw = mapOf("path" to path),
                    ),
                )
            }
        }
    }

    // ---- CPU 架构交叉验证 ----

    private fun checkCpuArchitecture(input: ScanInput): List<Finding> {
        val declaredAbi = Build.SUPPORTED_ABIS.firstOrNull() ?: return emptyList()
        val cpuInfo = runCatching { File("/proc/cpuinfo").readText() }.getOrDefault("")

        val isX86 = cpuInfo.contains("x86", true) || cpuInfo.contains("Intel", true) || cpuInfo.contains("AMD", true)
        val isArm = cpuInfo.contains("ARM", true) || cpuInfo.contains("Cortex", true)

        return if (isX86 && declaredAbi.startsWith("arm")) {
            // A 级：声明 ARM 但实际 x86 = 架构不一致 = 模拟器
            listOf(
                Finding(
                    ruleId = "emulator.cpu_mismatch",
                    collector = id,
                    confidence = Confidence.A,
                    category = Finding.Category.EMULATOR,
                    framework = null,
                    detail = "CPU 架构不一致: Build.CPU_ABI=$declaredAbi 但 /proc/cpuinfo 显示 x86",
                    raw = mapOf("declaredAbi" to declaredAbi, "cpuX86" to "true"),
                ),
            )
        } else if (isX86 && declaredAbi.startsWith("x86")) {
            // CPU_ABI 本来就声明 x86 → 合法 x86 设备（Chromebook 等），不产出
            emptyList()
        } else {
            emptyList()
        }
    }

    // ---- 传感器检测（C 级，需共振）+ 传感器节律（恒定值 = 虚拟传感器物理特征） ----

    private fun checkSensors(): List<Finding> = buildList {
        val sm = context.getSystemService(Context.SENSOR_SERVICE) as? SensorManager ?: return@buildList
        val hasAccelerometer = sm.getDefaultSensor(android.hardware.Sensor.TYPE_ACCELEROMETER) != null
        val hasGyroscope = sm.getDefaultSensor(android.hardware.Sensor.TYPE_GYROSCOPE) != null

        if (!hasAccelerometer && !hasGyroscope) {
            // 传感器全缺失 → C 级（低端设备合法缺失，需与其他维度共振）
            add(
                Finding(
                    ruleId = "emulator.sensors_absent",
                    collector = id,
                    confidence = Confidence.C,
                    category = Finding.Category.EMULATOR,
                    framework = null,
                    detail = "加速度计和陀螺仪均缺失（低端设备合法；需与其他模拟器信号共振）",
                    raw = mapOf("accel" to "absent", "gyro" to "absent"),
                ),
            )
        } else {
            // 节律检测：真实 MEMS 有热噪声，长时间完全恒定 = 虚拟传感器（云手机/部分模拟器）
            addAll(checkSensorRhythm(sm))
        }

        // 基带检测（C 级）
        val hasTelephony = context.packageManager.hasSystemFeature(PackageManager.FEATURE_TELEPHONY)
        if (!hasTelephony) {
            add(
                Finding(
                    ruleId = "emulator.no_telephony",
                    collector = id,
                    confidence = Confidence.C,
                    category = Finding.Category.EMULATOR,
                    framework = null,
                    detail = "无电话硬件（WiFi-only 平板合法；需共振）",
                    raw = mapOf("telephony" to "absent"),
                ),
            )
        }
    }

    /**
     * 传感器节律（恒定值检测）：真实加速度计静止时仍有 LSB 级热噪声抖动；
     * 足量样本内逐字节完全恒定 = 物理不可能 → 虚拟传感器（B 级）。
     * 判定门槛保守：≥16 样本 + 跨度 ≥100ms 全等才产出，规避低端量化噪声极端情形。
     * 采样窗口 ~150ms，不主导扫描时延。
     */
    private fun checkSensorRhythm(sm: SensorManager): List<Finding> {
        val accel = sm.getDefaultSensor(android.hardware.Sensor.TYPE_ACCELEROMETER) ?: return emptyList()
        val samples = java.util.concurrent.ConcurrentLinkedQueue<FloatArray>()
        val latch = java.util.concurrent.CountDownLatch(1)
        var startTime = 0L

        // registerListener 需要带 Looper 的线程；用一次性 HandlerThread
        val thread = android.os.HandlerThread("sensor-rhythm").apply { start() }
        var collected = false
        try {
            val listener = object : android.hardware.SensorEventListener {
                override fun onSensorChanged(e: android.hardware.SensorEvent) {
                    if (startTime == 0L) startTime = android.os.SystemClock.elapsedRealtime()
                    samples.add(floatArrayOf(e.values[0], e.values[1], e.values[2]))
                    // 跨度 >120ms 且样本够即收工
                    if (samples.size >= 24 ||
                        (samples.size >= 16 && android.os.SystemClock.elapsedRealtime() - startTime >= 120)
                    ) {
                        collected = true
                        latch.countDown()
                    }
                }

                override fun onAccuracyChanged(s: android.hardware.Sensor?, a: Int) {}
            }
            val handler = android.os.Handler(thread.looper)
            val registered = runCatching {
                sm.registerListener(listener, accel, android.hardware.SensorManager.SENSOR_DELAY_FASTEST, handler)
            }.isSuccess
            if (!registered) return emptyList()

            latch.await(300, java.util.concurrent.TimeUnit.MILLISECONDS)
            runCatching { sm.unregisterListener(listener) }

            if (!collected || samples.size < 16 || startTime == 0L) return emptyList()
            val spanMs = android.os.SystemClock.elapsedRealtime() - startTime
            if (spanMs < 100) return emptyList()

            val first = samples.first()
            val allConstant = samples.all { it[0] == first[0] && it[1] == first[1] && it[2] == first[2] }
            return if (allConstant) {
                listOf(
                    Finding(
                        ruleId = "emulator.sensor_constant",
                        collector = id,
                        confidence = Confidence.B,
                        category = Finding.Category.EMULATOR,
                        framework = null,
                        detail = "加速度计 ${samples.size} 样本/${spanMs}ms 内逐值完全恒定——真实 MEMS 热噪声下物理不可能（虚拟传感器：云手机/模拟器特征）",
                        raw = mapOf(
                            "samples" to samples.size.toString(),
                            "spanMs" to spanMs.toString(),
                            "value" to "${first[0]},${first[1]},${first[2]}",
                        ),
                    ),
                )
            } else emptyList()
        } finally {
            thread.quitSafely()
        }
    }

    // ---- 容器检测（排除 Work Profile） ----

    private fun checkContainer(): List<Finding> = buildList {
        // 仅在主用户（userId=0）下检测；user≥10 = Work Profile / 副用户，不产出
        val userId = android.os.Process.myUserHandle().hashCode()
        if (userId != 0) return@buildList

        val expectedPath = "/data/data/${context.packageName}"
        val actualPath = context.filesDir?.parentFile?.absolutePath ?: return@buildList

        if (actualPath != expectedPath && !actualPath.contains(context.packageName)) {
            add(
                Finding(
                    ruleId = "virtual.container.path",
                    collector = id,
                    confidence = Confidence.B,
                    category = Finding.Category.VIRTUAL_ENV,
                    framework = null,
                    detail = "数据目录路径异常: 期望 $expectedPath 实际 $actualPath（容器特征）",
                    raw = mapOf("expected" to expectedPath.hashCode().toString(), "actual" to actualPath.hashCode().toString()),
                ),
            )
        }
    }

    // ---- G2: GL 渲染器指纹（calibration #6） ----
    // 模拟器特有的 GL_RENDERER/GL_VENDOR 字符串
    // SwiftShader → C 级（Android Go 低端设备合法使用）
    // Translator/VBox/Bluestacks/ttVM → B 级（仅模拟器出现）

    private data class GlInfo(val renderer: String?, val vendor: String?)

    private fun queryGlInfo(): GlInfo = runCatching {
        val eglDisplay = android.opengl.EGL14.eglGetDisplay(android.opengl.EGL14.EGL_DEFAULT_DISPLAY)
        if (eglDisplay == android.opengl.EGL14.EGL_NO_DISPLAY) return@runCatching GlInfo(null, null)
        val ver = IntArray(2)
        if (!android.opengl.EGL14.eglInitialize(eglDisplay, ver, 0, ver, 1)) return@runCatching GlInfo(null, null)

        val attribs = intArrayOf(android.opengl.EGL14.EGL_RED_SIZE, 8, android.opengl.EGL14.EGL_GREEN_SIZE, 8, android.opengl.EGL14.EGL_BLUE_SIZE, 8, android.opengl.EGL14.EGL_SURFACE_TYPE, android.opengl.EGL14.EGL_PBUFFER_BIT, android.opengl.EGL14.EGL_NONE)
        var numConfigs = IntArray(1)
        val configs = arrayOfNulls<android.opengl.EGLConfig>(1)
        if (!android.opengl.EGL14.eglChooseConfig(eglDisplay, attribs, 0, configs, 0, 1, numConfigs, 0) || numConfigs[0] == 0) {
            android.opengl.EGL14.eglTerminate(eglDisplay)
            return@runCatching GlInfo(null, null)
        }

        val pbufferAttribs = intArrayOf(android.opengl.EGL14.EGL_WIDTH, 1, android.opengl.EGL14.EGL_HEIGHT, 1, android.opengl.EGL14.EGL_NONE)
        val surface = android.opengl.EGL14.eglCreatePbufferSurface(eglDisplay, configs[0], pbufferAttribs, 0)
        val ctxAttribs = intArrayOf(android.opengl.EGL14.EGL_CONTEXT_CLIENT_VERSION, 2, android.opengl.EGL14.EGL_NONE)
        val context = android.opengl.EGL14.eglCreateContext(eglDisplay, configs[0], android.opengl.EGL14.EGL_NO_CONTEXT, ctxAttribs, 0)

        android.opengl.EGL14.eglMakeCurrent(eglDisplay, surface, surface, context)
        val renderer = android.opengl.GLES20.glGetString(android.opengl.GLES20.GL_RENDERER)
        val vendor = android.opengl.GLES20.glGetString(android.opengl.GLES20.GL_VENDOR)

        android.opengl.EGL14.eglMakeCurrent(eglDisplay, android.opengl.EGL14.EGL_NO_SURFACE, android.opengl.EGL14.EGL_NO_SURFACE, android.opengl.EGL14.EGL_NO_CONTEXT)
        android.opengl.EGL14.eglDestroySurface(eglDisplay, surface)
        android.opengl.EGL14.eglDestroyContext(eglDisplay, context)
        android.opengl.EGL14.eglTerminate(eglDisplay)
        GlInfo(renderer, vendor)
    }.getOrDefault(GlInfo(null, null))

    private fun checkGlRenderer(): List<Finding> = buildList {
        val gl = queryGlInfo() ?: return@buildList
        val renderer = gl.renderer ?: return@buildList

        // B 级：仅模拟器出现的字符串
        val emulatorGlPatterns = mapOf(
            "Translator" to "emulator_translator",
            "Bluestacks" to "bluestacks",
            "ttVM" to "tencent_gameloop",
            "VBox" to "vbox",
            "Android Emulator" to "avd",
        )
        for ((pattern, name) in emulatorGlPatterns) {
            if (renderer.contains(pattern, true)) {
                add(
                    Finding(
                        ruleId = "emulator.gl.$name",
                        collector = id,
                        confidence = Confidence.B,
                        category = Finding.Category.EMULATOR,
                        framework = name,
                        detail = "GL 渲染器含模拟器特征: $pattern",
                        raw = mapOf("rendererHash" to renderer.hashCode().toString()),
                    ),
                )
            }
        }

        // C 级：SwiftShader（低端设备合法，calibration #6）
        if (renderer.contains("SwiftShader", true)) {
            add(
                Finding(
                    ruleId = "emulator.gl.swiftshader",
                    collector = id,
                    confidence = Confidence.C,
                    category = Finding.Category.EMULATOR,
                    framework = "swiftshader",
                    detail = "GL 渲染器为 SwiftShader 软件渲染（低端设备合法；需共振）",
                    raw = mapOf("rendererHash" to renderer.hashCode().toString()),
                ),
            )
        }
    }

}
```

`compat/platform/src/main/kotlin/com/detectany/compat/platform/KernelVersion.kt`:

```kt
package com.detectany.compat.platform

/**
 * 内核版本（uname release 解析结果）。
 * 形如 "3.18.35-perf+" / "4.4.302-g1234ab" / "5.15.104-android13-4k"。
 */
data class KernelVersion(val major: Int, val minor: Int, val patch: Int) : Comparable<KernelVersion> {

    override fun compareTo(other: KernelVersion): Int = comparator.compare(this, other)

    companion object {
        private val comparator = compareBy(
            KernelVersion::major,
            KernelVersion::minor,
            KernelVersion::patch,
        )

        /** 解析 uname release 串；无法识别时返回 null（调用方按最保守策略处理）。 */
        fun parse(release: String): KernelVersion? {
            val head = release.substringBefore('-')
            val parts = head.split('.')
            if (parts.size < 2) return null
            val major = parts[0].toIntOrNull() ?: return null
            val minor = parts[1].toIntOrNull() ?: return null
            val patch = parts.getOrNull(2)?.takeWhile { it.isDigit() }?.toIntOrNull() ?: 0
            return KernelVersion(major, minor, patch)
        }
    }
}

```

```kt
/**
 * 平台环境快照：ProbeCap 的全部输入。

 * 本模块保持纯 JVM 以便矩阵单测。

    val apiLevel: Int,
    val targetSdk: Int,
    val kernel: KernelVersion?,
```

`compat/platform/src/main/kotlin/com/detectany/compat/platform/Probe.kt`:

```kt
package com.detectany.compat.platform

/**
 * 受版本 × 内核 × targetSdk 门控的探测能力清单。
 * 依据 design.md D7 兼容矩阵；全部采集器 MUST 经 [ProbeCap] 取能力，
 * 禁止散落的 Build.VERSION 判断与「试探再回退」的裸 syscall 模式。
 */
enum class Probe {
    /** /proc/net/unix 读取（Android 7 红利；10+ 对现代 targetSdk EACCES） */
    PROC_NET_UNIX,

    /** statx：一律禁用 —— Android 7 内核(<4.11)不存在，且 9-11 上未列 seccomp 白名单的调用以 SIGSYS 杀进程 */
    STATX,

    /** memfd_create 裸 syscall：需内核 ≥3.17（3.10 机型 ENOSYS 甚至段错误；bionic 包装 API 30） */
    MEMFD_CREATE,

    /** getrandom：裸 syscall 需内核 3.17、bionic 包装 API 28；降级走 arc4random_buf */
    GETRANDOM,

    /** 从应用可写目录 execve 落盘 ELF：targetSdk ≥29 被 W^X 禁止（Termux 钉死 28 佐证） */
    EXEC_FROM_HOME,

    /** 经可写 fd 映射可执行段（W^X）：targetSdk ≥29 禁止 */
    WX_MAPPING,

    /** key attestation API（setAttestationChallenge 自 API 24 存在；可信度分级在 attestation-verify 内处理） */
    KEY_ATTESTATION,
}

/** 门控裁决。 */
enum class ProbeCapability {
    /** 正常执行 */
    ALLOWED,

    /** 可执行但结果受限/需走替代路径（reason 说明） */
    DEGRADED,

    /** 禁止执行；采集器输出降级标记 */
    BLOCKED,
}

/** 门控结果：裁决 + 机器可读原因。 */
data class ProbeStatus(val probe: Probe, val capability: ProbeCapability, val reason: String)

```

`compat/platform/src/main/kotlin/com/detectany/compat/platform/ProbeCap.kt`:

```kt
package com.detectany.compat.platform

/**
 * 版本 × 内核 × targetSdk 三维门控矩阵（design.md D7 的单一查询入口）。
 *
 * 数据依据（2026-11 调研）：
 * - /proc/net/unix：API ≤8 全体放行；9 起 untrusted_app 按 targetSdk 分域（≤25 豁免、≥26 拒）；
 *   10+ 官方声明 apps cannot access /proc/net。
 * - statx：内核 4.11+ 才存在（Android 7 全系 3.10/3.18/4.4 无）；Android 9-11 seccomp 白名单外 = SIGSYS
 *   杀进程而非 ENOSYS —— 因此本项目无条件禁用，不做任何试探。
 * - memfd_create：内核 3.17+；3.10 机型 ENOSYS 且 ART 记录过旧内核直接段错误的案例，禁止试探回退。
 * - getrandom：bionic 包装 API 28、裸调用需内核 3.17；降级路径 arc4random_buf（全版本可用）。
 * - exec/W^X：targetSdk ≥29（Android 10）禁止 execve 应用 home 目录与可写 fd 映射可执行段。
 * - key attestation：setAttestationChallenge 自 API 24。
 */
object ProbeCap {

    private val KERNEL_MEMFD = KernelVersion(3, 17, 0)
    private val KERNEL_GETRANDOM = KernelVersion(3, 17, 0)

    fun status(probe: Probe, env: PlatformEnv): ProbeStatus = when (probe) {
        Probe.PROC_NET_UNIX -> procNetUnix(env)

        // 无条件禁止：见 Probe.STATX 文档 —— 存在性与 SIGSYS 双重理由，任何环境都不放行
        Probe.STATX -> ProbeStatus(probe, ProbeCapability.BLOCKED, "statx disabled by policy: kernel<4.11 on API24/25 and SIGSYS on 9-11")

        Probe.MEMFD_CREATE -> gateKernel(probe, env, KERNEL_MEMFD, "memfd_create requires kernel>=3.17 (3.10 may crash, never probe-fallback)")

        Probe.GETRANDOM -> when {
            env.apiLevel >= 28 && (env.kernel == null || env.kernel >= KERNEL_GETRANDOM) ->
                ProbeStatus(probe, ProbeCapability.ALLOWED, "bionic getrandom wrapper available")

            else -> ProbeStatus(probe, ProbeCapability.DEGRADED, "fallback to arc4random_buf (full-version available)")
        }

        Probe.EXEC_FROM_HOME -> if (env.targetSdk >= 29) {
            ProbeStatus(probe, ProbeCapability.BLOCKED, "W^X: execve from app home dir blocked for targetSdk>=29")
        } else {
            ProbeStatus(probe, ProbeCapability.ALLOWED, "allowed for legacy targetSdk (not relied upon)")
        }

        Probe.WX_MAPPING -> if (env.targetSdk >= 29) {
            ProbeStatus(probe, ProbeCapability.BLOCKED, "W^X: PROT_EXEC on writable fd blocked for targetSdk>=29")
        } else {
            ProbeStatus(probe, ProbeCapability.ALLOWED, "allowed for legacy targetSdk (not relied upon)")
        }

        Probe.KEY_ATTESTATION -> if (env.apiLevel >= 24) {
            ProbeStatus(probe, ProbeCapability.ALLOWED, "setAttestationChallenge since API 24; trust grading handled downstream")
        } else {
            ProbeStatus(probe, ProbeCapability.BLOCKED, "attestation API requires API 24")
        }
    }

    private fun procNetUnix(env: PlatformEnv): ProbeStatus = when {
        env.apiLevel <= 28 -> ProbeStatus(
            Probe.PROC_NET_UNIX,
            ProbeCapability.ALLOWED,
            "readable on API<=28 (Android 7 dividend)",
        )

        env.apiLevel == 29 && env.targetSdk <= 25 -> ProbeStatus(
            Probe.PROC_NET_UNIX,
            ProbeCapability.ALLOWED,
            "untrusted_app_25 domain exempted on API 29",
        )

        else -> ProbeStatus(
            Probe.PROC_NET_UNIX,
            ProbeCapability.BLOCKED,
            "EACCES on API>=29 for targetSdk>=26 / API>=10; use connect() probing fallback",
        )
    }

    private fun gateKernel(
        probe: Probe,
        env: PlatformEnv,
        min: KernelVersion,
        blockedReason: String,
    ): ProbeStatus {
        val kernel = env.kernel
            ?: return ProbeStatus(probe, ProbeCapability.BLOCKED, "unknown kernel version: conservative block")
        return if (kernel >= min) {
            ProbeStatus(probe, ProbeCapability.ALLOWED, "kernel ${kernel} >= $min")
        } else {
            ProbeStatus(probe, ProbeCapability.BLOCKED, blockedReason)
        }
    }
}

```

`compat/platform/src/main/kotlin/com/detectany/compat/platform/net/SecureChannel.kt`:

```kt
package com.detectany.compat.platform.net

import okhttp3.CertificatePinner
import okhttp3.OkHttpClient
import java.net.InetSocketAddress
import java.net.URL
import java.security.Provider
import java.security.SecureRandom
import java.security.cert.X509Certificate
import java.util.concurrent.TimeUnit
import javax.net.ssl.SSLContext
import javax.net.ssl.SSLSocket
import javax.net.ssl.SSLSocketFactory
import javax.net.ssl.TrustManagerFactory
import javax.net.ssl.X509TrustManager

/**
 * 安全网络通道（design.md D7 / 任务 1.4）：
 * - 自有信任锚（仅信任显式提供的证书，不使用系统信任库）
 * - SPKI pinning（按主机钉住公钥摘要）
 * - 可注入 TLS Provider（Android 侧由 app 注入 GMS Conscrypt Provider——不内置
 *   conscrypt AAR：其 2.5.2 so 为 4KB 对齐，被 16KB 门禁拦截不可发布）
 *
 * 本类保持纯 JVM：Provider 的选择与安装是宿主(app)的责任。
 */
class SecureChannel(
    trustAnchors: List<X509Certificate>,
    pins: Map<String, List<String>> = emptyMap(),
    private val provider: Provider? = null,
    private val connectTimeoutMs: Long = 10_000,
    private val readTimeoutMs: Long = 15_000,
) {
    val trustManager: X509TrustManager
    val sslSocketFactory: SSLSocketFactory

    init {
        require(trustAnchors.isNotEmpty()) { "至少提供一个信任锚" }
        val tmf = TrustManagerFactory.getInstance(TrustManagerFactory.getDefaultAlgorithm())
        tmf.init(SpkiPins.anchorKeyStore(trustAnchors))
        trustManager = tmf.trustManagers.filterIsInstance<X509TrustManager>().first()
        val ctx = if (provider != null) SSLContext.getInstance("TLS", provider) else SSLContext.getInstance("TLS")
        ctx.init(null, arrayOf(trustManager), SecureRandom())
        sslSocketFactory = ctx.socketFactory
    }

    private val certificatePinner: CertificatePinner = pins.entries.fold(CertificatePinner.Builder()) { b, (host, hostPins) ->
        hostPins.fold(b) { bb, pin -> bb.add(host, pin) }
    }.build()

    fun newClient(): OkHttpClient = OkHttpClient.Builder()
        .sslSocketFactory(sslSocketFactory, trustManager)
        .certificatePinner(certificatePinner)
        .connectTimeout(connectTimeoutMs, TimeUnit.MILLISECONDS)
        .readTimeout(readTimeoutMs, TimeUnit.MILLISECONDS)
        .build()

    /**
     * 原生 SSLSocket 协商探测：返回协商出的协议名（如 "TLSv1.3"）。
     * 用于在目标设备上直接验证 TLS 1.3 能力（Android 7 验证用）。
     */
    fun negotiate(host: String, port: Int, timeoutMs: Int = connectTimeoutMs.toInt()): String {
        val raw = sslSocketFactory.createSocket()
        require(raw is SSLSocket) { "factory 未产出 SSLSocket" }
        raw.use { socket ->
            socket.soTimeout = timeoutMs
            socket.connect(InetSocketAddress(host, port), timeoutMs)
            socket.startHandshake()
            return socket.session.protocol
        }
    }

    /** 便捷重载。 */
    fun negotiate(url: URL): String = negotiate(url.host, url.port.takeIf { it != -1 } ?: url.defaultPort)
}

```

`compat/platform/src/main/kotlin/com/detectany/compat/platform/net/SpkiPins.kt`:

```kt
package com.detectany.compat.platform.net

import java.security.KeyStore
import java.security.MessageDigest
import java.security.cert.X509Certificate
import java.util.Base64

/**
 * SPKI pin 工具：计算/承载证书公钥的 sha256/BASE64 pin（OkHttp CertificatePinner 语法）。
 */
object SpkiPins {

    /** 计算证书 SubjectPublicKeyInfo 的 sha256/xxx pin。 */
    fun of(cert: X509Certificate): String {
        val digest = MessageDigest.getInstance("SHA-256").digest(cert.publicKey.encoded)
        return "sha256/" + Base64.getEncoder().encodeToString(digest)
    }

    /** 由锚证书集合构造仅信任这些锚的 KeyStore（忽略系统信任库）。 */
    fun anchorKeyStore(anchors: List<X509Certificate>): KeyStore {
        val ks = KeyStore.getInstance(KeyStore.getDefaultType())
        ks.load(null)
        anchors.forEachIndexed { index, cert ->
            ks.setCertificateEntry("detectany-anchor-$index", cert)
        }
        return ks
    }
}

```

`compat/platform/src/test/kotlin/com/detectany/compat/platform/KernelVersionTest.kt`:

```kt
package com.detectany.compat.platform

import kotlin.test.Test
import kotlin.test.assertEquals
import kotlin.test.assertNull
import kotlin.test.assertTrue

class KernelVersionTest {

    @Test
    fun parsesAndroidReleaseStrings() {
        assertEquals(KernelVersion(3, 18, 35), KernelVersion.parse("3.18.35-perf+"))
        assertEquals(KernelVersion(4, 4, 302), KernelVersion.parse("4.4.302-g9d5a2b3f9c"))
        assertEquals(KernelVersion(5, 15, 104), KernelVersion.parse("5.15.104-android13-4k-o"))
        assertEquals(KernelVersion(6, 1, 0), KernelVersion.parse("6.1"))
        assertEquals(KernelVersion(6, 12, 0), KernelVersion.parse("6.12.0-android16-0"))
    }

    @Test
    fun rejectsMalformedInput() {
        assertNull(KernelVersion.parse(""))
        assertNull(KernelVersion.parse("android"))
        assertNull(KernelVersion.parse("x.y.z"))
    }

    @Test
    fun comparesCorrectly() {
        assertTrue(KernelVersion(3, 17, 0) > KernelVersion(3, 10, 101))
        assertTrue(KernelVersion(4, 14, 0) > KernelVersion(3, 18, 100))
        assertTrue(KernelVersion(3, 10, 9) < KernelVersion(3, 17, 0))
    }
}
```

`compat/platform/src/test/kotlin/com/detectany/compat/platform/ProbeCapTest.kt`:

```kt
package com.detectany.compat.platform

import kotlin.test.Test
import kotlin.test.assertEquals

/**
 * 任务 1.3 验证单测：矩阵关键格。
 * 覆盖：statx 于 9-11 blocked（及全环境 blocked）、/proc/net/unix 10+ blocked（7 红利 allowed）、
 * memfd 按内核 ≥3.17 门控、getrandom 走 arc4random 回退。
 */
class ProbeCapTest {

    private fun env(api: Int, target: Int = 35, kernel: String? = "4.14.186") =
        PlatformEnv(api, target, kernel?.let(KernelVersion::parse))

    // --- statx：策略性无条件禁用（内核缺失 + 9-11 SIGSYS 双重理由） ---

    @Test
    fun statxBlockedOnAndroid9To11() {
        for (api in 28..30) {
            assertEquals(ProbeCapability.BLOCKED, ProbeCap.status(Probe.STATX, env(api)).capability, "api=$api")
        }
    }

    @Test
    fun statxBlockedEverywhereIncludingNewKernels() {
        assertEquals(ProbeCapability.BLOCKED, ProbeCap.status(Probe.STATX, env(24, kernel = "3.10.101")).capability)
        assertEquals(ProbeCapability.BLOCKED, ProbeCap.status(Probe.STATX, env(35, kernel = "6.1.75")).capability)
    }

    // --- /proc/net/unix：Android 7 红利 → 10+ 现代.targetSdk 阻塞 ---

    @Test
    fun procNetUnixReadableOnAndroid7And8() {
        for (api in 24..28) {
            assertEquals(ProbeCapability.ALLOWED, ProbeCap.status(Probe.PROC_NET_UNIX, env(api)).capability, "api=$api")
        }
    }

    @Test
    fun procNetUnixBlockedOnAndroid10Plus() {
        for (api in 30..35) {
            assertEquals(ProbeCapability.BLOCKED, ProbeCap.status(Probe.PROC_NET_UNIX, env(api)).capability, "api=$api")
        }
    }

    @Test
    fun procNetUnixOnApi29DependsOnTargetSdk() {
        assertEquals(ProbeCapability.ALLOWED, ProbeCap.status(Probe.PROC_NET_UNIX, env(29, target = 25)).capability)
        assertEquals(ProbeCapability.BLOCKED, ProbeCap.status(Probe.PROC_NET_UNIX, env(29, target = 26)).capability)
    }

    // --- memfd_create：内核 ≥3.17 门控（3.10 真空，禁止试探回退） ---

    @Test
    fun memfdGatedByKernelVersion() {
        assertEquals(ProbeCapability.BLOCKED, ProbeCap.status(Probe.MEMFD_CREATE, env(24, kernel = "3.10.101")).capability)
        assertEquals(ProbeCapability.BLOCKED, ProbeCap.status(Probe.MEMFD_CREATE, env(25, kernel = "3.10.84")).capability)
    }

    @Test
    fun memfdAllowedOnModernKernels() {
        assertEquals(ProbeCapability.ALLOWED, ProbeCap.status(Probe.MEMFD_CREATE, env(24, kernel = "3.18.120")).capability)
        assertEquals(ProbeCapability.ALLOWED, ProbeCap.status(Probe.MEMFD_CREATE, env(24, kernel = "4.4.302")).capability)
        assertEquals(ProbeCapability.ALLOWED, ProbeCap.status(Probe.MEMFD_CREATE, env(35, kernel = "6.1.75")).capability)
    }

    @Test
    fun memfdBlockedWhenKernelUnknown() {
        assertEquals(ProbeCapability.BLOCKED, ProbeCap.status(Probe.MEMFD_CREATE, env(35, kernel = null)).capability)
    }

    // --- getrandom：API<28 或老内核 → arc4random 回退 ---

    @Test
    fun getrandomFallsBackToArc4randomOnApi24Kernel310() {
        val s = ProbeCap.status(Probe.GETRANDOM, env(24, kernel = "3.10.101"))
        assertEquals(ProbeCapability.DEGRADED, s.capability)
    }

    @Test
    fun getrandomDegradedOnApi24EvenWithNewKernelBecauseBionicWrapperMissing() {
        // bionic 包装 API 28 才有；24/25 即使内核支持也走 arc4random
        assertEquals(ProbeCapability.DEGRADED, ProbeCap.status(Probe.GETRANDOM, env(25, kernel = "3.18.120")).capability)
    }

    @Test
    fun getrandomAllowedOnApi28PlusWithModernKernel() {
        assertEquals(ProbeCapability.ALLOWED, ProbeCap.status(Probe.GETRANDOM, env(28, kernel = "4.4.302")).capability)
        assertEquals(ProbeCapability.ALLOWED, ProbeCap.status(Probe.GETRANDOM, env(35, kernel = "6.1.75")).capability)
    }

    // --- W^X / exec：targetSdk 29 分界 ---

    @Test
    fun execFromHomeBlockedForModernTargetSdk() {
        assertEquals(ProbeCapability.BLOCKED, ProbeCap.status(Probe.EXEC_FROM_HOME, env(30, target = 29)).capability)
        assertEquals(ProbeCapability.ALLOWED, ProbeCap.status(Probe.EXEC_FROM_HOME, env(28, target = 28)).capability)
        assertEquals(ProbeCapability.BLOCKED, ProbeCap.status(Probe.WX_MAPPING, env(35, target = 35)).capability)
    }

    // --- key attestation：API 24 起 ---

    @Test
    fun keyAttestationAvailableSinceApi24() {
        assertEquals(ProbeCapability.ALLOWED, ProbeCap.status(Probe.KEY_ATTESTATION, env(24)).capability)
        assertEquals(ProbeCapability.BLOCKED, ProbeCap.status(Probe.KEY_ATTESTATION, env(23)).capability)
    }
}

```

`compat/platform/src/test/kotlin/com/detectany/compat/platform/net/SecureChannelTest.kt`:

```kt
package com.detectany.compat.platform.net

import com.sun.net.httpserver.HttpsConfigurator
import com.sun.net.httpserver.HttpsServer
import okhttp3.Request
import org.bouncycastle.asn1.x500.X500Name
import org.bouncycastle.asn1.x509.BasicConstraints
import org.bouncycastle.asn1.x509.Extension
import org.bouncycastle.asn1.x509.GeneralName
import org.bouncycastle.asn1.x509.GeneralNames
import org.bouncycastle.asn1.x509.KeyUsage
import org.bouncycastle.cert.jcajce.JcaX509CertificateConverter
import org.bouncycastle.cert.jcajce.JcaX509v3CertificateBuilder
import org.bouncycastle.operator.jcajce.JcaContentSignerBuilder
import java.io.OutputStream
import java.math.BigInteger
import java.net.InetSocketAddress
import java.net.URL
import java.security.KeyPairGenerator
import java.security.PrivateKey
import java.security.SecureRandom
import java.security.cert.X509Certificate
import java.util.Date
import javax.net.ssl.SSLContext
import javax.net.ssl.SSLPeerUnverifiedException
import kotlin.test.AfterTest
import kotlin.test.BeforeTest
import kotlin.test.Test
import kotlin.test.assertEquals
import kotlin.test.assertFailsWith
import kotlin.test.assertTrue

/**
 * 任务 1.4 主机侧验证：TLS 1.3 握手 + 自有信任锚 + SPKI pinning 的通道逻辑。
 * Android 7 设备侧验证（Conscrypt 在真机/模拟器上）见任务 1.4 勾选说明——本测试证明
 * 通道的锚定/pinning/协议协商逻辑在 provider 无关层面成立。
 */
class SecureChannelTest {

    private lateinit var server: HttpsServer
    private lateinit var serverCert: X509Certificate
    private lateinit var caCert: X509Certificate
    private lateinit var otherCaCert: X509Certificate
    private var port: Int = 0

    @BeforeTest
    fun startTls13OnlyServer() {
        val (caCertX, caKey) = genCa("CN=detectany-test-ca")
        val (otherCa, _) = genCa("CN=detectany-other-ca")
        val (serverCertX, serverKey) = genServerCert(caCertX, caKey)
        caCert = caCertX
        otherCaCert = otherCa
        serverCert = serverCertX

        val kmf = javax.net.ssl.KeyManagerFactory.getInstance(javax.net.ssl.KeyManagerFactory.getDefaultAlgorithm())
        val ks = java.security.KeyStore.getInstance(java.security.KeyStore.getDefaultType())
        ks.load(null)
        ks.setKeyEntry("server", serverKey, charArrayOf(), arrayOf(serverCertX, caCertX))
        kmf.init(ks, charArrayOf())

        val ctx = SSLContext.getInstance("TLS")
        ctx.init(kmf.keyManagers, null, null)

        server = HttpsServer.create(InetSocketAddress("localhost", 0), 0)
        server.httpsConfigurator = object : HttpsConfigurator(ctx) {
            override fun configure(params: com.sun.net.httpserver.HttpsParameters) {
                // 仅放行 TLS 1.3：客户端必须以 TLS 1.3 完成协商
                params.protocols = arrayOf("TLSv1.3")
            }
        }
        server.createContext("/") { exchange ->
            val body = "pong".toByteArray()
            exchange.sendResponseHeaders(200, body.size.toLong())
            (exchange.responseBody as OutputStream).use { it.write(body) }
        }
        server.executor = null
        server.start()
        port = server.address.port
    }

    @AfterTest
    fun stopServer() {
        server.stop(0)
    }

    @Test
    fun negotiatesTls13() {
        val channel = SecureChannel(trustAnchors = listOf(caCert), pins = mapOf("localhost" to listOf(SpkiPins.of(serverCert))))
        assertEquals("TLSv1.3", channel.negotiate("localhost", port))
    }

    @Test
    fun pinnedGetOverTls13Succeeds() {
        val channel = SecureChannel(trustAnchors = listOf(caCert), pins = mapOf("localhost" to listOf(SpkiPins.of(serverCert))))
        val response = channel.newClient().newCall(Request.Builder().url(URL("https://localhost:$port/")).build()).execute()
        response.use {
            assertEquals(200, it.code)
            assertEquals("pong", it.body!!.string())
        }
    }

    @Test
    fun wrongPinIsRejected() {
        // 锚正确但 pin 不匹配：证书链验证通过、pinning 失败
        val wrongPin = "sha256/AAAAAAAAAAAAAAAAAAAAAAAAAAAAAAAAAAAAAAAAAAA="
        val channel = SecureChannel(trustAnchors = listOf(caCert), pins = mapOf("localhost" to listOf(wrongPin)))
        val ex = assertFailsWith<Exception> {
            channel.newClient().newCall(Request.Builder().url(URL("https://localhost:$port/")).build()).execute()
        }
        // OkHttp 将 pin 失败包装为 SSLPeerUnverifiedException（或其包装）
        var cause: Throwable? = ex
        var matched = ex is SSLPeerUnverifiedException
        while (cause != null && !matched) {
            cause = cause.cause
            matched = cause is SSLPeerUnverifiedException
        }
        assertTrue(matched, "expected SSLPeerUnverifiedException in cause chain, got $ex")
    }

    @Test
    fun foreignAnchorIsRejected() {
        // 服务器证书由 detectany-test-ca 签发，客户端只信 other-ca：握手必须失败
        val channel = SecureChannel(trustAnchors = listOf(otherCaCert))
        assertFailsWith<Exception> {
            channel.negotiate("localhost", port)
        }
    }

    // --- 证书生成（仅测试） ---

    private fun genCa(subject: String): Pair<X509Certificate, PrivateKey> {
        val kp = KeyPairGenerator.getInstance("RSA").apply { initialize(2048) }.genKeyPair()
        val now = Date()
        val holder = JcaX509v3CertificateBuilder(
            X500Name(subject),
            BigInteger(64, SecureRandom()),
            now,
            Date(now.time + 3600_000L),
            X500Name(subject),
            kp.public,
        )
        holder.addExtension(Extension.basicConstraints, true, BasicConstraints(true))
        holder.addExtension(Extension.keyUsage, true, KeyUsage(KeyUsage.keyCertSign or KeyUsage.cRLSign))
        val cert = JcaX509CertificateConverter().getCertificate(
            holder.build(JcaContentSignerBuilder("SHA256withRSA").build(kp.private)),
        )
        return cert to kp.private
    }

    private fun genServerCert(caCert: X509Certificate, caKey: PrivateKey): Pair<X509Certificate, PrivateKey> {
        val kp = KeyPairGenerator.getInstance("RSA").apply { initialize(2048) }.genKeyPair()
        val now = Date()
        val builder = JcaX509v3CertificateBuilder(
            X500Name(caCert.subjectX500Principal.name),
            BigInteger(64, SecureRandom()),
            now,
            Date(now.time + 3600_000L),
            X500Name("CN=localhost"),
            kp.public,
        )
        builder.addExtension(
            Extension.subjectAlternativeName,
            false,
            GeneralNames(arrayOf(GeneralName(GeneralName.dNSName, "localhost"), GeneralName(GeneralName.iPAddress, "127.0.0.1"))),
        )
        val cert = JcaX509CertificateConverter().getCertificate(
            builder.build(JcaContentSignerBuilder("SHA256withRSA").build(caKey)),
        )
        return cert to kp.private
    }
}
```

`core/engine/src/main/kotlin/com/detectany/core/engine/DetectionEngine.kt`:

```kt
package com.detectany.core.engine

import com.detectany.core.model.Collector
import com.detectany.core.model.CollectorId
import com.detectany.core.model.CollectorResult
import com.detectany.core.model.Finding
import com.detectany.core.model.ScanInput
import com.detectany.core.model.ScanTier
import com.detectany.core.rules.RulesEngine
import java.util.concurrent.CompletableFuture
import java.util.concurrent.ExecutorService
import java.util.concurrent.Executors

/**
 * 检测引擎（任务 4.4 + 时延优化 2026-11-06）：
 * - 并行采集器执行（L2: 总耗时 ≈ max(单采集器) 而非 sum）
 * - L1 快通道（≤10ms）：仅 C1 路径 + C3 属性，共享 daemon 线程池
 * - 逐采集器耗时注入 meta
 */
class DetectionEngine(
    private val collectors: List<Collector>,
    private val rules: RulesEngine,
    private val scoreModel: ScoreModel = ScoreModel(),
    /** P2-1：L3 档专属采集器（硬件证明）；null = L3 退化为 L2 同集 */
    private val attestationCollector: Collector? = null,
    /** L1 快集合 */
    private val fastCollectorIds: Set<CollectorId> = setOf(CollectorId.C1_PATH, CollectorId.C3_PROPERTY),
    /** 并行线程池（daemon：不阻塞 JVM 退出；IO 型采集器为主，核数即够） */
    private val executor: ExecutorService = Executors.newFixedThreadPool(
        Runtime.getRuntime().availableProcessors().coerceIn(2, 4),
    ) { r ->
        Thread(r, "DetectAny-Collector").apply { isDaemon = true }
    },
) {

    data class ScanReport(
        val tier: ScanTier,
        val verdict: ScanVerdict,
        val collectorResults: List<CollectorResult>,
        val degradedCollectors: List<CollectorId>,
        val elapsedMs: Long,
    ) {
        val l1Boolean: Boolean get() = verdict.conclusive
    }

    fun scan(input: ScanInput, tier: ScanTier = ScanTier.L2, extraFindings: List<Finding> = emptyList()): ScanReport {
        val start = System.nanoTime()
        val selected = when (tier) {
            ScanTier.L1 -> collectors.filter { it.id in fastCollectorIds }
            ScanTier.L2 -> collectors
            ScanTier.L3 -> collectors + listOfNotNull(attestationCollector)
        }

        // 并行执行全部选中采集器（单项失败不中断整体，specs：降级透明）
        val futures = selected.associateWith { c ->
            CompletableFuture.supplyAsync({
                val t0 = System.nanoTime()
                val r = runCatching { c.collect(input) }.getOrElse {
                    CollectorResult(c.id, CollectorResult.Status.BLOCKED, meta = mapOf("error" to (it.message ?: it.javaClass.simpleName)))
                }
                val ms = (System.nanoTime() - t0) / 1_000_000
                r.copy(meta = r.meta + ("elapsedMs" to ms.toString()))
            }, executor)
        }

        // 等待全部完成，按原始顺序返回
        val results = selected.mapNotNull { c ->
            futures[c]?.join()
        }

        val findings = results.flatMap { it.findings } + extraFindings
        val hits = rules.evaluate(findings)
        val verdict = scoreModel.score(findings, hits)
        val elapsed = (System.nanoTime() - start) / 1_000_000
        return ScanReport(
            tier = tier,
            verdict = verdict,
            collectorResults = results,
            degradedCollectors = results.filter { it.status != CollectorResult.Status.OK }.map { it.collector },
            elapsedMs = elapsed,
        )
    }

    fun shutdown() {
        executor.shutdown()
    }
}
```

`core/engine/src/main/kotlin/com/detectany/core/engine/ScoreModel.kt`:

```kt
package com.detectany.core.engine

import com.detectany.core.model.Confidence
import com.detectany.core.model.Finding
import com.detectany.core.rules.RuleHit

/**
 * 扫描结论（specs/detection-engine「证据向量输出」）。
 */
data class ScanVerdict(
    val riskScore: Int,
    val conclusive: Boolean,
    val categories: Set<Finding.Category>,
    /** 框架命中分（framework -> 累计权重） */
    val frameworkScores: Map<String, Int>,
    val evidenceVector: List<String>,
    val antiDetectPresent: Boolean,
)

/**
 * 评分模型（任务 4.4，对齐 design.md D4 判定权限）：
 * - 强（A）：单条即可定性（conclusive）
 * - 中（B）：同框架（或同 category 的 hook/zygisk 域）两条 B、或一 B 一 C 共振才定性
 * - 弱（C）：仅加权，永不定性
 * - anti_detect_present 权重高于普通 root
 */
class ScoreModel(
    private val conclusiveThreshold: Int = 50,
) {

    /**
     * 云手机合围信号集（2026-11-10 第四批）：单条全为 C 级（各自存在合法场景），
     * 但真实硬件同时满足 ≥3 项近乎不可能——容器/云手机在 SIM/电池/传感器三个
     * 独立子系统上同时呈现虚拟形态才能凑齐。
     * 注意：no_telephony 与 sim.all_absent 已在采集器侧用 FEATURE_TELEPHONY 门控，
     * WiFi 平板场景（无电话+无传感器）最多凑 2 项，不触发。
     */
    internal val cloudPhoneSignals = setOf(
        "sim.all_absent",
        "battery.framework_only",
        "battery.zero_temperature",
        "battery.level_without_voltage",
        "emulator.sensors_absent",
    )

    fun score(findings: List<Finding>, hits: List<RuleHit>): ScanVerdict {
        val aFindings = findings.filter { it.confidence == Confidence.A }
        val bByDomain = findings.filter { it.confidence == Confidence.B }.groupBy { it.framework ?: it.category.name }
        val cByDomain = findings.filter { it.confidence == Confidence.C }.groupBy { it.framework ?: it.category.name }

        val categories = findings.map { it.category }.toSet()
        val antiDetect = Finding.Category.ANTI_DETECT_PRESENT in categories

        // 框架/域得分：A=40（含 anti_detect ×1.5）、B=15、C=5 + 规则权重加成
        val domainScores = mutableMapOf<String, Int>()
        findings.forEach { f ->
            val domain = f.framework ?: f.category.name
            val base = when (f.confidence) {
                Confidence.A -> 40
                Confidence.B -> 15
                Confidence.C -> 5
            }
            val multiplier = if (f.category == Finding.Category.ANTI_DETECT_PRESENT) 1.5 else 1.0
            domainScores.merge(domain, (base * multiplier).toInt(), Int::plus)
        }
        hits.forEach { h ->
            val domain = h.rule.framework ?: h.rule.category ?: "generic"
            domainScores.merge(domain, h.rule.weight, Int::plus)
        }

        // 定性判定（判定权限矩阵）
        // 2026-11-06 校准：B 共振放宽为「任意域两条 B」——B 为高伪装成本信号
        // （内容指纹/行为探针级），跨域双 B 在干净设备上近乎不可能；原同域限制导致
        // 真机「APatch 管理器(B) + zygiskdetect(B)」误判未达定性（rooted 设备漏定性）。
        // B+C 共振仍要求同域（C 级噪声高，需域内佐证）。
        val conclusive = run {
            if (aFindings.isNotEmpty()) return@run true
            val allB = findings.filter { it.confidence == Confidence.B }
            if (allB.size >= 2) return@run true
            bByDomain.forEach { (domain, bs) ->
                if (bs.size == 1 && (cByDomain[domain]?.size ?: 0) >= 1) return@run true
            }
            // C 级合围共振（云手机形态）：三个独立子系统的虚拟形态同时出现
            if (findings.count { it.ruleId in cloudPhoneSignals } >= 3) return@run true
            false
        }

        // 总分：各域得分开方和（多域共振抬升但不线性爆炸）封顶 100
        val raw = domainScores.values.sumOf { kotlin.math.sqrt(it.toDouble()) }.toInt() * 2
        val score = raw.coerceAtLeast(0).coerceAtMost(100)
        val conclusiveScore = if (conclusive) score.coerceAtLeast(conclusiveThreshold) else score.coerceAtMost(conclusiveThreshold - 1)

        return ScanVerdict(
            riskScore = conclusiveScore,
            conclusive = conclusive,
            categories = categories,
            frameworkScores = domainScores,
            evidenceVector = findings.map { it.ruleId }.distinct(),
            antiDetectPresent = antiDetect,
        )
    }
}
```
