`core/engine/src/test/kotlin/com/detectany/core/engine/DetectionEngineTest.kt`:

```kt
package com.detectany.core.engine

import com.detectany.core.model.Collector
import com.detectany.core.model.CollectorId
import com.detectany.core.model.CollectorResult
import com.detectany.core.model.Confidence
import com.detectany.core.model.Finding
import com.detectany.core.model.ScanInput
import com.detectany.core.model.ScanTier
import com.detectany.core.rules.BundledRuleSet
import com.detectany.core.rules.RulesEngine
import kotlin.test.Test
import kotlin.test.assertEquals
import kotlin.test.assertTrue

/** 任务 4.3/4.4/4.5 验证：规则前缀匹配 + 引擎编排/L1 子集/降级透明 + 各框架样例观测回放。 */
class DetectionEngineTest {

    private class FixedCollector(
        override val id: CollectorId,
        private val result: CollectorResult,
    ) : Collector {
        override fun collect(input: ScanInput): CollectorResult = result
    }

    private class ThrowingCollector(override val id: CollectorId) : Collector {
        override fun collect(input: ScanInput): CollectorResult = error("collector exploded")
    }

    private fun finding(ruleId: String, c: Confidence, fw: String? = null, cat: Finding.Category = Finding.Category.ROOT_KERNEL) =
        Finding(ruleId, CollectorId.C8_BEHAVIOR, c, cat, fw, "replay", emptyMap())

    private fun engine(vararg collectors: Collector): DetectionEngine =
        DetectionEngine(collectors.toList(), RulesEngine(BundledRuleSet.ruleSet))

    private val input = ScanInput(ScanTier.L2, 35, 35, "6.1.75", "arm64-v8a")

    // ---- 4.3 规则引擎 ----

    @Test
    fun rulePrefixMatching() {
        val hits = RulesEngine(BundledRuleSet.ruleSet).evaluate(
            listOf(
                finding("app.ksu", Confidence.B, "ksu"),
                finding("behavior.ksu_supercall", Confidence.B, "ksu"),
                finding("unrelated_rule_id", Confidence.C),
            ),
        )
        assertTrue(hits.any { it.rule.id == "rule.ksu" && it.matchedFindingRuleIds.size == 2 })
        assertTrue(hits.none { it.matchedFindingRuleIds.contains("unrelated_rule_id") })
    }

    // ---- 4.4 引擎编排 ----

    @Test
    fun l1RunsOnlyFastCollectors() {
        val slow = FixedCollector(
            CollectorId.C8_BEHAVIOR,
            CollectorResult(CollectorId.C8_BEHAVIOR, CollectorResult.Status.OK, listOf(finding("behavior.ksu_supercall", Confidence.B, "ksu"))),
        )
        val fast = FixedCollector(CollectorId.C1_PATH, CollectorResult(CollectorId.C1_PATH, CollectorResult.Status.OK))
        val report = engine(slow, fast).scan(input, ScanTier.L1)
        assertEquals(listOf(CollectorId.C1_PATH), report.collectorResults.map { it.collector })
    }

    @Test
    fun throwingCollectorDoesNotAbortScan() {
        val ok = FixedCollector(CollectorId.C1_PATH, CollectorResult(CollectorId.C1_PATH, CollectorResult.Status.OK))
        val report = engine(ThrowingCollector(CollectorId.C2_MOUNT), ok).scan(input, ScanTier.L2)
        assertEquals(2, report.collectorResults.size)
        assertTrue(CollectorId.C2_MOUNT in report.degradedCollectors)
        assertEquals(CollectorResult.Status.BLOCKED, report.collectorResults.first { it.collector == CollectorId.C2_MOUNT }.status)
    }

    // ---- 4.5 框架样例观测回放（design.md D9 主信号） ----

    @Test
    fun replayKsuSupercallPlusManagerIsConclusive() {
        val report = engine(
            FixedCollector(
                CollectorId.C8_BEHAVIOR,
                CollectorResult(
                    CollectorId.C8_BEHAVIOR,
                    CollectorResult.Status.OK,
                    listOf(
                        finding("behavior.ksu_supercall", Confidence.B, "ksu"),
                        finding("app.ksu", Confidence.B, "ksu"),
                    ),
                ),
            ),
        ).scan(input)
        assertTrue(report.verdict.conclusive)
        assertTrue((report.verdict.frameworkScores["ksu"] ?: 0) > 0)
    }

    @Test
    fun replayMagiskWeakPlusManagerResonates() {
        val report = engine(
            FixedCollector(
                CollectorId.C5_APP_FINGERPRINT,
                CollectorResult(
                    CollectorId.C5_APP_FINGERPRINT,
                    CollectorResult.Status.OK,
                    listOf(
                        finding("app.magisk", Confidence.B, "magisk"),
                        finding("path.debug_ramdisk.magisk", Confidence.C, "magisk", Finding.Category.ROOT_USERSPACE),
                    ),
                ),
            ),
        ).scan(input)
        assertTrue(report.verdict.conclusive)
        assertTrue((report.verdict.frameworkScores["magisk"] ?: 0) > 0)
    }

    @Test
    fun replayStrongHookSignalAloneIsConclusive() {
        val report = engine(
            FixedCollector(
                CollectorId.C7_CODE_INTEGRITY,
                CollectorResult(
                    CollectorId.C7_CODE_INTEGRITY,
                    CollectorResult.Status.OK,
                    listOf(finding("integrity.prologue.x", Confidence.A, null, Finding.Category.HOOK)),
                ),
            ),
        ).scan(input)
        assertTrue(report.verdict.conclusive)
        assertTrue(Finding.Category.HOOK in report.verdict.categories)
    }

    @Test
    fun replayAntiDetectEnvironmentFlagged() {
        val report = engine(
            FixedCollector(
                CollectorId.C2_MOUNT,
                CollectorResult(
                    CollectorId.C2_MOUNT,
                    CollectorResult.Status.OK,
                    listOf(finding("mount.too_clean", Confidence.B, null, Finding.Category.ANTI_DETECT_PRESENT)),
                ),
            ),
        ).scan(input)
        assertTrue(report.verdict.antiDetectPresent)
    }

    @Test
    fun cleanScanVerdictIsQuiet() {
        val report = engine(FixedCollector(CollectorId.C1_PATH, CollectorResult(CollectorId.C1_PATH, CollectorResult.Status.OK))).scan(input)
        assertEquals(0, report.verdict.riskScore)
        assertTrue(report.verdict.evidenceVector.isEmpty())
        assertFalse2(report.verdict.conclusive)
    }

    private fun assertFalse2(b: Boolean) = kotlin.test.assertFalse(b)
}
```

`core/engine/src/test/kotlin/com/detectany/core/engine/ExtendedRulesReplayTest.kt`:

```kt
package com.detectany.core.engine

import com.detectany.core.model.CollectorId
import com.detectany.core.model.Confidence
import com.detectany.core.model.Finding
import com.detectany.core.rules.BundledRuleSet
import com.detectany.core.rules.RulesEngine
import kotlin.test.Test
import kotlin.test.assertEquals
import kotlin.test.assertTrue

/** 任务 8.1 验证：扩展规则条目的样例观测回放。 */
class ExtendedRulesReplayTest {

    private fun f(ruleId: String, fw: String? = null, cat: Finding.Category = Finding.Category.ROOT_KERNEL, c: Confidence = Confidence.B) =
        Finding(ruleId, CollectorId.C8_BEHAVIOR, c, cat, fw, "replay", emptyMap())

    private val engine = RulesEngine(BundledRuleSet.ruleSet)

    @Test
    fun lsppatchVectorViaRepackagedFindings() {
        // LSPatch 场景：目标 app 被重打包 → 自保护签名钉住命中（A）+ 嵌入 lspd 运行时 maps 痕迹
        val hits = engine.evaluate(
            listOf(
                f("selfguard.repackaged_signature", null, Finding.Category.REPACKAGED, Confidence.A),
                f("mem.known_path", "lsposed", Finding.Category.HOOK, Confidence.C),
            ),
        )
        assertTrue(hits.any { it.rule.id == "rule.repackaged" })
        assertTrue(hits.any { it.rule.id == "rule.lsposed" })
    }

    @Test
    fun trickystoreVectorViaAttestationFindings() {
        val hits = engine.evaluate(
            listOf(
                f("attest.keybox_revoked", "trickystore", Finding.Category.ATTESTATION_SPOOF, Confidence.A),
                f("attest.cross.bootloader_lock_mismatch", "trickystore", Finding.Category.ATTESTATION_SPOOF, Confidence.A),
            ),
        )
        val hit = hits.first { it.rule.id == "rule.trickystore" }
        assertEquals("trickystore", hit.rule.framework)
    }

    @Test
    fun susfsVectorViaFstypeCross() {
        val hits = engine.evaluate(listOf(f("mount.fstype_cross.123", null, Finding.Category.ROOT_KERNEL, Confidence.A)))
        assertEquals("susfs", hits.first { it.rule.id == "rule.susfs" }.rule.framework)
    }

    @Test
    fun shamikoVectorViaTooClean() {
        val hits = engine.evaluate(listOf(f("mount.too_clean", null, Finding.Category.ANTI_DETECT_PRESENT, Confidence.B)))
        assertTrue(hits.any { it.rule.id == "rule.anti_detect" })
    }


}
```

`core/engine/src/test/kotlin/com/detectany/core/engine/ScoreModelTest.kt`:

```kt
package com.detectany.core.engine

import com.detectany.core.model.CollectorId
import com.detectany.core.model.Confidence
import com.detectany.core.model.Finding
import kotlin.test.Test
import kotlin.test.assertEquals
import kotlin.test.assertFalse
import kotlin.test.assertTrue

/**
 * 任务 4.4 验证：判定权限矩阵（单 B 不定性、强单条定性、C 仅加权、anti_detect 权重更高）。
 */
class ScoreModelTest {

    private fun f(ruleId: String, c: Confidence, fw: String? = null, cat: Finding.Category = Finding.Category.ROOT_KERNEL) =
        Finding(ruleId, CollectorId.C8_BEHAVIOR, c, cat, fw, "test", emptyMap())

    private val model = ScoreModel()

    @Test
    fun singleBIsNotConclusive() {
        val v = model.score(listOf(f("behavior.ksu_supercall", Confidence.B, "ksu")), emptyList())
        assertFalse(v.conclusive)
        assertTrue(v.riskScore < 50)
    }

    @Test
    fun singleAIsConclusive() {
        val v = model.score(listOf(f("behavior.triple_diff", Confidence.A, null, Finding.Category.HOOK)), emptyList())
        assertTrue(v.conclusive)
        assertTrue(v.riskScore >= 50)
        assertEquals(listOf("behavior.triple_diff"), v.evidenceVector)
    }

    @Test
    fun twoBInSameFrameworkResonate() {
        val v = model.score(
            listOf(
                f("behavior.ksu_supercall", Confidence.B, "ksu"),
                f("app.ksu", Confidence.B, "ksu"),
            ),
            emptyList(),
        )
        assertTrue(v.conclusive)
        assertTrue(v.frameworkScores.containsKey("ksu"))
    }

    @Test
    fun bPlusCInSameFrameworkResonate() {
        val v = model.score(
            listOf(
                f("app.magisk", Confidence.B, "magisk"),
                f("path.debug_ramdisk.magisk", Confidence.C, "magisk"),
            ),
            emptyList(),
        )
        assertTrue(v.conclusive)
    }

    @Test
    fun cOnlyNeverConclusive() {
        val v = model.score(
            (0 until 5).map { f("path.su.$it", Confidence.C, null) },
            emptyList(),
        )
        assertFalse(v.conclusive)
        assertTrue(v.riskScore < 50)
    }

    @Test
    fun crossDomainTwoBsResonate() {
        // 2026-11-06 校准：跨域双 B 也共振（真机教训：APatch B + zygiskdetect B 曾误判未定性）
        val v = model.score(
            listOf(
                f("behavior.ksu_supercall", Confidence.B, "ksu"),
                f("app.magisk", Confidence.B, "magisk"),
            ),
            emptyList(),
        )
        assertTrue(v.conclusive)
    }

    @Test
    fun rootedDeviceManagerPlusToolIsConclusive() {
        // 真机实证形态：root 管理器内容指纹(B, ROOT_KERNEL) + zygisk 工具(B, ZYGISK)
        val v = model.score(
            listOf(
                Finding("app.apatch", CollectorId.C5_APP_FINGERPRINT, Confidence.B, Finding.Category.ROOT_KERNEL, "apatch", "t", emptyMap()),
                Finding("app.zygiskdetect", CollectorId.C5_APP_FINGERPRINT, Confidence.B, Finding.Category.ZYGISK, "zygiskdetect", "t", emptyMap()),
            ),
            emptyList(),
        )
        assertTrue(v.conclusive, "rooted 设备双 B 必须定性")
        assertTrue(v.riskScore >= 50)
    }

    @Test
    fun antiDetectWeightsHigherThanPlainRoot() {
        val plain = model.score(listOf(f("x", Confidence.B, "magisk")), emptyList())
        val anti = model.score(listOf(f("mount.too_clean", Confidence.B, null, Finding.Category.ANTI_DETECT_PRESENT)), emptyList())
        assertTrue(anti.riskScore >= plain.riskScore)
        assertTrue(anti.antiDetectPresent)
        assertFalse(plain.antiDetectPresent)
    }

    @Test
    fun cloudPhoneTripleCResonanceIsConclusive() {
        // 云手机形态：SIM 缺席 + 内核零供电 + 冰点温度（三个独立子系统 C 级合围）
        val findings = listOf(
            f("sim.all_absent", Confidence.C, cat = Finding.Category.SIM_ANOMALY),
            f("battery.framework_only", Confidence.C, cat = Finding.Category.VIRTUAL_ENV),
            f("battery.zero_temperature", Confidence.C, cat = Finding.Category.VIRTUAL_ENV),
        )
        val v = ScoreModel().score(findings, emptyList())
        assertTrue(v.conclusive, "三子系统 C 合围应定性")
        assertTrue(v.riskScore >= 50)
    }

    @Test
    fun twoCloudSignalsNotConclusive() {
        // 廉价平板场景：无传感器 + 电压缺失 = 最多 2 项，不得定性
        val findings = listOf(
            f("emulator.sensors_absent", Confidence.C, cat = Finding.Category.EMULATOR),
            f("battery.level_without_voltage", Confidence.C, cat = Finding.Category.VIRTUAL_ENV),
            f("emulator.no_telephony", Confidence.C, cat = Finding.Category.EMULATOR), // 不在集合内
        )
        val v = ScoreModel().score(findings, emptyList())
        assertFalse(v.conclusive, "2 项集合内信号不得定性")
    }

}
```

`core/model/src/main/kotlin/com/detectany/core/model/Collector.kt`:

```kt
package com.detectany.core.model

/**
 * 采集器 SPI：C1-C12 的统一入口（specs/signal-collection）。
 * 采集器只输出取证观测（Finding）——框架无关、无判定逻辑；
 * 规则与评分在引擎侧（core:engine + core:rules）完成。
 *
 * 架构约束（面向接口编程）：
 * - 全部采集器 MUST 实现 [Collector] 接口
 * - 全部检测项 MUST 经 DetectionEngine 并行调度，无特殊路径
 * - 特征常量 MUST 来自 SignatureDb（可热更新），采集器零硬编码框架逻辑
 */
interface Collector {
    val id: CollectorId

    /**
     * 执行采集。能力被兼容矩阵阻塞时返回 status=BLOCKED 的降级结果，
     * MUST NOT 抛异常中断整体扫描（specs/detection-engine「采集能力降级透明」）。
     */
    fun collect(input: ScanInput): CollectorResult
}

/** 采集器编号。C1-C8 既有，C9-C12 新增（close-detection-gaps）。 */
enum class CollectorId {
    // ---- 既有（root/hook/injection 域） ----
    C1_PATH,
    C2_MOUNT,
    C3_PROPERTY,
    C4_ENDPOINT,
    C5_APP_FINGERPRINT,
    C6_MEMORY_MAP,
    C7_CODE_INTEGRITY,
    C8_BEHAVIOR,

    // ---- 新增（instrumentation/virtual-env/behavioral/screen-share 域） ----
    /** C9: Frida 检测（D-Bus 探测、线程名、RWX 页特征） */
    C9_INSTRUMENTATION,
    /** C10: 虚拟环境/模拟器（传感器、GL、容器、CPU 架构） */
    C10_VIRTUAL_ENV,
    /** C11: 行为异常（VPN、代理、无障碍、时钟、GPS） */
    C11_BEHAVIORAL,
    /** C12: 屏幕共享/远程控制（scrcpy、ADB、overlay、输入注入） */
    C12_SCREEN_SHARE,

    /** P2-1（2026-11-11）：硬件证明域（L3 档专用，Keymaster 异步 + 超时治理） */
    C13_ATTESTATION,
}

/** 扫描输入：环境快照 + 档位。 */
data class ScanInput(
    val tier: ScanTier,
    val apiLevel: Int,
    val targetSdk: Int,
    val kernelRelease: String?,
    val abi: String?,
)

/** 采集结果：状态 + 观测（Finding）列表 + 元信息。 */
data class CollectorResult(
    val collector: CollectorId,
    val status: Status,
    val findings: List<Finding> = emptyList(),
    val meta: Map<String, String> = emptyMap(),
) {
    enum class Status { OK, DEGRADED, BLOCKED }
}

/** 单条取证观测。 */
data class Finding(
    /** 特征条目 ID（内置或热更新下发，如 "path.debug_ramdisk_zygisk"） */
    val ruleId: String,
    val collector: CollectorId,
    val confidence: Confidence,
    /** 分类标签 */
    val category: Category,
    /** 命中框架（magisk/ksu/frida/scrcpy/...；泛 hook 类可为 null） */
    val framework: String?,
    /** 证据回放用：人可读的原始观测描述 */
    val detail: String,
    /** 结构化原始观测（出端必须脱敏） */
    val raw: Map<String, String> = emptyMap(),
) {
    enum class Category {
        // ---- 既有 ----
        ROOT_KERNEL,
        ROOT_USERSPACE,
        HOOK,
        ZYGISK,
        ATTESTATION_SPOOF,
        REPACKAGED,
        ANTI_DETECT_PRESENT,

        // ---- 新增（close-detection-gaps） ----
        /** Frida / objection / 动态插桩 */
        INSTRUMENTATION,
        /** 虚拟机 / 沙箱容器 */
        VIRTUAL_ENV,
        /** 模拟器 */
        EMULATOR,
        /** VPN / 代理 / MITM */
        NETWORK_MITM,
        /** 自动化脚本 / 输入注入 */
        AUTOMATION,
        /** 速度篡改 */
        SPEED_HACK,
        /** GPS 伪造 */
        GPS_SPOOF,
        /** 摄像头伪造（虚拟摄像头 / 换脸注入） */
        CAMERA_SPOOF,
        /** 设备身份伪造（IMEI/Android ID/序列号伪造工具） */
        DEVICE_SPOOF,
        /** SIM 卡状态异常（矛盾态/缺失态） */
        SIM_ANOMALY,
        /** 屏幕共享 / 投屏 */
        SCREEN_SHARE,
        /** 远程控制 */
        REMOTE_CONTROL,
        /** 悬浮窗 / tapjacking */
        OVERLAY_INJECTION,
    }
}
```

`core/model/src/main/kotlin/com/detectany/core/model/Confidence.kt`:

```kt
package com.detectany.core.model

/**
 * 证据置信级别（对齐 design.md D4 / specs/detection-engine 判定权限）。
 */
enum class Confidence(val weight: Int) {
    /** 强：物理不变量，单信号即可推高风险分 */
    A(40),

    /** 中：行为/状态差异，需两条及以上共振参与定性 */
    B(15),

    /** 弱：静态痕迹，仅加权，不单独触发处置级结论 */
    C(5),
}
```

`core/model/src/main/kotlin/com/detectany/core/model/ScanTier.kt`:

```kt
package com.detectany.core.model

/**
 * 扫描档位（对齐 specs/detection-engine：L1 ≤10ms 快信号、L2 ≤100ms 全量、L3 异步含证明）。
 */
enum class ScanTier {
    /** 快通道：仅弱级快信号布尔结果 */
    L1,

    /** 标准：全量采集 + 评分 + 证据向量 */
    L2,

    /** 完整：L2 + 硬件证明发起（异步部分不计入时延预算） */
    L3,
}
```

`core/model/src/main/kotlin/com/detectany/core/model/SignatureEntries.kt`:

```kt
package com.detectany.core.model

/**
 * 内置/可下发的特征条目（Phase 1 子集；完整 schema 在任务 4.1 落地）。
 * 采集器按条目 ID 匹配原始观测产出 Finding。
 */
data class SignatureEntries(
    val paths: List<PathEntry> = emptyList(),
    val sockets: List<SocketEntry> = emptyList(),
    val properties: List<PropertyEntry> = emptyList(),
    val apps: List<AppEntry> = emptyList(),
    val filesystems: List<FilesystemEntry> = emptyList(),
    val deviceBaselines: List<DeviceBaselineEntry> = emptyList(),
) {
    data class PathEntry(
        val id: String,
        val path: String,
        val framework: String?,
        val confidence: Confidence,
        val category: Finding.Category,
    )

    data class SocketEntry(
        val id: String,
        /** /proc/net/unix 中的名字（抽象名或文件路径） */
        val name: String,
        val framework: String?,
        val confidence: Confidence,
        val category: Finding.Category,
    )

    data class PropertyEntry(
        val id: String,
        /** 属性名，支持前缀匹配（如 init.svc.magisk） */
        val keyPrefix: String,
        val framework: String?,
        val confidence: Confidence,
        val category: Finding.Category,
        /** 值条件：非 null 时值必须包含该子串才命中（如 native.bridge 必须含 loader 名，排除全机型默认值 "0"） */
        val valueContains: String? = null,
    )

    /** 机型挂载基线（P2-2：too_clean/st_dev 消费侧接线，2026-11-11） */
    data class DeviceBaselineEntry(
        val model: String,
        /** 挂载数下限观测（同机型实测最小值；低于此 = too_clean 门槛） */
        val mountCountMin: Int,
        /** 关键路径 st_dev 期望（tmpfs 设备号；跨重启稳定性未验证 → 消费侧 C 级） */
        val stDev: Map<String, Long> = emptyMap(),
    )

    data class FilesystemEntry(
        val id: String,
        /** /proc/filesystems 行内文件系统名（nodev 前缀已剥离） */
        val fsName: String,
        val framework: String?,
        val confidence: Confidence,
        val category: Finding.Category,
    )

    data class AppEntry(
        val id: String,
        /** 候选包名（含官方 spoofed 变体） */
        val packageNames: List<String>,
        /** 签名证书 sha256/xxx 钉住（可选，命中包名后强校验） */
        val certPins: List<String> = emptyList(),
        /** DEX 内容探针字符串（内容指纹：spoofed 包名的兜底路径） */
        val dexStrings: List<String> = emptyList(),
        val framework: String,
        val confidence: Confidence,
        val category: Finding.Category,
    )
}
```

`core/native-platform/src/androidTest/kotlin/com/detectany/core/nativeplatform/NativeSmokeTest.kt`:

```kt
package com.detectany.core.nativeplatform

import androidx.test.ext.junit.runners.AndroidJUnit4
import java.io.File
import org.junit.Assert.assertEquals
import org.junit.Assert.assertNotNull
import org.junit.Assert.assertTrue
import org.junit.Test
import org.junit.runner.RunWith

/**
 * 模拟器/真机上的 native 基座验证（任务 2.1/2.3/2.4 的设备侧验证）。
 */
@RunWith(AndroidJUnit4::class)
class NativeSmokeTest {

    private fun mappedLibc(): String? {
        // 现代 Android 的 app 可见 libc 在 /apex 下；从 maps 找真实映射路径。
        // maps 行格式：addr perms offset dev inode pathname —— pathname 是最后的 '/' 开头字段
        File("/proc/self/maps").useLines { lines ->
            for (line in lines) {
                val path = line.trim().split(Regex("\\s+")).lastOrNull { it.startsWith("/") } ?: continue
                if (path.endsWith("/libc.so")) return path
            }
        }
        return null
    }

    @Test
    fun nativeLibraryLoads() {
        assertTrue(NativePlatform.nativeVersion().startsWith("detectany-native/"))
    }

    @Test
    fun tripleExecutorSvcLibcConsistentOnMappedLibc() {
        val libc = mappedLibc() ?: "/system/lib64/libc.so"
        val diff = TriplePathExecutor(JniSyscallBackend).probe(libc)
        assertEquals("svc vs libc 不一致: ${diff.svcVsLibc}", null, diff.svcVsLibc)
    }

    @Test
    fun elfSelfParseMatchesDlsym() {
        val libc = mappedLibc() ?: error("maps 中未找到 libc.so")
        val expected = ElfInspector().expectedRuntimeAddress(libc, "fopen")
        val actual = ElfJni.dlsymAddress("fopen")
        assertTrue("expected=$expected (disk st_value+base)", expected != null && expected != 0L)
        assertTrue("dlsym 实测为 0", actual != 0L)
        assertEquals("磁盘自解析与 dlsym 不一致", expected!!, actual)
    }

    @Test
    fun syscallGuardRejectsForeignNr() {
        val arm64 = android.os.Build.SUPPORTED_ABIS.firstOrNull() == "arm64-v8a"
        val allowedNr = if (arm64) 79L else 300L  // newfstatat / fstatat64
        val statxNr = if (arm64) 291L else 397L
        assertTrue(SyscallJni.svcNrAllowed(allowedNr))
        assertTrue("statx 必须被 native 守卫拒绝", !SyscallJni.svcNrAllowed(statxNr))
    }

    @Test
    fun propsListAndGetWork() {
        val bridge = SystemPropertiesBridge()
        val props = bridge.list()
        assertTrue("属性枚举为空", props.isNotEmpty())
        assertTrue("缺少 ro.build.fingerprint", props.containsKey("ro.build.fingerprint"))
        assertNotNull("ro.build.type 读取失败", bridge.get("ro.build.type"))
    }
}
```

`core/native-platform/src/main/cpp/CMakeLists.txt`:

```txt
cmake_minimum_required(VERSION 3.22.1)
project(detectany_native LANGUAGES C CXX)

# 唯一的 native 库：采集器探针基座（裸 svc 三路执行器/seccomp 守卫/ELF 自解析）
add_library(detectany_native SHARED
        native.cpp
        raw_syscalls.cpp
        elf_inspect.cpp
        props.cpp
        probes.cpp
        guard.cpp)

target_link_libraries(detectany_native log)

target_compile_options(detectany_native PRIVATE
        -Wall
        -Wextra
        -fvisibility=hidden
        -ffunction-sections
        -fdata-sections)

# arm32：r7 在 Thumb 构建中是帧指针保留寄存器，裸 svc 需要以 r7 传系统调用号 —— 释放它
if (${ANDROID_ABI} STREQUAL "armeabi-v7a")
    target_compile_options(detectany_native PRIVATE -fomit-frame-pointer)
endif ()

# 任务 1.2 硬性要求（design.md D7）：
# 1) 16KB 页对齐（Android 15+ 设备强制；NDK r25 需显式 linker flags）
# 2) 不动态依赖系统 libcrypto/libssl —— 本库只链 log，加密全部位于 Kotlin 层(BouncyCastle)
# 3) 保留标准 ELF 节头 —— 不传任何 strip/节剔除参数，加固混淆时同样必须保留
target_link_options(detectany_native PRIVATE
        "-Wl,-z,max-page-size=16384"
        "-Wl,-z,common-page-size=16384"
        "-Wl,--gc-sections"
        "-Wl,--hash-style=both")

```

`core/native-platform/src/main/cpp/elf_inspect.cpp`:

```cpp
#include <elf.h>
#include <errno.h>
#include <fcntl.h>
#include <stdio.h>
#include <stdlib.h>
#include <string.h>
#include <unistd.h>

#include <string>
#include <vector>

// 任务 2.3：ELF 自解析 —— 从磁盘读取共享库的 .dynsym/.dynstr，解析符号 → st_value。
// 不经过 dlsym（dlsym 结果可被 hook 篡改），供 C7 的 GOT/PLT 完整性比对：
//   期望地址 = 磁盘 st_value + 进程内该库的加载基址（由 /proc/self/maps 求得）。
// 支持 ELF32/ELF64，仅处理本机字节序。

namespace {

struct SymbolHit {
    unsigned long st_value = 0;
    unsigned char st_info = 0;
    bool found = false;
};

template <typename Ehdr, typename Shdr, typename Sym>
bool parse_dynsym(int fd, const std::string &want, SymbolHit *out) {
    Ehdr ehdr;
    if (pread(fd, &ehdr, sizeof(ehdr), 0) != static_cast<ssize_t>(sizeof(ehdr))) return false;
    if (memcmp(ehdr.e_ident, ELFMAG, SELFMAG) != 0) return false;

    std::vector<Shdr> shdrs(ehdr.e_shnum);
    if (ehdr.e_shnum == 0 ||
        pread(fd, shdrs.data(), sizeof(Shdr) * ehdr.e_shnum, ehdr.e_shoff) !=
            static_cast<ssize_t>(sizeof(Shdr) * ehdr.e_shnum)) {
        return false;
    }

    for (const Shdr &sh : shdrs) {
        if (sh.sh_type != SHT_DYNSYM) continue;
        // 2026-11-10 审计修复：sh_link 为磁盘字段，恶意/损坏 ELF 可任意置值——
        // maps 被 hook 时 C7 解析的是攻击者可控文件，越界下标 = UB/崩溃
        // （用崩溃规避检测的攻击面）。负值/超界一律跳过。
        if (sh.sh_link >= shdrs.size()) continue;
        const Shdr &strsec = shdrs[sh.sh_link];

        std::vector<char> strtab(strsec.sh_size);
        if (pread(fd, strtab.data(), strsec.sh_size, strsec.sh_offset) !=
            static_cast<ssize_t>(strsec.sh_size)) {
            continue;
        }

        const size_t count = sh.sh_size / sizeof(Sym);
        std::vector<Sym> syms(count);
        if (pread(fd, syms.data(), sh.sh_size, sh.sh_offset) != static_cast<ssize_t>(sh.sh_size)) {
            continue;
        }

        for (const Sym &sym : syms) {
            if (sym.st_name >= strsec.sh_size) continue;
            const char *name = strtab.data() + sym.st_name;
            if (name == want) {
                out->st_value = static_cast<unsigned long>(sym.st_value);
                out->st_info = sym.st_info;
                out->found = true;
                return true;
            }
        }
    }
    return true;  // 解析成功但没有命中
}

}  // namespace

// 返回：0 = 未命中/失败；>0 = 磁盘上的 st_value（vaddr）。
unsigned long da_elf_export_value(const char *path, const char *symbol) {
    const int fd = open(path, O_RDONLY | O_CLOEXEC);
    if (fd < 0) return 0;
    unsigned char class_byte = 0;
    if (pread(fd, &class_byte, 1, EI_CLASS) != 1) {
        close(fd);
        return 0;
    }
    SymbolHit hit;
    bool ok = false;
    if (class_byte == ELFCLASS64) {
        ok = parse_dynsym<Elf64_Ehdr, Elf64_Shdr, Elf64_Sym>(fd, symbol, &hit);
    } else if (class_byte == ELFCLASS32) {
        ok = parse_dynsym<Elf32_Ehdr, Elf32_Shdr, Elf32_Sym>(fd, symbol, &hit);
    }
    close(fd);
    return (ok && hit.found) ? hit.st_value : 0;
}

// ---- C7 支持：按 vaddr 读磁盘字节（PT_LOAD 换算）+ 重定位表枚举 ----

struct PhdrRange {
    unsigned long p_vaddr = 0;
    unsigned long p_offset = 0;
    unsigned long p_filesz = 0;
};

template <typename Ehdr, typename Phdr>
bool collect_loads(int fd, std::vector<PhdrRange> *out) {
    Ehdr ehdr;
    if (pread(fd, &ehdr, sizeof(ehdr), 0) != static_cast<ssize_t>(sizeof(ehdr))) return false;
    if (memcmp(ehdr.e_ident, ELFMAG, SELFMAG) != 0) return false;
    if (ehdr.e_phnum == 0 || ehdr.e_phoff == 0) return false;
    std::vector<Phdr> phdrs(ehdr.e_phnum);
    if (pread(fd, phdrs.data(), sizeof(Phdr) * ehdr.e_phnum, ehdr.e_phoff) !=
        static_cast<ssize_t>(sizeof(Phdr) * ehdr.e_phnum)) {
        return false;
    }
    for (const Phdr &p : phdrs) {
        if (p.p_type == PT_LOAD) {
            out->push_back({static_cast<unsigned long>(p.p_vaddr),
                            static_cast<unsigned long>(p.p_offset),
                            static_cast<unsigned long>(p.p_filesz)});
        }
    }
    return !out->empty();
}

// 读磁盘 ELF 中 [vaddr, vaddr+len) 的原始字节（.text prologue 比对用）。
// 返回实际读取字节数；0 = vaddr 不在任何 PT_LOAD。
long da_elf_read_at_vaddr(const char *path, unsigned long vaddr, unsigned char *buf, size_t len) {
    const int fd = open(path, O_RDONLY | O_CLOEXEC);
    if (fd < 0) return 0;
    unsigned char class_byte = 0;
    if (pread(fd, &class_byte, 1, EI_CLASS) != 1) {
        close(fd);
        return 0;
    }
    std::vector<PhdrRange> loads;
    bool ok = false;
    if (class_byte == ELFCLASS64) {
        ok = collect_loads<Elf64_Ehdr, Elf64_Phdr>(fd, &loads);
    } else if (class_byte == ELFCLASS32) {
        ok = collect_loads<Elf32_Ehdr, Elf32_Phdr>(fd, &loads);
    }
    if (!ok) {
        close(fd);
        return 0;
    }
    for (const PhdrRange &r : loads) {
        if (vaddr >= r.p_vaddr && vaddr < r.p_vaddr + r.p_filesz) {
            const size_t avail = static_cast<size_t>(r.p_vaddr + r.p_filesz - vaddr);
            const size_t want = len < avail ? len : avail;
            const ssize_t n = pread(fd, buf, want, static_cast<off_t>(r.p_offset + (vaddr - r.p_vaddr)));
            close(fd);
            return n > 0 ? static_cast<long>(n) : 0;
        }
    }
    close(fd);
    return 0;
}

// 枚举动态重定位：输出 "symbolName\tgot_vaddr"（JUMP_SLOT/GLOB_DAT，C7 GOT 完整性用）。
template <typename Ehdr, typename Shdr, typename Sym, typename Rel>
int relocs_common(int fd, std::vector<std::string> *out) {
    Ehdr ehdr;
    if (pread(fd, &ehdr, sizeof(ehdr), 0) != static_cast<ssize_t>(sizeof(ehdr))) return -1;
    if (memcmp(ehdr.e_ident, ELFMAG, SELFMAG) != 0) return -1;
    std::vector<Shdr> shdrs(ehdr.e_shnum);
    if (ehdr.e_shnum == 0 ||
        pread(fd, shdrs.data(), sizeof(Shdr) * ehdr.e_shnum, ehdr.e_shoff) !=
            static_cast<ssize_t>(sizeof(Shdr) * ehdr.e_shnum)) {
        return -1;
    }
    // 找 .dynsym/.dynstr
    size_t dynsym_idx = 0, dynstr_idx = 0;
    for (size_t i = 0; i < shdrs.size(); i++) {
        if (shdrs[i].sh_type == SHT_DYNSYM) {
            dynsym_idx = i;
            dynstr_idx = shdrs[i].sh_link;
            break;
        }
    }
    if (dynsym_idx == 0) return 0;
    // 同 parse_dynsym：sh_link 磁盘字段不可信（2026-11-10 审计修复）
    if (dynstr_idx >= shdrs.size()) return -1;
    const Shdr &symsec = shdrs[dynsym_idx];
    const Shdr &strsec = shdrs[dynstr_idx];
    std::vector<char> strtab(strsec.sh_size);
    if (pread(fd, strtab.data(), strsec.sh_size, strsec.sh_offset) != static_cast<ssize_t>(strsec.sh_size)) return -1;
    const size_t sym_count = symsec.sh_size / sizeof(Sym);
    std::vector<Sym> syms(sym_count);
    if (pread(fd, syms.data(), symsec.sh_size, symsec.sh_offset) != static_cast<ssize_t>(symsec.sh_size)) return -1;

    for (const Shdr &sh : shdrs) {
        const bool isRela = sh.sh_type == SHT_RELA;
        const bool isRel = sh.sh_type == SHT_REL;
        if (!isRela && !isRel) continue;
        const size_t ent = isRela ? sizeof(Rel) : sizeof(Rel);
        const size_t count = sh.sh_size / ent;
        std::vector<unsigned char> raw(sh.sh_size);
        if (pread(fd, raw.data(), sh.sh_size, sh.sh_offset) != static_cast<ssize_t>(sh.sh_size)) continue;
        for (size_t i = 0; i < count; i++) {
            const Rel *rel = reinterpret_cast<const Rel *>(raw.data() + i * ent);
            const unsigned long r_offset = static_cast<unsigned long>(rel->r_offset);
            const unsigned long sym_idx = isRela
                ? static_cast<unsigned long>(reinterpret_cast<const Elf64_Rela *>(rel)->r_info) >> 32
                : static_cast<unsigned long>(reinterpret_cast<const Elf32_Rel *>(rel)->r_info) >> 8;
            const unsigned long type = isRela
                ? (static_cast<unsigned long>(reinterpret_cast<const Elf64_Rela *>(rel)->r_info) & 0xFFFFFFFFL)
                : (static_cast<unsigned long>(reinterpret_cast<const Elf32_Rel *>(rel)->r_info) & 0xFFL);
            // arm64: JUMP_SLOT=1026, GLOB_DAT=1025；arm32: JUMP_SLOT=22, GLOB_DAT=21
#if defined(__aarch64__)
            const bool wanted = (type == 1026 || type == 1025);
#else
            const bool wanted = (type == 22 || type == 21);
#endif
            if (!wanted || sym_idx == 0 || sym_idx >= sym_count) continue;
            const Sym &sym = syms[sym_idx];
            if (sym.st_name >= strsec.sh_size) continue;
            char line[640];
            snprintf(line, sizeof(line), "%s\t%lu", strtab.data() + sym.st_name, r_offset);
            out->emplace_back(line);
        }
    }
    return 0;
}

int da_elf_relocations(const char *path, std::vector<std::string> *out) {
    out->clear();
    const int fd = open(path, O_RDONLY | O_CLOEXEC);
    if (fd < 0) return -1;
    unsigned char class_byte = 0;
    if (pread(fd, &class_byte, 1, EI_CLASS) != 1) {
        close(fd);
        return -1;
    }
    int rc = -1;
    if (class_byte == ELFCLASS64) {
        rc = relocs_common<Elf64_Ehdr, Elf64_Shdr, Elf64_Sym, Elf64_Rela>(fd, out);
    } else if (class_byte == ELFCLASS32) {
        rc = relocs_common<Elf32_Ehdr, Elf32_Shdr, Elf32_Sym, Elf32_Rel>(fd, out);
    }
    close(fd);
    return rc;
}

// 从 /proc/self/maps 求指定库文件在本进程的加载基址（首个可执行映射的起始地址）。
// 供「期望地址 = st_value + 基址」与 dlsym 实测交叉（任务 2.3 验证）。
unsigned long da_maps_base(const char *path) {
    FILE *f = fopen("/proc/self/maps", "re");
    if (f == nullptr) return 0;
    char line[1024];
    unsigned long best = 0;
    const size_t path_len = strlen(path);
    while (fgets(line, sizeof(line), f) != nullptr) {
        const size_t len = strlen(line);
        if (len < path_len + 1) continue;
        const char *candidate = line + (len - path_len - 1);
        if (candidate[0] != '/' || memcmp(candidate, path, path_len) != 0 || candidate[path_len] != '\n') {
            // 允许行尾就是路径（换行已被 fgets 保留）——上面已按 '\n' 对齐校验
            continue;
        }
        unsigned long start = strtoul(line, nullptr, 16);
        if (best == 0 || start < best) best = start;
    }
    fclose(f);
    return best;
}
```

`core/native-platform/src/main/cpp/guard.cpp`:

```cpp
#include <errno.h>
#include <jni.h>
#include <stdint.h>
#include <stdio.h>
#include <string.h>
#include <sys/wait.h>
#include <unistd.h>

#include <memory>

#include "svc_arch.h"

// 任务 6.1/6.2：自保护 native 层。
// 预期值「加密分散存储」：签名摘要与 DEX CRC 均拆成两段常量、运行时异或还原，
// 且常数时间比较——smali 层看不到明文预期值。
// 占位常量在发布装配时由构建脚本替换（与发布签名/DEX 一一对应）。

long da_raw_ptrace(long request, long pid, long addr, long data);

namespace {

// 预期 APK 签名证书 sha256（占位；发布时替换）：
constexpr unsigned char SIG_A[32] = {
    0x5a, 0x11, 0xc3, 0x7f, 0x9b, 0x02, 0x4d, 0xe8,
    0x33, 0xa0, 0x66, 0x1d, 0xcf, 0x54, 0x90, 0x2e,
    0x77, 0x0b, 0x21, 0xf6, 0x18, 0x8d, 0xa4, 0x35,
    0xc9, 0x60, 0x03, 0xbe, 0xd2, 0x4f, 0x17, 0x8a,
};
constexpr unsigned char SIG_B[32] = {
    0x35, 0x62, 0x18, 0x31, 0x0d, 0x99, 0xe1, 0x4c,
    0x7a, 0x2f, 0xd1, 0x93, 0x60, 0x11, 0xf5, 0x68,
    0x88, 0x3d, 0x52, 0x0b, 0xe6, 0x29, 0x5b, 0xca,
    0x16, 0x8f, 0x74, 0x25, 0x43, 0x99, 0x06, 0xd5,
};

// 预期 classes.dex CRC32（占位；发布时替换），两段异或：
constexpr uint32_t CRC_A = 0x3fa2c910u;
constexpr uint32_t CRC_B = 0x6c17d5e8u;

void assemble_sig(unsigned char out[32]) {
    for (int i = 0; i < 32; i++) out[i] = SIG_A[i] ^ SIG_B[i];
}

}  // namespace

// ---- GuardJni（com.detectany.selfguard）----

extern "C" JNIEXPORT jbyteArray JNICALL
Java_com_detectany_selfguard_GuardJni_assembleExpectedSignature(JNIEnv *env, jobject) {
    unsigned char buf[32];
    assemble_sig(buf);
    const jbyteArray arr = env->NewByteArray(32);
    env->SetByteArrayRegion(arr, 0, 32, reinterpret_cast<const jbyte *>(buf));
    return arr;
}

extern "C" JNIEXPORT jlong JNICALL
Java_com_detectany_selfguard_GuardJni_assembleExpectedDexCrc(JNIEnv *, jobject) {
    return static_cast<jlong>(static_cast<uint32_t>(CRC_A ^ CRC_B));
}

extern "C" JNIEXPORT jboolean JNICALL
Java_com_detectany_selfguard_GuardJni_constantTimeEquals(JNIEnv *env, jobject,
                                                          jbyteArray ja, jbyteArray jb) {
    const jsize na = env->GetArrayLength(ja);
    const jsize nb = env->GetArrayLength(jb);
    if (na <= 0 || nb <= 0 || na != nb) return JNI_FALSE;
    std::unique_ptr<unsigned char[]> a(new unsigned char[na]);
    std::unique_ptr<unsigned char[]> b(new unsigned char[na]);
    env->GetByteArrayRegion(ja, 0, na, reinterpret_cast<jbyte *>(a.get()));
    env->GetByteArrayRegion(jb, 0, na, reinterpret_cast<jbyte *>(b.get()));
    unsigned char diff = 0;
    for (jsize i = 0; i < na; i++) diff |= static_cast<unsigned char>(a[i] ^ b[i]);
    return diff == 0 ? JNI_TRUE : JNI_FALSE;
}

// ---- 调试器探针（任务 6.2）：fork 子进程 PTRACE_ATTACH 父进程 ----
// 父进程已被调试器附加时，子进程的 ATTACH 返回 -EPERM。
// 返回：0 = 无调试器；1 = 有调试器；-1 = 探针不可执行。
extern "C" JNIEXPORT jint JNICALL
Java_com_detectany_selfguard_GuardJni_debuggerProbe(JNIEnv *env, jobject) {
    int pipefd[2];
    if (pipe(pipefd) != 0) return -1;
    const pid_t parent = getppid();
    const pid_t child = fork();
    if (child < 0) {
        close(pipefd[0]);
        close(pipefd[1]);
        return -1;
    }
    if (child == 0) {
        close(pipefd[0]);
        errno = 0;
        const long r = da_raw_ptrace(16 /*PTRACE_ATTACH*/, parent, 0, 0);
        int flag;
        if (r == 0) {
            int status = 0;
            waitpid(parent, &status, 0);
            da_raw_ptrace(17 /*PTRACE_DETACH*/, parent, 0, 0);
            flag = 0;
        } else if (errno == EPERM) {
            flag = 1;  // 已被调试器占用
        } else {
            flag = -1;
        }
        const uint32_t v = static_cast<uint32_t>(flag);
        if (write(pipefd[1], &v, sizeof(v)) != static_cast<ssize_t>(sizeof(v))) _exit(3);
        _exit(0);
    }
    close(pipefd[1]);
    uint32_t v = 0xFFFFFFFFu;
    const ssize_t n = read(pipefd[0], &v, sizeof(v));
    close(pipefd[0]);
    int status = 0;
    waitpid(child, &status, 0);
    if (n != static_cast<ssize_t>(sizeof(v))) return -1;
    return static_cast<jint>(v);
}
```

```cpp
#include <dlfcn.h>
#include <fcntl.h>
#include <jni.h>
#include <sys/stat.h>
#include <sys/system_properties.h>
#include <unistd.h>

#include <string>
#include <vector>

// ---- 内部桥接声明（实现见 raw_syscalls.cpp / elf_inspect.cpp / props.cpp） ----
long da_svc_fstatat(int dirfd, const char *path, struct stat *st, int flags);
long da_libc_fstatat(int dirfd, const char *path, struct stat *st, int flags);
long da_svc_faccessat(int dirfd, const char *path, int mode);

int da_prop_get(const char *key, char *buf, size_t buf_size, int *truncated);

int da_supercall_timing(long *out_truncate_ns, long *out_ftruncate_ns, long *out_getpid_ns, int iterations);
int da_fd_names(std::vector<std::string> *out);

#define DA_PKG(method) Java_com_detectany_core_nativeplatform_##method

extern "C" JNIEXPORT jint JNI_OnLoad(JavaVM * /*vm*/, void * /*reserved*/) {
    return JNI_VERSION_1_6;
}

// ---------- NativePlatform ----------

extern "C" JNIEXPORT jstring JNICALL DA_PKG(NativePlatform_nativeVersion)(JNIEnv *env, jobject) {
    return env->NewStringUTF("detectany-native/0.2.0");
}

// ---------- SyscallJni：三路执行器（stat/access 的 libc 与 svc 两路） ----------
// 约定：statXxx 返回 long[9] = [tag, errno, dev, ino, mode, uid, gid, size, mtimeSec]
//       tag: 0=成功, 1=errno；accessXxx 返回 long[2] = [tag, errno]

static jlongArray pack_stat(JNIEnv *env, long ret, int errnoValue, const struct stat &st) {
    jlong out[9] = {0, 0, 0, 0, 0, 0, 0, 0, 0};
    if (ret == 0) {
        out[0] = 0;
        out[2] = static_cast<jlong>(st.st_dev);
        out[3] = static_cast<jlong>(st.st_ino);
        out[4] = static_cast<jlong>(st.st_mode);
        out[5] = static_cast<jlong>(st.st_uid);
        out[6] = static_cast<jlong>(st.st_gid);
        out[7] = static_cast<jlong>(st.st_size);
        out[8] = static_cast<jlong>(st.st_mtime);
    } else {
        out[0] = 1;
        out[1] = errnoValue;
    }
    jlongArray arr = env->NewLongArray(9);
    env->SetLongArrayRegion(arr, 0, 9, out);
    return arr;
}

static jlongArray stat_common(JNIEnv *env, jstring jpath, long (*fn)(int, const char *, struct stat *, int), bool rawSvc) {
    const char *path = env->GetStringUTFChars(jpath, nullptr);
    struct stat st = {};
    errno = 0;
    const long ret = fn(AT_FDCWD, path, &st, 0);
    int e = errno;
    if (rawSvc && ret != 0 && ret >= -4095L) {
        e = static_cast<int>(-ret);  // 裸 svc 不写 errno —— 从负返回值反推
    }
    env->ReleaseStringUTFChars(jpath, path);
    return pack_stat(env, ret, e, st);
}

extern "C" JNIEXPORT jlongArray JNICALL DA_PKG(SyscallJni_statSvc)(JNIEnv *env, jobject, jstring jpath) {
    return stat_common(env, jpath, da_svc_fstatat, true);
}

extern "C" JNIEXPORT jlongArray JNICALL DA_PKG(SyscallJni_statLibc)(JNIEnv *env, jobject, jstring jpath) {
    return stat_common(env, jpath, da_libc_fstatat, false);
}

static jlongArray access_common(JNIEnv *env, jstring jpath, jint mode, long (*fn)(int, const char *, int), bool rawSvc) {
    const char *path = env->GetStringUTFChars(jpath, nullptr);
    errno = 0;
    const long ret = fn(AT_FDCWD, path, static_cast<int>(mode));
    int e = errno;
    if (rawSvc && ret != 0 && ret >= -4095L) {
        e = static_cast<int>(-ret);
    }
    env->ReleaseStringUTFChars(jpath, path);
    jlong out[2] = {(ret == 0) ? 0LL : 1LL, e};
    jlongArray arr = env->NewLongArray(2);
    env->SetLongArrayRegion(arr, 0, 2, out);
    return arr;
}

extern "C" JNIEXPORT jlongArray JNICALL DA_PKG(SyscallJni_accessSvc)(JNIEnv *env, jobject, jstring jpath, jint mode) {
    return access_common(env, jpath, mode, da_svc_faccessat, true);
}

extern "C" JNIEXPORT jlongArray JNICALL DA_PKG(SyscallJni_accessLibc)(JNIEnv *env, jobject, jstring jpath, jint mode) {
    return access_common(env, jpath, mode, da_libc_faccessat, false);
}

extern "C" JNIEXPORT jboolean JNICALL DA_PKG(SyscallJni_svcNrAllowed)(JNIEnv *, jobject, jlong nr) {
    return da_svc_nr_allowed(nr) ? JNI_TRUE : JNI_FALSE;
}

// ---------- ElfJni：磁盘自解析（不经 dlsym） ----------

extern "C" JNIEXPORT jlong JNICALL DA_PKG(ElfJni_exportValue)(JNIEnv *env, jobject, jstring jpath, jstring jsymbol) {
    const char *path = env->GetStringUTFChars(jpath, nullptr);
    const char *symbol = env->GetStringUTFChars(jsymbol, nullptr);
    const unsigned long value = da_elf_export_value(path, symbol);
    env->ReleaseStringUTFChars(jsymbol, symbol);
    env->ReleaseStringUTFChars(jpath, path);
    return static_cast<jlong>(value);
}

extern "C" JNIEXPORT jlong JNICALL DA_PKG(ElfJni_mappedBase)(JNIEnv *env, jobject, jstring jpath) {
    const char *path = env->GetStringUTFChars(jpath, nullptr);
    const unsigned long base = da_maps_base(path);
    env->ReleaseStringUTFChars(jpath, path);
    return static_cast<jlong>(base);
}

// 仅用于验证（任务 2.3）：dlsym 实测地址，与磁盘自解析的期望地址交叉比对。
// 生产判定路径不使用 dlsym。
extern "C" JNIEXPORT jlong JNICALL DA_PKG(ElfJni_dlsymAddress)(JNIEnv *env, jobject, jstring jsymbol) {
    const char *symbol = env->GetStringUTFChars(jsymbol, nullptr);
    void *addr = dlsym(RTLD_DEFAULT, symbol);
    env->ReleaseStringUTFChars(jsymbol, symbol);
    return reinterpret_cast<jlong>(addr);
}

// ---------- ProbeJni：C2/C6/C8 行为与结构探针 ----------

extern "C" JNIEXPORT jlongArray JNICALL DA_PKG(SyscallJni_statfsSvc)(JNIEnv *env, jobject, jstring jpath) {
    const char *path = env->GetStringUTFChars(jpath, nullptr);
    unsigned long f_type = 0, bsize = 0, blocks = 0;
    errno = 0;
    const long r = da_svc_statfs(path, &f_type, &bsize, &blocks);
    const int e = errno;
    env->ReleaseStringUTFChars(jpath, path);
    jlong out[5] = {(r == 0) ? 0LL : 1LL, e, static_cast<jlong>(f_type), static_cast<jlong>(bsize), static_cast<jlong>(blocks)};
    jlongArray arr = env->NewLongArray(5);
    env->SetLongArrayRegion(arr, 0, 5, out);
    return arr;
}

static jobjectArray to_string_array(JNIEnv *env, const std::vector<std::string> &v) {
    const jclass stringCls = env->FindClass("java/lang/String");
    const jobjectArray arr = env->NewObjectArray(static_cast<jsize>(v.size()), stringCls, nullptr);
    for (size_t i = 0; i < v.size(); i++) {
        env->SetObjectArrayElement(arr, static_cast<jsize>(i), env->NewStringUTF(v[i].c_str()));
    }
    return arr;
}

extern "C" JNIEXPORT jobjectArray JNICALL DA_PKG(ProbeJni_fdNames)(JNIEnv *env, jobject) {
    std::vector<std::string> names;
    da_fd_names(&names);
    return to_string_array(env, names);
}

// 返回 String[]：元素 0 = "ret=<r>;errno=<e>"，后续 = 新增 fd 名
extern "C" JNIEXPORT jobjectArray JNICALL DA_PKG(ProbeJni_ksuProbe)(JNIEnv *env, jobject,
                                                                    jlong m1, jlong m2, jlong m3, jlong arg4) {
    long ret = 0;
    int err = 0;
    std::vector<std::string> new_fds;
    // 2026-11-10 审计修复：原实现丢弃 -2（seccomp SIGSYS 不可判定）状态——
    // da_ksu_probe 清零 ret/err 返回 -2，JNI 输出 "ret=0;errno=0" 假装完成，
    // 掩盖了「探针在 API 28+ 从未真正执行」的事实（模拟器/P8 实测均此态）。
    const int pr = da_ksu_probe(m1, m2, m3, arg4, &ret, &err, &new_fds);
    std::vector<std::string> out;
    char head[96];
    if (pr == -2) {
        snprintf(head, sizeof(head), "undetermined=seccomp;ret=%ld;errno=%d", ret, err);
    } else {
        snprintf(head, sizeof(head), "ret=%ld;errno=%d", ret, err);
    }
    out.emplace_back(head);
    for (const auto &f : new_fds) out.push_back(f);
    return to_string_array(env, out);
}

extern "C" JNIEXPORT jlongArray JNICALL DA_PKG(ProbeJni_apatchProbe)(JNIEnv *env, jobject,
                                                                     jstring jkey, jlong cmd) {
    const char *key = env->GetStringUTFChars(jkey, nullptr);
    long ret = 0, elapsed = 0;
    int err = 0;
    da_apatch_probe(key, cmd, &ret, &err, &elapsed);
    env->ReleaseStringUTFChars(jkey, key);
    jlong out[3] = {ret, err, elapsed};
    jlongArray arr = env->NewLongArray(3);
    env->SetLongArrayRegion(arr, 0, 3, out);
    return arr;
}

    const char *label = env->GetStringUTFChars(jlabel, nullptr);

}

// ---------- PropJni：属性访问（API 24 兼容） ----------

extern "C" JNIEXPORT jobjectArray JNICALL DA_PKG(PropJni_propList)(JNIEnv *env, jobject) {
    std::vector<std::string> props;
    da_prop_list(&props);
    const jclass stringCls = env->FindClass("java/lang/String");
    const jobjectArray arr = env->NewObjectArray(static_cast<jsize>(props.size()), stringCls, nullptr);
    for (size_t i = 0; i < props.size(); i++) {
        env->SetObjectArrayElement(arr, static_cast<jsize>(i), env->NewStringUTF(props[i].c_str()));
    }

extern "C" JNIEXPORT jstring JNICALL DA_PKG(PropJni_propGet)(JNIEnv *env, jobject, jstring jkey) {
    const char *key = env->GetStringUTFChars(jkey, nullptr);
    char value[PROP_VALUE_MAX];
    int truncated = 0;
    const int rc = da_prop_get(key, value, sizeof(value), &truncated);
    env->ReleaseStringUTFChars(jkey, key);
    if (rc != 0) return nullptr;
    // 截断约定：Kotlin 侧以 value.length == PROP_VALUE_MAX-1(91) 判「疑似截断」
    return env->NewStringUTF(value);
}

```

`core/native-platform/src/main/cpp/probes.cpp`:

```cpp
#include <dirent.h>
#include <signal.h>
#include <dlfcn.h>
#include <errno.h>
#include <fcntl.h>
#include <link.h>
#include <string.h>
#include <sys/mman.h>
#include <sys/stat.h>
#include <sys/types.h>
#include <sys/uio.h>
#include <sys/wait.h>
#include <time.h>
#include <unistd.h>

#include <algorithm>
#include <cstdint>
#include <string>
#include <vector>

// 任务 3.6/3.8 的行为与结构探针。全部只读或无副作用；SELinux label 探针写入的
// 是必然不存在的 label（EINVAL/EPERM 语义判定），绝不会成功改变自身上下文。

long da_ksu_reboot_supercall(long magic1, long magic2, long magic3, long arg4);
long da_apatch_supercall(const char *key, long ver_and_cmd, long arg3, long arg4);
long da_raw_ptrace(long request, long pid, long addr, long data);

namespace {

std::vector<std::string> scan_fds() {
    std::vector<std::string> names;
    DIR *d = opendir("/proc/self/fd");
    if (d == nullptr) return names;
    struct dirent *de;
    char link[512];
    while ((de = readdir(d)) != nullptr) {
        if (de->d_name[0] == '.') continue;
        snprintf(link, sizeof(link), "/proc/self/fd/%s", de->d_name);
        char buf[512];
        const ssize_t n = readlink(link, buf, sizeof(buf) - 1);
        if (n > 0) {
            buf[n] = '\0';
            names.emplace_back(buf);
        }
    }
    closedir(d);
    return names;
}

long now_ns() {
    struct timespec ts {};
    clock_gettime(CLOCK_MONOTONIC, &ts);
    return static_cast<long>(ts.tv_sec) * 1000000000L + ts.tv_nsec;
}

}  // namespace

// ---- fd 表扫描（KSU anon_inode 检出 + C6 fd readlink 复用） ----
int da_fd_names(std::vector<std::string> *out) {
    *out = scan_fds();
    return 0;
}

// ---- KSU supercall 探针：reboot 双魔数 → fd 表 diff（fork 隔离） ----
// 实测（API 35 模拟器，2026-11）：reboot(142) 不在 untrusted_app 的 seccomp 白名单，
// app 进程直接裸调会 SIGSYS 杀进程 —— 必须 fork：子进程探测并经 pipe 回传，父进程收割。
// 子进程被 SIGSYS 杀死 = seccomp 阻断 → 探针不可判定（返回 -2），不是证据。
// Android 7 无 seccomp，probe 直达内核（原厂 -EPERM，KSU 安装 anon_inode fd）。
// 返回：0 = 子进程完成探测；-1 = fork/pipe 失败；-2 = 子进程被 seccomp SIGSYS。
int da_ksu_probe(long magic1, long magic2, long magic3, long arg4,
                 long *ret, int *err, std::vector<std::string> *new_fds) {
    int pipefd[2];
    if (pipe(pipefd) != 0) return -1;
    const pid_t child = fork();
    if (child < 0) {
        close(pipefd[0]);
        close(pipefd[1]);
        return -1;
    }
    if (child == 0) {
        close(pipefd[0]);
        // Android seccomp 对白名单外 syscall 投递 SIGSYS（code=SYS_SECCOMP，TRAP 可捕获）：
        // 预装 handler 让子进程以退出码 86 干净离场——不触发 debuggerd 墓碑、
        // 不在 logcat 刷 Fatal signal、不污染崩溃统计（2026-11-06 真机实测教训）
        struct sigaction sa {};
        sa.sa_handler = [](int) { _exit(86); };
        sigemptyset(&sa.sa_mask);
        sa.sa_flags = SA_NODEFER;
        sigaction(31 /*SIGSYS*/, &sa, nullptr);
        const auto before = scan_fds();
        errno = 0;
        const long r = da_ksu_reboot_supercall(magic1, magic2, magic3, arg4);
        const int e = errno;
        const auto after = scan_fds();
        char head[96];
        snprintf(head, sizeof(head), "ret=%ld;errno=%d\n", r, e);
        if (write(pipefd[1], head, strlen(head)) < 0) _exit(3);
        for (const auto &name : after) {
            if (std::find(before.begin(), before.end(), name) == before.end()) {
                char line[513];
                snprintf(line, sizeof(line), "%s\n", name.c_str());
                if (write(pipefd[1], line, strlen(line)) < 0) _exit(3);
            }
        }
        close(pipefd[1]);
        _exit(0);
    }
    close(pipefd[1]);
    // 父进程：读 pipe + 收割
    std::string report;
    char buf[512];
    ssize_t n;
    while ((n = read(pipefd[0], buf, sizeof(buf) - 1)) > 0) {
        buf[n] = '\0';
        report += buf;
    }
    close(pipefd[0]);
    int status = 0;
    waitpid(child, &status, 0);
    if ((WIFEXITED(status) && WEXITSTATUS(status) == 86) ||
        (WIFSIGNALED(status) && WTERMSIG(status) == 31 /*SIGSYS*/)) {
        *ret = 0;
        *err = 0;
        new_fds->clear();
        return -2;  // seccomp 阻断（handler 干净退出 86 / 兜底：带信号死亡）：不可判定
    }
    // 解析子进程报告
    size_t pos = 0;
    bool first = true;
    while (pos < report.size()) {
        size_t nl = report.find('\n', pos);
        if (nl == std::string::npos) nl = report.size();
        std::string line = report.substr(pos, nl - pos);
        pos = nl + 1;
        if (line.empty()) continue;
        if (first) {
            *ret = strtol(line.c_str() + 4, nullptr, 10);
            const char *ep = strstr(line.c_str(), "errno=");
            if (ep != nullptr) *err = atoi(ep + 6);
            first = false;
        } else {
            new_fds->push_back(line);
        }
    }
    return 0;
}

// ---- APatch supercall 探针：syscall(45, key, ver_and_cmd) + 计时 ----
int da_apatch_probe(const char *key, long ver_and_cmd, long *ret, int *err, long *elapsed_ns) {
    errno = 0;
    const long t0 = now_ns();
    *ret = da_apatch_supercall(key, ver_and_cmd, 0, 0);
    *elapsed_ns = now_ns() - t0;
    *err = errno;
    return 0;
}

// ---- SELinux errno 语义探针（PayPal 2026 向量）----
// 向 /proc/thread-self/attr/current 写入探测 label：
//   EINVAL = label 不存在（类型未定义）；EPERM = label 存在但禁止写入。
// errno 差即证明对应 SELinux 域（如 u:r:magisk:s0）是否存在于策略中。
// 返回：>=0 写入 syscall 返回值；-1 = open 失败（errno 透传给调用方）。
long da_selinux_label_probe(const char *label, int *out_errno) {
    const char *kPaths[] = {"/proc/thread-self/attr/current", nullptr};
    char fallback[128];
    const long tid = static_cast<long>(gettid());
    snprintf(fallback, sizeof(fallback), "/proc/self/task/%ld/attr/current", tid);
    int fd = -1;
    for (const char *p : kPaths) {
        fd = open(p, O_WRONLY | O_CLOEXEC);
        if (fd >= 0) break;
    }
    if (fd < 0) {
        fd = open(fallback, O_WRONLY | O_CLOEXEC);
    }
    if (fd < 0) {
        *out_errno = errno;
        return -1;
    }
    errno = 0;
    const long r = write(fd, label, strlen(label));
    *out_errno = errno;
    close(fd);
    return r;
}

// ---- NativeBridgeError() 粘滞标志（ZN/ReZygisk 向量）----
// dlopen libnativebridge → dlsym NativeBridgeError → 调用。
// 返回：-1 = 库/符号不可用（不可判定）；0 = 无错误标志；1 = had_error 为真（曾尝试加载 NativeBridge）。
int da_nativebridge_error() {
    const char *kCandidates[] = {
        "/apex/com.android.art/lib64/libnativebridge.so",
        "/apex/com.android.art/lib/libnativebridge.so",
        "libnativebridge.so",
    };
    void *handle = nullptr;
    for (const char *path : kCandidates) {
        handle = dlopen(path, RTLD_NOW | RTLD_LOCAL);
        if (handle != nullptr) break;
    }
    if (handle == nullptr) return -1;
    using Fn = bool (*)();
    const auto fn = reinterpret_cast<Fn>(dlsym(handle, "NativeBridgeError"));
    if (fn == nullptr) {
        dlclose(handle);
        return -1;
    }
    const bool hadError = fn();
    dlclose(handle);
    return hadError ? 1 : 0;
}

// ---- 父进程 ptrace 注入残留（DetectZygisk 向量）----
// fork 子进程 → PTRACE_ATTACH 父进程 → PTRACE_GETEVENTMSG 读取遗留事件消息。
// 若本进程曾被 zygote 注入（ZN/ReZygisk/NeoZygisk），msg == zygote pid。
// 返回：>=0 = 读到的 eventmsg；-1 = 探针不可执行。
long da_ptrace_parent_eventmsg() {
    int pipefd[2];
    if (pipe(pipefd) != 0) return -1;
    const pid_t parent = getppid();
    const pid_t child = fork();
    if (child < 0) {
        close(pipefd[0]);
        close(pipefd[1]);
        return -1;
    }
    if (child == 0) {
        // 子进程
        close(pipefd[0]);
        long msg = -1;
        errno = 0;
        if (da_raw_ptrace(16 /*PTRACE_ATTACH*/, parent, 0, 0) == 0) {
            int status = 0;
            waitpid(parent, &status, 0);
            errno = 0;
            if (da_raw_ptrace(0x4201 /*PTRACE_GETEVENTMSG*/, parent, 0,
                              reinterpret_cast<long>(&msg)) != 0) {
                msg = -1;
            }
            da_raw_ptrace(17 /*PTRACE_DETACH*/, parent, 0, 0);
        }
        const auto m = static_cast<uint64_t>(msg);
        if (write(pipefd[1], &m, sizeof(m)) != static_cast<ssize_t>(sizeof(m))) {
            _exit(2);
        }
        _exit(0);
    }
    // 父进程
    close(pipefd[1]);
    uint64_t msg = 0xFFFFFFFFFFFFFFFFULL;
    const ssize_t n = read(pipefd[0], &msg, sizeof(msg));
    close(pipefd[0]);
    int status = 0;
    waitpid(child, &status, 0);
    return (n == static_cast<ssize_t>(sizeof(msg))) ? static_cast<long>(msg) : -1;
}

// ---- 自身内存安全读取（C7 GOT/prologue 用；process_vm_readv 读自身不崩溃） ----
long da_mem_read(unsigned long addr, void *buf, size_t len) {
    struct iovec local {buf, len};
    struct iovec remote {reinterpret_cast<void *>(addr), len};
    return process_vm_readv(getpid(), &local, 1, &remote, 1, 0);
}

// execute-only 文本段读取（Pixel 8/Android 16 实测：r-x/--xp 文本上 process_vm_readv 返回全零，
// 头部 r--p 单独映射故魔数可读）。做法：查 /proc/self/maps 取该区间原始权限 ->
// 临时 mprotect 加 PROT_READ -> memcpy -> 恢复原始权限。页大小运行时获取（16KB 设备安全）。
static int parse_perm_char(char c) {
    switch (c) {
        case 'r': return PROT_READ;
        case 'w': return PROT_WRITE;
        case 'x': return PROT_EXEC;
        default: return 0;
    }
}

long da_text_read(unsigned long addr, void *buf, size_t len) {
    const long page = sysconf(_SC_PAGESIZE);
    if (page <= 0) return -1;
    const unsigned long astart = addr & ~static_cast<unsigned long>(page - 1);
    const unsigned long aend = (addr + len + page - 1) & ~static_cast<unsigned long>(page - 1);

    // 找覆盖该区间的 maps 行，恢复每行原始权限
    FILE *f = fopen("/proc/self/maps", "re");
    if (f == nullptr) return -1;
    unsigned long bestStart = 0, bestEnd = 0;
    int bestProt = -1;
    char line[1024];
    while (fgets(line, sizeof(line), f) != nullptr) {
        unsigned long s = strtoul(line, nullptr, 16);
        unsigned long e = strtoul(strchr(line, '-') + 1, nullptr, 16);
        if (s <= astart && aend <= e) {
            const char *perms = strchr(line, ' ') + 1;
            bestProt = parse_perm_char(perms[0]) | parse_perm_char(perms[1]) | parse_perm_char(perms[2]);
            bestStart = s;
            bestEnd = e;
            break;
        }
    }
    fclose(f);
    if (bestProt < 0) return -1;  // 未找到覆盖区间（越界/未映射）

    if ((bestProt & PROT_READ) == 0) {
        if (mprotect(reinterpret_cast<void *>(bestStart), bestEnd - bestStart, bestProt | PROT_READ) != 0) {
            return -1;
        }
    }
    memcpy(buf, reinterpret_cast<void *>(addr), len);
    if ((bestProt & PROT_READ) == 0) {
        mprotect(reinterpret_cast<void *>(bestStart), bestEnd - bestStart, bestProt);
    }
    return static_cast<long>(len);
}

// ---- linker 权威加载列表（dl_iterate_phdr，对抗 maps 抹除）----
// 返回 "name\tload_bias" 列表；与 maps 交叉：链表有而 maps 无 = 隐藏映射（B 级证据）。
struct SoListSink {
    std::vector<std::string> *out;
};

int da_solist_cb(struct dl_phdr_info *info, size_t /*size*/, void *data) {
    auto *out = static_cast<std::vector<std::string> *>(data);
    char line[640];
    const char *name = (info->dlpi_name != nullptr && info->dlpi_name[0] != '\0') ? info->dlpi_name : "[anon-linker]";
    snprintf(line, sizeof(line), "%s\t%lu", name, static_cast<unsigned long>(info->dlpi_addr));
    out->emplace_back(line);
    return 0;
}

int da_solist(std::vector<std::string> *out) {
    out->clear();
    dl_iterate_phdr(da_solist_cb, out);
    return 0;
}

// ---- RWX 自探针（对抗 maps 读取被 hook 抹除）----
// mmap 匿名页 → mprotect 加可执行 → 检查 maps 是否能看到该地址区间。
// 返回：1 = 可见（maps 读取可信）；0 = 不可见（maps 被篡改，强证据）；-1 = 分配失败。
// 2026-11-10 审计修复：原硬编码 4096——真 16KB 页设备上 mprotect 长度非页倍数
// 返回 EINVAL → 探针静默失效（C6 的 A 级信号在 16KB 设备上变盲）。改为运行时页大小。
int da_rwx_self_probe() {
    const long pg = sysconf(_SC_PAGESIZE);
    if (pg <= 0) return -1;
    const size_t psz = static_cast<size_t>(pg);
    void *page = mmap(nullptr, psz, PROT_READ | PROT_WRITE, MAP_PRIVATE | MAP_ANONYMOUS, -1, 0);
    if (page == MAP_FAILED) return -1;
    if (mprotect(page, psz, PROT_READ | PROT_EXEC) != 0) {
        munmap(page, psz);
        return -1;
    }
    const unsigned long start = reinterpret_cast<unsigned long>(page);
    int visible = 0;
    FILE *f = fopen("/proc/self/maps", "re");
    if (f != nullptr) {
        char line[1024];
        while (fgets(line, sizeof(line), f) != nullptr) {
            unsigned long s = strtoul(line, nullptr, 16);
            unsigned long e = strtoul(strchr(line, '-') + 1, nullptr, 16);
            if (start >= s && start < e) {
                visible = 1;
                break;
            }
        }
        fclose(f);
    }
    munmap(page, psz);
    return visible;
}

```

`core/native-platform/src/main/cpp/props.cpp`:

```cpp
#include <dlfcn.h>
#include <errno.h>
#include <string.h>
#include <sys/system_properties.h>

#include <string>
#include <vector>

// 任务 2.4：属性访问层（双路径）。
// API 26+：__system_property_read_callback（无 92 字节截断），经 dlsym 运行时解析；
// API 24/25：__system_property_read 旧签名（PROP_VALUE_MAX=92 截断，上层感知）。
//
// 实测教训（API 35 模拟器，2026-11）：
// 1. 旧 __system_property_read 遇到超长属性名（>=PROP_NAME_MAX）会 SIGSEGV —— 枚举也必须双路径；
// 2. bionic 回调原型是 callback(void* cookie, const char* name, const char* value, uint32_t serial)
//    —— cookie 在第一位（与直觉相反），参数序错位会把 serial 当指针用。

namespace {

constexpr size_t kValueBuf = 256;

// bionic 原型（sys/system_properties.h, API 26+）：
//   void __system_property_read_callback(const prop_info* pi,
//       void (*callback)(void* cookie, const char* name, const char* value, uint32_t serial),
//       void* cookie);
typedef void (*prop_cb_t)(void *cookie, const char *name, const char *value, uint32_t serial);
typedef void (*read_cb_fn)(const prop_info *pi, prop_cb_t callback, void *cookie);

read_cb_fn resolve_read_callback() {
    static read_cb_fn cached = reinterpret_cast<read_cb_fn>(-1);
    if (cached == reinterpret_cast<read_cb_fn>(-1)) {
        // dlsym 在属性读取路径上的使用是安全的：这里不做完整性验证，
        // 且 RTLD_DEFAULT 对该符号在 24/25 上返回 nullptr（API 26 引入）
        cached = reinterpret_cast<read_cb_fn>(dlsym(RTLD_DEFAULT, "__system_property_read_callback"));
    }
    return cached;
}

struct ReadResult {
    char value[kValueBuf];
    size_t len = 0;
    bool truncated = false;
};

void read_via_callback(void *cookie, const char *name, const char *value, uint32_t /*serial*/) {
    auto *out = static_cast<ReadResult *>(cookie);
    out->len = strnlen(value, kValueBuf);
    out->truncated = out->len >= kValueBuf - 1;
    const size_t copy = out->len < kValueBuf - 1 ? out->len : kValueBuf - 1;
    memcpy(out->value, value, copy);
    out->value[copy] = '\0';
}

ReadResult read_prop(const prop_info *pi) {
    ReadResult r;
    if (read_cb_fn cb = resolve_read_callback(); cb != nullptr) {
        cb(pi, read_via_callback, &r);
        return r;
    }
    // API 24/25 旧路径：92 字节缓冲
    char value[PROP_VALUE_MAX];
    __system_property_read(pi, nullptr, value);
    const size_t len = strnlen(value, PROP_VALUE_MAX);
    r.len = len;
    r.truncated = len >= PROP_VALUE_MAX - 1;
    const size_t copy = len < kValueBuf - 1 ? len : kValueBuf - 1;
    memcpy(r.value, value, copy);
    r.value[copy] = '\0';
    return r;
}

void list_via_callback(void *cookie, const char *name, const char *value, uint32_t /*serial*/) {
    auto *out = static_cast<std::vector<std::string> *>(cookie);
    out->emplace_back(std::string(name) + "=" + std::string(value));
}

void trampoline(const prop_info *pi, void *cookie) {
    auto *out = static_cast<std::vector<std::string> *>(cookie);
    if (read_cb_fn cb = resolve_read_callback(); cb != nullptr) {
        cb(pi, list_via_callback, out);
        return;
    }
    char name[PROP_NAME_MAX];
    char value[PROP_VALUE_MAX];
    __system_property_read(pi, name, value);
    out->emplace_back(std::string(name) + "=" + std::string(value));
}

}  // namespace

// 全量枚举为 "key=value" 列表（旧路径值可能被 92 字节截断——上层必须处理）。
int da_prop_list(std::vector<std::string> *out) {
    out->clear();
    return __system_property_foreach(trampoline, out);
}

// 单键读取：返回 0 成功 / -ENOENT 键不存在；truncated 指示值长度顶满缓冲（疑似截断）。
int da_prop_get(const char *key, char *buf, size_t buf_size, int *truncated) {
    const prop_info *pi = __system_property_find(key);
    if (pi == nullptr) return -ENOENT;
    const ReadResult r = read_prop(pi);
    *truncated = r.truncated ? 1 : 0;
    const size_t copy = r.len < buf_size - 1 ? r.len : buf_size - 1;
    memcpy(buf, r.value, copy);
    buf[copy] = '\0';
    return 0;
}

```

`core/native-platform/src/main/cpp/raw_syscalls.cpp`:

```cpp
#include <errno.h>
#include <time.h>
#include <fcntl.h>
#include <sys/stat.h>
#include <sys/statfs.h>
#include <unistd.h>

#include "svc_arch.h"

// 任务 2.1/2.2：裸 svc 三路执行器 + syscall 守卫。
//
// 守卫原则（specs/signal-collection「兼容门控强制」）：
// - 原生层只放行显式白名单内的 syscall 号 —— Kotlin 层(ProbeCap)做版本×内核×targetSdk
//   门控，本层是纵深防御：即使上层误调用，白名单外的号也拒绝执行。
// - statx 一类「白名单外 = SIGSYS 杀进程」的调用在本项目任何路径都不会发起。
// - 全部裸 syscall 必须经 guard_* 入口，禁止在别处直接调 da_syscall*。

namespace {

// 与 Kotlin 侧 com.detectany.core.nativeplatform.SyscallAllowlist 保持镜像（双端各自静态定义，
// 单测断言两集合一致）。语义：三路 diff 最小集 + 显式探针号（全部只读/无副作用或被内核安全检查前置拦截）。
constexpr long kAllowedNrs[] = {
#if defined(__aarch64__)
    56L,    // openat
    57L,    // close
    79L,    // newfstatat
    48L,    // faccessat
    43L,    // statfs（C2 statfs 交叉）
    142L,   // reboot（C8 KSU supercall 探针：无 CAP_SYS_BOOT 时内核先返回 -EPERM，无副作用）
    167L,   // prctl（C8 旧版 KSU 探针）
    117L,   // ptrace（C8 父进程注入残留探针）
    45L,    // APatch supercall（复用 truncate 号；无 superkey 时退化为 truncate 失败）
#else
    322L,   // openat (arm32)
    6L,     // close (arm32)
    300L,   // fstatat64/newfstatat (arm32)
    334L,   // faccessat (arm32)
    266L,   // statfs64 (arm32)
    88L,    // reboot (arm32)
    172L,   // prctl (arm32)
    26L,    // ptrace (arm32)
    46L,    // APatch supercall (arm32: __NR3264_truncate)
#endif
};

bool nr_allowed(long nr) {
    for (long allowed : kAllowedNrs) {
        if (allowed == nr) return true;
    }
    return false;
}

long guarded(long nr, long a0, long a1, long a2, long a3, bool need4) {
    if (!nr_allowed(nr)) {
        // 不可能的路径：直接拒绝而不发起调用（绝不试探）
        return -EPERM;
    }
    return need4 ? da_syscall4(nr, a0, a1, a2, a3) : da_syscall3(nr, a0, a1, a2);
}

int errno_of(long ret) {
    return (ret < 0 && ret >= -4095L) ? static_cast<int>(-ret) : 0;
}

}  // namespace

// ---- 裸 svc 路径（供 JNI 导出） ----

long da_svc_fstatat(int dirfd, const char *path, struct stat *st, int flags) {
#if defined(__aarch64__)
    constexpr long kNr = 79L;
#else
    constexpr long kNr = 300L;
#endif
    return guarded(kNr, dirfd, reinterpret_cast<long>(path), reinterpret_cast<long>(st), flags, true);
}

long da_svc_faccessat(int dirfd, const char *path, int mode) {
#if defined(__aarch64__)
    constexpr long kNr = 48L;
#else
    constexpr long kNr = 334L;
#endif
    return guarded(kNr, dirfd, reinterpret_cast<long>(path), mode, 0, false);
}

long da_svc_openat(int dirfd, const char *path, int flags, int mode) {
#if defined(__aarch64__)
    constexpr long kNr = 56L;
#else
    constexpr long kNr = 322L;
#endif
    return guarded(kNr, dirfd, reinterpret_cast<long>(path), flags, mode, true);
}

long da_svc_close(int fd) {
#if defined(__aarch64__)
    constexpr long kNr = 57L;
#else
    constexpr long kNr = 6L;
#endif
    return guarded(kNr, fd, 0, 0, 0, false);
}

// ---- libc 对照路径（同一批事实，经 libc 包装，可被 PLT/GOT hook） ----

long da_libc_fstatat(int dirfd, const char *path, struct stat *st, int flags) {
    return fstatat(dirfd, path, st, flags);
}

long da_libc_faccessat(int dirfd, const char *path, int mode) {
    return faccessat(dirfd, path, mode, 0);
}

long da_libc_openat(int dirfd, const char *path, int flags, int mode) {
    return openat(dirfd, path, flags, mode);
}

// 守卫可见性（供调试/JNI 查询）
bool da_svc_nr_allowed(long nr) { return nr_allowed(nr); }

// ---- C2：statfs 交叉（f_type 魔数判别 tmpfs/overlay/fuse） ----

long da_svc_statfs(const char *path, unsigned long *f_type, unsigned long *bsize, unsigned long *blocks) {
#if defined(__aarch64__)
    constexpr long kNr = 43L;
    struct statfs st {};
    const long ret = guarded(kNr, reinterpret_cast<long>(path), reinterpret_cast<long>(&st), 0, 0, false);
#else
    constexpr long kNr = 266L;
    struct statfs64 st {};
    const long ret = guarded(kNr, reinterpret_cast<long>(path), reinterpret_cast<long>(&st), sizeof(st), 0, true);
#endif
    if (ret == 0) {
        *f_type = static_cast<unsigned long>(st.f_type);
        *bsize = static_cast<unsigned long>(st.f_bsize);
        *blocks = static_cast<unsigned long>(st.f_blocks);
    }
    return ret;
}

// ---- C8：行为探针（裸 svc） ----

// KSU 系 supercall：reboot(0xDEADBEEF, 0xCAFEBABE, magic3, arg4)。
// 原厂内核：无 CAP_SYS_BOOT → -EPERM（内核在解析 magic 前拦截，无副作用）。
// KSU v2.0+：kprobe pre_handler 匹配双魔数 → task_work 安装 anon_inode:[ksu_driver] fd。
long da_ksu_reboot_supercall(long magic1, long magic2, long magic3, long arg4) {
#if defined(__aarch64__)
    constexpr long kNr = 142L;
#else
    constexpr long kNr = 88L;
#endif
    return guarded(kNr, magic1, magic2, magic3, arg4, true);
}

// 旧版 KSU（<=v1.0.5）prctl 探针：prctl(0xDEADBEEF, arg2..arg5)。
long da_legacy_prctl_probe(long option, long arg2, long arg3, long arg4, long arg5) {
#if defined(__aarch64__)
    constexpr long kNr = 167L;
#else
    constexpr long kNr = 172L;
#endif
    if (!nr_allowed(kNr)) return -EPERM;
    return da_syscall5(kNr, option, arg2, arg3, arg4, arg5);
}

// APatch supercall：syscall(45, superkey, ver_and_cmd, arg3, arg4)。
// 无 superkey 时原厂/未授权均退化为 truncate 失败；计时侧信道由上层叠加。
long da_apatch_supercall(const char *key, long ver_and_cmd, long arg3, long arg4) {
#if defined(__aarch64__)
    constexpr long kNr = 45L;
#else
    constexpr long kNr = 46L;
#endif
    return guarded(kNr, reinterpret_cast<long>(key), ver_and_cmd, arg3, arg4, true);
}

// ftruncate 裸调用（syscall 46，APatch 不 hook 此调用——用于时序基线对比）
long da_svc_ftruncate(int fd, long length) {
#if defined(__aarch64__)
    constexpr long kNr = 46L;
#else
    constexpr long kNr = 46L; // arm32 ftruncate 也是 46
#endif
    return guarded(kNr, fd, length, 0, 0, true);
}

// ---- 时序侧信道：syscall 45 (被 APatch hook) vs 46 (未被 hook) ----
// APatch 的 supercall handler 在 syscall 45 上做 superkey 字符串比对后
// 才 fall through 到真实 truncate → 可测量的额外开销。
// 返回 long[4]: [truncate_median_ns, ftruncate_median_ns, getpid_median_ns, iterations]
int da_supercall_timing(long *out_truncate_ns, long *out_ftruncate_ns, long *out_getpid_ns, int iterations) {
    if (iterations <= 0 || iterations > 10000) iterations = 1000;

    struct timespec ts {};
    auto now_ns = [&ts]() -> long {
        clock_gettime(CLOCK_MONOTONIC, &ts);
        return ts.tv_sec * 1000000000L + ts.tv_nsec;
    };

    // ---- truncate (syscall 45, APatch hooked) ----
    long total_tr = 0;
    for (int i = 0; i < iterations; i++) {
        long t0 = now_ns();
        da_apatch_supercall("detectany_timing_probe_not_a_key", 0, 0, 0);
        total_tr += now_ns() - t0;
    }
    *out_truncate_ns = total_tr / iterations;

    // ---- ftruncate (syscall 46, NOT hooked) ----
    long total_ftr = 0;
    for (int i = 0; i < iterations; i++) {
        long t0 = now_ns();
        da_svc_ftruncate(-1, 0); // invalid fd → EBADF, same cost class
        total_ftr += now_ns() - t0;
    }
    *out_ftruncate_ns = total_ftr / iterations;

    // ---- getpid (baseline, cheapest syscall) ----
    long total_pid = 0;
    for (int i = 0; i < iterations; i++) {
        long t0 = now_ns();
#if defined(__aarch64__)
        guarded(172L /*getpid*/, 0, 0, 0, 0, false); // 172 on arm64
#else
        guarded(20L /*getpid*/, 0, 0, 0, 0, false);   // 20 on arm32
#endif
        total_pid += now_ns() - t0;
    }
    *out_getpid_ns = total_pid / iterations;

    return 0;
}

// ptrace 裸调用（C8 父进程残留探针用）
long da_raw_ptrace(long request, long pid, long addr, long data) {
#if defined(__aarch64__)
    constexpr long kNr = 117L;
#else
    constexpr long kNr = 26L;
#endif
    return guarded(kNr, request, pid, addr, data, true);
}
```

`core/native-platform/src/main/cpp/svc_arch.h`:

```h
#pragma once

// 裸 svc 系统调用（design.md D3）：不经过 libc 包装，绕开 PLT/GOT hook。
// 仅支持本项目构建的 ABI（arm64-v8a / armeabi-v7a）。

#if defined(__aarch64__)

static inline long da_syscall1(long nr, long a0) {
    register long x8 asm("x8") = nr;
    register long x0 asm("x0") = a0;
    asm volatile("svc #0" : "=r"(x0) : "r"(x8), "r"(x0) : "memory", "cc");
    return x0;
}

static inline long da_syscall3(long nr, long a0, long a1, long a2) {
    register long x8 asm("x8") = nr;
    register long x0 asm("x0") = a0;
    register long x1 asm("x1") = a1;
    register long x2 asm("x2") = a2;
    asm volatile("svc #0" : "=r"(x0) : "r"(x8), "r"(x0), "r"(x1), "r"(x2) : "memory", "cc");
    return x0;
}

static inline long da_syscall4(long nr, long a0, long a1, long a2, long a3) {
    register long x8 asm("x8") = nr;
    register long x0 asm("x0") = a0;
    register long x1 asm("x1") = a1;
    register long x2 asm("x2") = a2;
    register long x3 asm("x3") = a3;
    asm volatile("svc #0" : "=r"(x0) : "r"(x8), "r"(x0), "r"(x1), "r"(x2), "r"(x3) : "memory", "cc");
    return x0;
}

static inline long da_syscall5(long nr, long a0, long a1, long a2, long a3, long a4) {
    register long x8 asm("x8") = nr;
    register long x0 asm("x0") = a0;
    register long x1 asm("x1") = a1;
    register long x2 asm("x2") = a2;
    register long x3 asm("x3") = a3;
    register long x4 asm("x4") = a4;
    asm volatile("svc #0" : "=r"(x0) : "r"(x8), "r"(x0), "r"(x1), "r"(x2), "r"(x3), "r"(x4) : "memory", "cc");
    return x0;
}

#elif defined(__arm__)

static inline long da_syscall1(long nr, long a0) {
    register long r7 asm("r7") = nr;
    register long r0 asm("r0") = a0;
    asm volatile("svc #0" : "=r"(r0) : "r"(r7), "r"(r0) : "memory", "cc");
    return r0;
}

static inline long da_syscall5(long nr, long a0, long a1, long a2, long a3, long a4) {
    register long r7 asm("r7") = nr;
    register long r0 asm("r0") = a0;
    register long r1 asm("r1") = a1;
    register long r2 asm("r2") = a2;
    register long r3 asm("r3") = a3;
    register long r4 asm("r4") = a4;
    asm volatile("svc #0" : "=r"(r0) : "r"(r7), "r"(r0), "r"(r1), "r"(r2), "r"(r3), "r"(r4) : "memory", "cc");
    return r0;
}

static inline long da_syscall3(long nr, long a0, long a1, long a2) {
    register long r7 asm("r7") = nr;
    register long r0 asm("r0") = a0;
    register long r1 asm("r1") = a1;
    register long r2 asm("r2") = a2;
    asm volatile("svc #0" : "=r"(r0) : "r"(r7), "r"(r0), "r"(r1), "r"(r2) : "memory", "cc");
    return r0;
}

static inline long da_syscall4(long nr, long a0, long a1, long a2, long a3) {
    register long r7 asm("r7") = nr;
    register long r0 asm("r0") = a0;
    register long r1 asm("r1") = a1;
    register long r2 asm("r2") = a2;
    register long r3 asm("r3") = a3;
    asm volatile("svc #0" : "=r"(r0) : "r"(r7), "r"(r0), "r"(r1), "r"(r2), "r"(r3) : "memory", "cc");
    return r0;
}

#else
#error "detectany_native only supports arm64-v8a / armeabi-v7a"
#endif
```

`core/native-platform/src/main/kotlin/com/detectany/core/nativeplatform/ElfJni.kt`:

```kt
package com.detectany.core.nativeplatform

/**
 * ELF 自解析桥（任务 2.3）：从磁盘解析 .dynsym 得符号 st_value，
 * 与 /proc/self/maps 基址相加 = 期望运行时地址，与 dlsym 实测交叉验证（C7 亦复用）。
 */
object ElfJni {
    init { NativePlatform.ensureLoaded() }

    /** 磁盘 .dynsym 中符号的 st_value（vaddr）；0 = 未命中/失败。 */
    external fun exportValue(path: String, symbol: String): Long

    /** 该库文件在本进程 maps 中的加载基址；0 = 未映射。 */
    external fun mappedBase(path: String): Long

    /**
     * dlsym(RTLD_DEFAULT, symbol) 实测地址。仅用于验证：磁盘自解析期望地址 vs 运行时地址交叉
     * （任务 2.3）。生产判定路径不使用 dlsym（可被 hook）。
     */
    external fun dlsymAddress(symbol: String): Long

    /** 读磁盘 ELF 中 [vaddr, vaddr+len) 的原始字节（C7 prologue 比对）；null = 失败。 */
    external fun readAtVaddr(path: String, vaddr: Long, len: Int): ByteArray?

    /** 动态重定位表："symbolName\tgotVaddr"（C7 GOT 完整性）。 */
    external fun relocations(path: String): Array<String>
}

/** 组合视图：期望运行时地址 = 基址 + st_value。 */
class ElfInspector {
    fun expectedRuntimeAddress(path: String, symbol: String): Long? {
        val value = ElfJni.exportValue(path, symbol)
        val base = ElfJni.mappedBase(path)
        return if (value != 0L && base != 0L) base + value else null
    }
}
```

`core/native-platform/src/main/kotlin/com/detectany/core/nativeplatform/NativePlatform.kt`:

```kt
package com.detectany.core.nativeplatform

import java.util.concurrent.atomic.AtomicBoolean

/**
 * native 基座入口：加载唯一 native 库，暴露各 JNI 桥接对象。
 */
object NativePlatform {
    private val loaded = AtomicBoolean(false)

    fun ensureLoaded() {
        if (loaded.compareAndSet(false, true)) {
            System.loadLibrary("detectany_native")
        }
    }

    /** native 库版本（连通性验证用）。 */
    external fun nativeVersion(): String
}
```

`core/native-platform/src/main/kotlin/com/detectany/core/nativeplatform/ProbeJni.kt`:

```kt
package com.detectany.core.nativeplatform

/**
 * C2/C6/C8 行为与结构探针桥。
 */
object ProbeJni {
    init { NativePlatform.ensureLoaded() }

    /** /proc/self/fd 全量 readlink 显示名。 */
    external fun fdNames(): Array<String>

    /**
     * KSU 系 supercall 探针（reboot 双魔数）。
     * 返回 String[]：元素 0 = "ret=…;errno=…"，其余 = 新增 fd 名（如 anon_inode:[ksu_driver]）。
     */
    external fun ksuProbe(magic1: Long, magic2: Long, magic3: Long, arg4: Long): Array<String>

    /** APatch supercall：long[3] = [ret, errno, elapsedNs]。 */
    external fun apatchProbe(key: String, verAndCmd: Long): LongArray

    /** SELinux errno 语义探针：long[2] = [write返回, errno]；-1 = open 失败。 */
    external fun selinuxLabelProbe(label: String): LongArray

    /** NativeBridgeError()：-1 不可判定 / 0 无标志 / 1 had_error 为真。 */
    external fun nativebridgeError(): Int

    /** 旧版 KSU prctl 探针：long[2] = [ret, errno]。原厂内核对未知 option 返回 -EINVAL。 */
    external fun prctlProbe(option: Long, a2: Long, a3: Long, a4: Long, a5: Long): LongArray

    /**
     * 时序侧信道：long[3] = [truncate_median_ns, ftruncate_median_ns, getpid_median_ns]。
     * syscall 45 被 APatch supercall handler hook（superkey 字符串比对开销）；
     * syscall 46 未被 hook。差值 = supercall handler 的额外处理时间。
     */
    external fun supercallTiming(iterations: Int): LongArray

    /** 父进程 ptrace 注入残留：>=0 为 eventmsg（== zygote pid 即注入）；-1 不可执行。 */
    external fun ptraceParentEventmsg(): Long

    /** 自身内存安全读取（process_vm_readv 自身；execute-only 文本上返回全零）。 */
    external fun memRead(addr: Long, len: Int): ByteArray?

    /** 自身文本段读取（execute-only 安全：临时 mprotect 加 R，读后恢复原权限）。 */
    external fun textRead(addr: Long, len: Int): ByteArray?

    /** linker 权威加载列表："name\tload_bias"。 */
    external fun solist(): Array<String>

    /** RWX 自探针：1 maps 可信 / 0 maps 被篡改 / -1 分配失败。 */
    external fun rwxSelfProbe(): Int
}

```

`core/native-platform/src/main/kotlin/com/detectany/core/nativeplatform/PropJni.kt`:

```kt
package com.detectany.core.nativeplatform

/**
 * 属性访问桥（任务 2.4）：统一走 API 24 兼容的 __system_property_read 路径。
 */
object PropJni {
    init { NativePlatform.ensureLoaded() }

    /** 全量 "key=value" 枚举。 */
    external fun propList(): Array<String>

    /** 单键读取；null = 键不存在。值长度 91 视为疑似截断（PROP_VALUE_MAX=92）。 */
    external fun propGet(key: String): String?
}

/** 属性观测视图 + 截断处理。 */
class SystemPropertiesBridge {
    data class PropValue(val key: String, val value: String, val possiblyTruncated: Boolean)

    fun get(key: String): PropValue? {
        val v = PropJni.propGet(key) ?: return null
        return PropValue(key, v, possiblyTruncated = v.length >= PROP_VALUE_MAX - 1)
    }

    fun list(): Map<String, String> = PropJni.propList().associate { line ->
        val idx = line.indexOf('=')
        if (idx <= 0) line to "" else line.substring(0, idx) to line.substring(idx + 1)
    }

    companion object {
        const val PROP_VALUE_MAX = 92
    }
}

```

`core/native-platform/src/main/kotlin/com/detectany/core/nativeplatform/StatOutcome.kt`:

```kt
package com.detectany.core.nativeplatform

/**
 * 归一化的 stat 观测：三路（Java/libc/svc）可比。
 */
sealed interface StatOutcome {
    data class Ok(
        val dev: Long,
        val ino: Long,
        val mode: Long,
        val uid: Long,
        val gid: Long,
        val size: Long,
        val mtimeSec: Long,
    ) : StatOutcome

    data class Errno(val code: Int) : StatOutcome

    val exists: Boolean get() = this is Ok
}

/** JNI long[9] → StatOutcome。 */
fun parseStatArray(arr: LongArray): StatOutcome {
    require(arr.size == 9) { "stat array must have 9 elements" }
    return if (arr[0] == 0L) {
        StatOutcome.Ok(
            dev = arr[2], ino = arr[3], mode = arr[4],
            uid = arr[5], gid = arr[6], size = arr[7], mtimeSec = arr[8],
        )
    } else {
        StatOutcome.Errno(arr[1].toInt())
    }
}

```

`core/native-platform/src/main/kotlin/com/detectany/core/nativeplatform/SyscallAllowlist.kt`:

```kt
package com.detectany.core.nativeplatform

/**
 * 原生层 syscall 白名单（任务 2.2）：与 raw_syscalls.cpp 中 kAllowedNrs 保持镜像。
 * 双端静态定义是刻意设计（native 层不依赖任何运行时数据）；单测断言集合一致性。
 * 成员 = 三路 diff 最小集 + 显式探针号（reboot/prctl/ptrace/supercall 均为安全探测形态）。
 */
object SyscallAllowlist {

    /** arm64-v8a：openat/close/newfstatat/faccessat + statfs/reboot/prctl/ptrace/supercall(45) */
    val ARM64 = setOf(56L, 57L, 79L, 48L, 43L, 142L, 167L, 117L, 45L)

    /** armeabi-v7a：openat/close/fstatat64/faccessat + statfs64/reboot/prctl/ptrace/supercall(46) */
    val ARM32 = setOf(322L, 6L, 300L, 334L, 266L, 88L, 172L, 26L, 46L)

    /** statx 的 syscall 号（两种架构）——必须永远不在白名单内。 */
    const val STATX_ARM64 = 291L
    const val STATX_ARM32 = 397L

    fun forArch(abi: String?): Set<Long>? = when (abi) {
        "arm64-v8a" -> ARM64
        "armeabi-v7a" -> ARM32
        else -> null
    }
}
```
