| 设备 | 日期 | 结果 | 备注 |
|---|---|---|---|
| Pixel 8 / Android 16（**ROOT + 作弊工具，对抗组**） | 2026-11-06 | **首条真阳性**：L2=50 定性检出，HOOK 域 5 条 A 级 prologue 命中——检测器全部 JNI 入口被定向 hook（mem=`ff442d58 0c1d5093 02940495` = LDR/SBFM/BL 补丁桩，磁盘为 paciasp/stp 标准序言）；root 静态信号全被隐藏栈压制（susfs/Shamiko 效果实证），指令完整性成为唯一存活强信号（设计文档「物理不变量」论断的实战验证）。**进程作用域发现**：主 app 进程被 hook、instrumentation 进程未注入（作弊模块按进程靶向）。修复链：execute-only 文本需 textRead（mprotect 临时放行）、base 投票第二因子改用不可 hook 的 phdr 表 |
| Pixel 8 / Android 16（干净） | 2026-11-06 | 设备测试 21/21，0 误报；**L1=19-20ms（超预算）、L2≈2.4s（超预算）** → 优化债务：C5 改 mmap 分块扫描、C3 属性枚举限流、C1 路径表 L1 子集化 | 首台真机。**修复 2 个真机发现**：① 真实 attestation 链 deviceLocked=BOOLEAN（旧 ENUMERATED 解析崩溃）→ 解析器防御化；② Android 16 原生挂载裸 /debug_ramdisk(+pts) → 挂载规则收窄至 `.magisk` 子路径 |

### 时延优化记录（2026-11-06 并行化改造；2026-11-10 C5 二次优化）

| 指标 | 优化前 | 优化后 | 手段 |
|---|---|---|---|
| L2 总耗时 | 1400-2400ms | **889ms**（2026-11-06，Pixel 8） | CompletableFuture 并行采集器（总耗时≈max 单项） |
| L2 瓶颈 | 串行 sum(C1..C8) | C5=880ms（单 manager） | C5 两步策略（先包名后 APK）；C6 lazy smaps |
| C5 双 manager 场景 | **3214ms**（SukiSU+APatch 全量字节扫描） | **398ms（7.6×）**，L2 整体 2362→**969ms** | 探针按条目下推：原实现每个 APK 扫全框架 18 探针超集而 matcher 只消费条目自身 2-3 探针——纯浪费；现在只扫条目自身探针且全部命中即停（早退实际生效） |
| L1 快扫 | 19-20ms | **9-13ms**（P8 实测，2026-11-10） | 并行化 + 三项修复（见下） |

### L1 时延修复链（2026-11-10）

| 层 | 问题 | 修复 | 实测 |
|---|---|---|---|
| SelfGuard | debuggerProbe 每次扫描同步 fork（大进程 fork 一次 80-118ms） | 后台定时探测（30s 周期）+ inspect 读缓存快照（3 周期新鲜度，过期视为无信号=FN 安全向）；probeIntervalMs=0 保留同步模式供测试 | inspect 83-118ms → 7-9ms |
| C3 | L1 也做 800+ 属性全量枚举 | L1 直读键集（特征条目精确键+成对两侧+vbs），L2/L3 维持全量 | 9-12ms → 1-5ms |
| C1 | L1 探测全部 32 条路径（C 级 /data/adb 族对 app 永远 EACCES 纯浪费） | L1 仅 A/B 级子集 | 2-3ms → 2ms |

下一步：C5 APK mmap 替代流式读 + 扫描结果缓存；C6 solist 增量。

> 真机矩阵执行：每台设备按上表逐项安装框架最新版 → 跑 L2 → 导出报告
> （`ReportExporter`）→ 汇总检出率/误报至本文件。对抗组单独记录组合风险分捕获率。

`docs/RUNBOOK.md`:

```md
# Detect-Any 使用与运维手册

## 构建

```bash
./gradlew assembleDebug      # 调试构建（16 模块 + 双 ABI native）
./gradlew assembleRelease    # 发布构建（见「发布装配清单」）
```

要求：JDK 17+、Android SDK（platforms 35、NDK 25.2.9519653、CMake 3.22.1）、Gradle 8.9（wrapper 自带）。

## 测试（本手册内命令均已验证可运行）

```bash
./gradlew test                              # 全部主机单测（compat/采集器分析器/引擎/证明/自保护/报告）
./gradlew :app:connectedDebugAndroidTest    # 设备/模拟器冒烟 + UI 走查 + 时延探针
```

注意：`connectedAndroidTest` **不支持** `--tests` 过滤器，请全量执行。

## 产物核验（16KB 对齐 / 无系统 crypto 依赖）

**首选：自动门禁**（packageDebug/packageRelease/packageDebugAndroidTest 后强制执行，
同时校验 APK zip 对齐与全部打包 so 的 PT_LOAD p_align≥16384）：

```bash
./gradlew :app:verify16kAlignment
```

> 2026-11-06：该门禁曾拦截 conscrypt-android 2.5.2（libconscrypt_jni.so p_align=4096，
> 无 16KB 版本、Play 已强制）——已移除该依赖，Android 7 TLS 1.3 改走 GMS
> Conscrypt Provider（GmsCore_OpenSSL），缺失时回落 TLS 1.2 + 自有锚/pinning。
> 新增 native 依赖必须过此门禁。

```bash
SO=$(find core/native-platform/build -name "libdetectany_native.so" -path "*stripped*arm64*" | head -1)
RE=$(ls ~/Library/Android/sdk/ndk/*/toolchains/llvm/prebuilt/*/bin/llvm-readelf | head -1)
$RE -lW "$SO" | grep LOAD     # Align 必须为 0x4000
$RE -dW "$SO" | grep NEEDED   # 仅允许 liblog/libm/libdl/libc
```

## 特征热更新运营

- 数据格式：`data/signature/src/main/kotlin/.../SignatureDb.kt`（JSON schema）。
- 更新信封：`SignedUpdate`（ed25519 签名，payload ≤200KB）。
- 发布流程：改动条目 → 语义化递增 `version` → 运营密钥签名 → 下发（Phase 2 通道）或手动导入。
- **手动导入**：更新文件 JSON 走 `SignatureImport.import(text)`——与在线更新同一验证/装载路径。
- 失效条目：置 `until`（框架版本区间），不需要代码变更。

## 发布装配清单（每次发版必做）

1. **自保护 XOR 常量替换**：`core/native-platform/src/main/cpp/guard.cpp` 中
   `SIG_A/SIG_B`（APK 签名摘要）与 `CRC_A/CRC_B`（classes.dex CRC32）按发布产物替换
   （debug 构建默认以当前构建实测值为预期，release 必须替换否则自保护失效）。
2. **Google attestation 双根 PEM**：装配 `AttestationVerifier(roots)`（旧 RSA 根 + 2026-02 起
   ECDSA P-384 新根）；未装配时 C9 走 Degraded（不误报）。
3. 更新密钥（ed25519 公钥）注入 `SignatureStore(verifier=...)`。
4. keybox 黑名单/机型基线随特征数据下发。

## 排障速查

| 症状 | 根因 → 处置 |
|---|---|
| 模拟器 props 测试进程崩溃 | 旧 `__system_property_read` 遇超长属性名 → 已双路径（26+ callback） |
| `reboot` 探针进程 SIGSYS | seccomp 白名单外 → 已 fork 隔离（父进程收割） |
| C7 找不到自身库 | `extractNativeLibs=false` → 已 solist 基址 + zip 抽取 |
| GOT 校验误报 memcpy | IFUNC 运行时变体 → 样本已排除 IFUNC 族 |
| 属性条目命中默认值 | 条目缺 `valueContains` → 加值条件 |

```

`feature/attestation/src/main/kotlin/com/detectany/feature/attestation/AttestationClient.kt`:

```kt
package com.detectany.feature.attestation

import android.security.keystore.KeyGenParameterSpec
import android.security.keystore.KeyProperties
import java.security.KeyPairGenerator
import java.security.KeyStore
import java.security.SecureRandom
import java.security.cert.X509Certificate

/**
 * 证明发起（任务 5.1，设备侧）：AndroidKeyStore EC P-256 + setAttestationChallenge（API 24+）。
 */
interface AttestationSource {
    data class Result(val challenge: ByteArray, val chain: List<X509Certificate>)
    fun attest(): Result?
    fun cleanup()
}

class AttestationClient(
    private val alias: String = "detectany_attest_${System.nanoTime()}",
) : AttestationSource {

    /** 发起一次 attestation；失败（无 Keymaster/不可用）返回 null（上层走降级）。 */
    override fun attest(): AttestationSource.Result? = runCatching {
        val challenge = ByteArray(32).also { SecureRandom().nextBytes(it) }
        val spec = KeyGenParameterSpec.Builder(alias, KeyProperties.PURPOSE_SIGN or KeyProperties.PURPOSE_VERIFY)
            .setAlgorithmParameterSpec(java.security.spec.ECGenParameterSpec("secp256r1"))
            .setDigests(KeyProperties.DIGEST_SHA256)
            .setAttestationChallenge(challenge)
            .build()
        val kpg = KeyPairGenerator.getInstance(KeyProperties.KEY_ALGORITHM_EC, "AndroidKeyStore")
        kpg.initialize(spec)
        kpg.generateKeyPair()

        val ks = KeyStore.getInstance("AndroidKeyStore").apply { load(null) }
        val chain = ks.getCertificateChain(alias)?.toList()?.filterIsInstance<X509Certificate>() ?: return null
        if (chain.isEmpty()) return null
        AttestationSource.Result(challenge, chain)
    }.getOrNull()

    override fun cleanup() {
        runCatching {
            val ks = KeyStore.getInstance("AndroidKeyStore").apply { load(null) }
            ks.deleteEntry(alias)
        }
    }
}

```

`feature/attestation/src/main/kotlin/com/detectany/feature/attestation/AttestationCollector.kt`:

```kt
package com.detectany.feature.attestation

import com.detectany.core.model.Collector
import com.detectany.core.model.CollectorId
import com.detectany.core.model.CollectorResult
import com.detectany.core.model.Confidence
import com.detectany.core.model.Finding
import com.detectany.core.model.ScanInput
import java.util.concurrent.Executors
import java.util.concurrent.TimeUnit
import java.util.concurrent.TimeoutException

/**
 * C13 硬件证明采集器（P2-1，2026-11-11）：把 AttestationService 接入 Collector 管道——
 * L3 档专用（DetectionEngine 注入），L1/L2 永不触发（Keymaster 慢/无响应不得拖垮快扫）。
 *
 * 超时治理：service.run()（含 KeyPairGenerator 同步等待 Keymaster）跑在专用单线程，
 * [timeoutMs] 预算内未返回 → DEGRADED + 超时 info finding（不采信不误报——specs 契约）。
 *
 * challenge 新鲜性：AttestationClient 每次调用内生成 32 字节 SecureRandom 并嵌入
 * attestation 扩展，Verifier echo 校验证明「链为本调用现铸」——扫描级防重放已满足；
 * 外部 nonce 绑定属服务端流程（Play Integrity 域，不在此层）。
 */
class AttestationCollector(
    private val service: AttestationService,
    private val timeoutMs: Long = 3000L,
) : Collector {

    override val id: CollectorId = CollectorId.C13_ATTESTATION

    private val executor = Executors.newSingleThreadExecutor { r ->
        Thread(r, "DetectAny-Attest").apply { isDaemon = true }
    }

    override fun collect(input: ScanInput): CollectorResult = try {
        val future = executor.submit { runCatching { service.run() }.getOrNull() }
        val outcome = try {
            future.get(timeoutMs, TimeUnit.MILLISECONDS)
        } catch (_: TimeoutException) {
            future.cancel(true) // KeyPairGenerator 不可中断时线程驻留至进程退出（daemon）
            null
        }

        when (outcome) {
            null -> CollectorResult(
                collector = id,
                status = CollectorResult.Status.DEGRADED,
                findings = listOf(timeoutInfo()),
                meta = mapOf("attest" to "timeout", "budgetMs" to timeoutMs.toString()),
            )
            is AttestationService.Outcome.Trusted -> CollectorResult(
                collector = id,
                status = CollectorResult.Status.OK,
                findings = outcome.findings,
                meta = mapOf(
                    "attest" to "trusted",
                    "verdict" to "hardware-backed chain verified",
                    "patchLevel" to (outcome.description.rootOfTrust?.verifiedBootState?.toString() ?: "?"),
                ),
            )
            is AttestationService.Outcome.Degraded -> CollectorResult(
                collector = id,
                status = CollectorResult.Status.DEGRADED,
                findings = outcome.findings,
                meta = mapOf("attest" to "degraded", "reason" to outcome.reason.take(120)),
            )
            is AttestationService.Outcome.SpoofSuspected -> CollectorResult(
                collector = id,
                status = CollectorResult.Status.OK,
                findings = outcome.findings,
                meta = mapOf("attest" to "spoof_suspected", "reason" to outcome.reason.take(120)),
            )
            else -> CollectorResult(collector = id, status = CollectorResult.Status.DEGRADED, meta = mapOf("attest" to "unreachable"))
        }
    } catch (e: Throwable) {
        CollectorResult(collector = id, status = CollectorResult.Status.BLOCKED, meta = mapOf("error" to (e.message ?: e.javaClass.simpleName)))
    }

    private fun timeoutInfo() = Finding(
        ruleId = "attest.degraded.timeout",
        collector = id,
        confidence = Confidence.C,
        category = Finding.Category.ATTESTATION_SPOOF,
        framework = null,
        detail = "硬件证明 ${timeoutMs}ms 内未返回（Keymaster 慢/无响应）——只采集不采信",
        raw = mapOf("budgetMs" to timeoutMs.toString()),
    )
}

`feature/attestation/src/main/kotlin/com/detectany/feature/attestation/AttestationService.kt`:

```kt
package com.detectany.feature.attestation

import com.detectany.core.model.CollectorId
import com.detectany.core.model.Confidence
import com.detectany.core.model.Finding

/**
 * C9 编排（任务 5.3）：发起 → 链校验 → CRL → 字段交叉 → first_api_level 降级。
 * 输出 Finding 供引擎聚合（attestation_spoof / anti_detect 类）。
 */
class AttestationService(
    private val client: AttestationSource = AttestationClient(),
    private val verifier: AttestationVerifier,
    private val crl: CrlStore,
    /** first_api_level 读取（PropJni.propGet("ro.product.first_api_level") 装配层注入） */
    private val firstApiLevel: () -> Int?,
    private val ownPackageName: String? = null,
    private val crossInput: AttestationVerifier.CrossInput = AttestationVerifier.CrossInput(),
) {
    sealed interface Outcome {
        /** 硬件可信 */
        data class Trusted(val description: KeyDescriptionParser.KeyDescription, val findings: List<Finding>) : Outcome

        /** 只采集不采信（first_api_level<26 / 软件链 / 不可用）——降级标注 */
        data class Degraded(val reason: String, val findings: List<Finding>) : Outcome

        /** 证明链异常 = 伪造证据（A 级）
         *  吊销命中 / RootOfTrust 与系统声称矛盾等 */
        data class SpoofSuspected(val reason: String, val findings: List<Finding>) : Outcome
    }

    fun run(): Outcome {
        val fal = firstApiLevel()
        if (fal != null && fal < 26) {
            // specs/attestation-verify：首发 API <26 设备硬件链不保证（软件链可被完整伪造）→ 只采集不采信
            val attested = client.attest()
            client.cleanup()
            return Outcome.Degraded(
                "first_api_level=$fal < 26",
                attested?.let { listOf(infoFinding("attest.degraded.first_api_level", "证明已采集但不采信（首发 API $fal）")) } ?: emptyList(),
            )
        }

        val attested = client.attest()
            ?: return Outcome.Degraded("attestation unavailable (no keymaster or error)", emptyList())
        try {
            val revoked = runCatching { crl.revokedSerials() }.getOrDefault(emptySet())
            return when (val v = verifier.verify(attested.chain, attested.challenge, ownPackageName, revoked, AttestationVerifier.RkpPolicy(fal))) {
                is AttestationVerifier.Verdict.Revoked -> Outcome.SpoofSuspected(
                    "revoked serial ${v.serialHex}",
                    listOf(
                        Finding(
                            ruleId = "attest.keybox_revoked",
                            collector = CollectorId.C13_ATTESTATION, // P2-1：独立编号取代 C1 占位
                            confidence = Confidence.A,
                            category = Finding.Category.ATTESTATION_SPOOF,
                            framework = "trickystore",
                            detail = "证书链序列号 ${v.serialHex} 命中吊销清单（注意：CRL-clean 亦不等于可信——Google 存在影子降级 Strong→Device 不入 CRL）",
                            raw = mapOf("serial" to v.serialHex),
                        ),
                    ),
                )

                is AttestationVerifier.Verdict.Rejected -> Outcome.Degraded(
                    "chain rejected: ${v.reason}",
                    listOf(infoFinding("attest.rejected", v.reason)),
                )

                is AttestationVerifier.Verdict.SoftwareBacked -> Outcome.Degraded(
                    "software-backed attestation (forgeable)",
                    listOf(
                        Finding(
                            ruleId = "attest.software_backed",
                            collector = CollectorId.C13_ATTESTATION,
                            confidence = Confidence.B,
                            category = Finding.Category.ATTESTATION_SPOOF,
                            framework = null,
                            detail = "证明为软件背书（可被 AOSP 软件 keybox 伪造）",
                            raw = emptyMap(),
                        ),
                    ),
                )

                is AttestationVerifier.Verdict.Trusted -> {
                    val anomalies = verifier.crossCheck(v.description, crossInput)
                    val findings = anomalies.map {
                        Finding(
                            ruleId = "attest.cross.${it.kind}",
                            collector = CollectorId.C13_ATTESTATION,
                            confidence = Confidence.A,
                            category = Finding.Category.ATTESTATION_SPOOF,
                            framework = "trickystore",
                            detail = it.detail,
                            raw = emptyMap(),
                        )
                    }
                    if (findings.isEmpty()) Outcome.Trusted(v.description, emptyList())
                    else Outcome.SpoofSuspected("cross-check anomalies: ${anomalies.size}", findings)
                }
            }
        } catch (e: Throwable) {
            // 2026-11-10 审计修复：原 try 只有 finally 无 catch——ASN.1 解析异常
            // （历史教训：deviceLocked BOOLEAN schema 变体曾致崩）会穿透冒泡。
            // 证明层异常 = 优雅降级（不采信不误报），永不上抛扫描流程。
            return Outcome.Degraded("attestation parse error: " + e.javaClass.simpleName, emptyList())
        } finally {
            client.cleanup()
        }
    }

    private fun infoFinding(id: String, detail: String) = Finding(
        ruleId = id,
        collector = CollectorId.C13_ATTESTATION,
        confidence = Confidence.C,
        category = Finding.Category.ATTESTATION_SPOOF,
        framework = null,
        detail = detail,
        raw = emptyMap(),
    )
}

```

`feature/attestation/src/main/kotlin/com/detectany/feature/attestation/AttestationVerifier.kt`:

```kt
package com.detectany.feature.attestation

import java.security.KeyStore
import java.security.cert.CertPathValidator
import java.security.cert.CertificateFactory
import java.security.cert.PKIXParameters
import java.security.cert.X509Certificate
import java.util.Date

/**
 * 证明链校验（任务 5.2/5.3）：锚定 + 只取第一个扩展 + 安全级别 + 吊销 + 应用绑定 + 字段交叉。
 * 纯 Kotlin，测试向量全部主机可测。
 */
/**
 * A2: 根证书锚定集动态消费 —— 官方端点 android.googleapis.com/attestation/root
 * 返回 JSON 数组（PEM 编码根证书列表）。RSA 根 2016 版已于 2026-05-24 过期，
 * Google 官方要求继续信任 SERIALNUMBER=f92009e853b6b045 除非入 CRL。
 */
class RootAnchorStore(private val fetcher: (String) -> String? = { null }) {
    private val url get() = "https://android.googleapis.com/attestation/root"

    data class AnchorResult(
        val roots: List<java.security.cert.X509Certificate>,
        val source: String,
    )

    fun loadDynamic(): AnchorResult? = runCatching {
        val body = fetcher(url) ?: return@runCatching null
        val pems = Regex("-----BEGIN CERTIFICATE-----[\\s\\S]*?-----END CERTIFICATE-----")
            .findAll(body).map { it.value }.toList()
        if (pems.isEmpty()) return@runCatching null
        val cf = java.security.cert.CertificateFactory.getInstance("X.509")
        val roots = pems.mapNotNull { pem ->
            runCatching {
                cf.generateCertificate(java.io.ByteArrayInputStream(pem.toByteArray()))
                    as java.security.cert.X509Certificate
            }.getOrNull()
        }
        if (roots.isEmpty()) null else AnchorResult(roots, "dynamic")
    }.getOrNull()

    companion object {
        /**
         * RSA 过期信任豁免（官方政策）：
         * Google 要求对 2016 RSA 根（SERIALNUMBER=f92009e853b6b045）在过期后
         * 继续信任签发的链，除非该序列号入 CRL。RKP 证书则必须检查过期。
         */
        fun isExpiredRootTrusted(cert: java.security.cert.X509Certificate): Boolean {
            val isRsaRoot = cert.publicKey.algorithm == "RSA"
            val isGoogleRoot = cert.subjectX500Principal.name.contains("Google")
            return isRsaRoot && isGoogleRoot && cert.notAfter < java.util.Date()
        }
    }
}

class AttestationVerifier(
    /** 信任锚（Google 双根：旧 RSA 根 + 2026-02 起 ECDSA P-384 新根；由装配层注入）。 */
    private val roots: List<X509Certificate>,
) {

    sealed interface Verdict {
        /** 硬件链、锚定通过、无吊销：可作为信任依据 */
        data class Trusted(val description: KeyDescriptionParser.KeyDescription) : Verdict

        /** 链有效但非硬件背书（软件 attestation——可被 TrickyStore 的 AOSP 软件 keybox 完整伪造） */
        data class SoftwareBacked(val description: KeyDescriptionParser.KeyDescription) : Verdict

        /** 吊销序列号命中 → attestation_spoof 证据 */
        data class Revoked(val cert: X509Certificate, val serialHex: String) : Verdict

        /** 锚定/签名失败、无扩展、challenge 或 appKey 不符、追加叶子被拒等 */
        data class Rejected(val reason: String) : Verdict
    }

    /**
     * A1 (P0): Android 16 首发（first_api_level ≥ 36）为 RKP-only 设备——
     * 官方策略要求仅接受 ECDSA P-384 RKP 链。RSA 根链在 RKP-only 设备上
     * 出现即为强伪造信号（2026-02-01 起 ECDSA 根独占 RKP 设备）。
     */
    data class RkpPolicy(val firstApiLevel: Int? = null) {
        val isRkpOnlyDevice: Boolean get() = (firstApiLevel ?: 0) >= 36
    }

    fun verify(
        chain: List<X509Certificate>,
        expectedChallenge: ByteArray? = null,
        expectedPackageName: String? = null,
        revokedSerials: Set<String> = emptySet(),
        rkpPolicy: RkpPolicy = RkpPolicy(),
    ): Verdict {
        if (roots.isEmpty()) return Verdict.Rejected("no trust anchors configured")
        if (chain.isEmpty()) return Verdict.Rejected("empty chain")

        // 1) PKIX 锚定（仅信任显式锚；追加伪叶子/断链在此被拒）
        val anchorResult = validateChainToAnchors(chain)
        if (anchorResult != null) return Verdict.Rejected(anchorResult)

        // 2) 只取链上第一个 attestation 扩展
        val parsed = KeyDescriptionParser.parseFirstFromChain(chain)
            ?: return Verdict.Rejected("no attestation extension in chain")
        val desc = parsed.second

        // 3) challenge 回显
        if (expectedChallenge != null && !desc.attestationChallenge.contentEquals(expectedChallenge)) {
            return Verdict.Rejected("challenge mismatch")
        }

        // 4) attestationApplicationId 绑定（Quarkslab：跨应用中继在此被拒）
        if (expectedPackageName != null) {
            val names = desc.attestationApplicationId?.packageNames ?: emptyList()
            if (names.none { it == expectedPackageName }) {
                return Verdict.Rejected("attestationApplicationId mismatch (relay suspected)")
            }
        }

        // 5) 吊销序列号（全链逐证书）
        for (cert in chain) {
            val serial = cert.serialNumber.toString(16).lowercase()
            if (serial in revokedSerials) {
                return Verdict.Revoked(cert, serial)
            }
        }

        // A1: RKP-only 策略检查——Android 16 首发+RSA 根 = 强伪造信号
        if (desc.hardwareBacked && rkpPolicy.isRkpOnlyDevice) {
            val isRkpChain = chain.any { cert ->
                cert.publicKey.algorithm == "EC" && cert.sigAlgName.contains("384")
            }
            if (!isRkpChain) {
                return Verdict.Rejected(
                    "RKP-only device (first_api_level=${rkpPolicy.firstApiLevel}) presented non-RKP RSA chain " +
                        "— Android 16 launch devices exclusively use ECDSA P-384 RKP; RSA chain is a spoof signal",
                )
            }
        }

        return if (desc.hardwareBacked) Verdict.Trusted(desc) else Verdict.SoftwareBacked(desc)
    }

    /** 字段交叉（任务 5.3）：RootOfTrust/补丁级别/StrongBox 能力 vs 系统声称与机型能力库。 */
    data class CrossInput(
        val claimedBootloaderLocked: Boolean? = null,
        val claimedPatchLevelYymm: Int? = null,
        /** 机型能力库：model -> 是否具备 StrongBox */
        val strongBoxCapableModels: Map<String, Boolean> = emptyMap(),
        val claimedModel: String? = null,
    )

    data class CrossAnomaly(val kind: String, val detail: String)

    fun crossCheck(desc: KeyDescriptionParser.KeyDescription, input: CrossInput): List<CrossAnomaly> = buildList {
        val rot = desc.rootOfTrust
        if (rot != null) {
            if (rot.verifiedBootState !in listOf(
                    KeyDescriptionParser.VerifiedBootState.VERIFIED,
                    KeyDescriptionParser.VerifiedBootState.SELF_SIGNED,
                )
            ) {
                add(CrossAnomaly("verified_boot_state", "verifiedBootState=${rot.verifiedBootState}（非 Verified/SelfSigned）"))
            }
            input.claimedBootloaderLocked?.let { claimed ->
                if (claimed && !rot.deviceLocked) {
                    add(CrossAnomaly("bootloader_lock_mismatch", "系统声称锁定但证明 deviceLocked=false（TrickyStore leaf-hack 形态）"))
                }
            }
        }
        input.claimedPatchLevelYymm?.let { claimed ->
            val attested = desc.osPatchLevel
            if (attested != null && attested > claimed) {
                add(CrossAnomaly("patch_level_future", "证明补丁级别 $attested 晚于系统声称 $claimed"))
            }
        }
        if (desc.attestationSecurityLevel == KeyDescriptionParser.SecurityLevel.STRONGBOX) {
            val model = input.claimedModel
            if (model != null && input.strongBoxCapableModels[model] != true) {
                add(CrossAnomaly("strongbox_capability_mismatch", "声称 StrongBox 但机型库无此能力: $model"))
            }
        }
    }

    private fun validateChainToAnchors(chain: List<X509Certificate>): String? = runCatching {
        val ks = KeyStore.getInstance(KeyStore.getDefaultType())
        ks.load(null)
        roots.forEachIndexed { i, r -> ks.setCertificateEntry("attest-root-$i", r) }
        val cf = CertificateFactory.getInstance("X.509")
        val cp = cf.generateCertPath(chain)
        val params = PKIXParameters(ks).apply {
            isRevocationEnabled = false // 吊销走专用 CRL 信号（Google 官方状态端点）
            date = Date()
        }
        CertPathValidator.getInstance("PKIX").validate(cp, params)
        null
    }.getOrElse { "chain validation failed: ${it.message ?: it.javaClass.simpleName}" }
}

```

`feature/attestation/src/main/kotlin/com/detectany/feature/attestation/CrlStore.kt`:

```kt
package com.detectany.feature.attestation

import kotlinx.serialization.Serializable
import kotlinx.serialization.json.Json

/**
 * Google 官方 keybox 吊销状态消费（任务 5.2）：
 * 端点 https://android.googleapis.com/attestation/status（公开 JSON），
 * 按 Cache-Control max-age 节流拉取，离线快照兜底。
 */
class CrlStore(
    /** fetcher: url -> (body, cacheControlMaxAgeSeconds?) */
    private val fetcher: (String) -> Pair<String, Long?> = { error("no fetcher wired") },
    private val snapshotStore: SnapshotStore = NoopSnapshotStore(),
    private val url: String = "https://android.googleapis.com/attestation/status",
) {
    interface SnapshotStore {
        fun load(): String?
        fun save(body: String)
    }

    class NoopSnapshotStore : SnapshotStore {
        override fun load(): String? = null
        override fun save(body: String) {}
    }

    @Serializable
    data class StatusResponse(val entries: Map<String, StatusEntry> = emptyMap(), val validUntil: String? = null) {
        @Serializable
        data class StatusEntry(val status: String? = null, val reason: String? = null)
    }

    @Volatile
    private var cached: StatusResponse? = null

    @Volatile
    private var lastFetchAtMs: Long = 0

    @Volatile
    private var cachedMaxAgeSec: Long = DEFAULT_MAX_AGE_SEC

    /** 当前吊销序列号集合（小写 hex）。force = 忽略节流。 */
    fun revokedSerials(nowMs: Long = System.currentTimeMillis(), force: Boolean = false): Set<String> {
        val fresh = cached != null && nowMs - lastFetchAtMs < cachedMaxAgeSec * 1000
        if (!force && fresh) return current().entries.filter { it.value.status == "REVOKED" }.keys

        val (body, maxAge) = runCatching { fetcher(url) }.getOrElse {
            // 离线：快照兜底
            val snap = snapshotStore.load()
            if (snap != null) {
                cached = parse(snap)
            }
            return current().entries.filter { it.value.status == "REVOKED" }.keys
        }
        maxAge?.let { cachedMaxAgeSec = it }
        val parsed = parse(body)
        cached = parsed
        lastFetchAtMs = nowMs
        snapshotStore.save(body)
        return parsed.entries.filter { it.value.status == "REVOKED" }.keys
    }

    fun current(): StatusResponse = cached ?: (snapshotStore.load()?.let { parse(it) } ?: StatusResponse())

    internal fun parse(body: String): StatusResponse =
        Json { ignoreUnknownKeys = true }.decodeFromString(StatusResponse.serializer(), body)

    companion object {
        const val DEFAULT_MAX_AGE_SEC = 3600L
    }
}

```

`feature/attestation/src/main/kotlin/com/detectany/feature/attestation/KeyDescription.kt`:

```kt
package com.detectany.feature.attestation

import java.security.cert.X509Certificate
import org.bouncycastle.asn1.ASN1EncodableVector
import org.bouncycastle.asn1.ASN1Enumerated
import org.bouncycastle.asn1.ASN1Integer
import org.bouncycastle.asn1.ASN1OctetString
import org.bouncycastle.asn1.ASN1Primitive
import org.bouncycastle.asn1.ASN1Sequence
import org.bouncycastle.asn1.DEROctetString
import org.bouncycastle.asn1.DERSequence
import org.bouncycastle.asn1.DERTaggedObject

/**
 * Key attestation 扩展解析（任务 5.1）。
 * OID 1.3.6.1.4.1.11129.2.1.17；KeyDescription ASN.1（AOSP keymaster attestation record）。
 * AuthorizationList 记录 tag 空间：703 keySize / 724 rootOfTrust / 725 osVersion /
 * 726 osPatchLevel / 727 attestationApplicationId / 736 vendorPatchLevel / 737 bootPatchLevel。
 */
object KeyDescriptionParser {

    const val EXTENSION_OID = "1.3.6.1.4.1.11129.2.1.17"

    enum class SecurityLevel { SOFTWARE, TRUSTED_ENVIRONMENT, STRONGBOX }

    enum class VerifiedBootState { VERIFIED, SELF_SIGNED, UNVERIFIED, FAILED }

    data class RootOfTrust(
        val verifiedBootKey: ByteArray,
        val deviceLocked: Boolean,
        val verifiedBootState: VerifiedBootState,
        val verifiedBootHash: ByteArray,
    )

    data class ApplicationId(
        val packageNames: List<String>,
        val signatureDigests: List<ByteArray>,
    )

    data class KeyDescription(
        val attestationVersion: Int,
        val attestationSecurityLevel: SecurityLevel,
        val keymasterVersion: Int,
        val attestationChallenge: ByteArray,
        val rootOfTrust: RootOfTrust?,
        val osVersion: Int?,
        val osPatchLevel: Int?,
        val vendorPatchLevel: Int?,
        val bootPatchLevel: Int?,
        val keySize: Int?,
        val attestationApplicationId: ApplicationId?,
    ) {
        val hardwareBacked: Boolean
            get() = attestationSecurityLevel != SecurityLevel.SOFTWARE
    }

    /** 从证书链解析「第一个」出现的 attestation 扩展（specs/attestation-verify：只信第一个）。 */
    fun parseFirstFromChain(chain: List<X509Certificate>): Pair<X509Certificate, KeyDescription>? {
        for (cert in chain) {
            val ext = cert.getExtensionValue(EXTENSION_OID) ?: continue
            val octets = ASN1OctetString.getInstance(ASN1Primitive.fromByteArray(ext)).octets
            return cert to parse(octets)
        }
        return null
    }

    fun parse(bytes: ByteArray): KeyDescription {
        val seq = ASN1Sequence.getInstance(bytes)
        var i = 0
        val attestationVersion = ASN1Integer.getInstance(seq.getObjectAt(i++)).intValueExact()
        val attestationSecurityLevel = level(ASN1Enumerated.getInstance(seq.getObjectAt(i++)).intValueExact())
        val keymasterVersion = ASN1Integer.getInstance(seq.getObjectAt(i++)).intValueExact()
        // keymasterSecurityLevel（ENUMERATED）——不参与本层判定，跳过
        i++
        val challenge = ASN1OctetString.getInstance(seq.getObjectAt(i++)).octets
        // uniqueId
        i++
        val softwareEnforced = ASN1Sequence.getInstance(seq.getObjectAt(i++))
        val teeEnforced = ASN1Sequence.getInstance(seq.getObjectAt(i))
        val holder = mutableMapOf<Int, org.bouncycastle.asn1.ASN1TaggedObject>()

        fun scan(list: ASN1Sequence, into: MutableMap<Int, org.bouncycastle.asn1.ASN1TaggedObject>) {
            for (el in list) {
                val tagged = el as? org.bouncycastle.asn1.ASN1TaggedObject ?: continue
                into[tagged.tagNo] = tagged
            }
        }
        scan(softwareEnforced, holder)
        scan(teeEnforced, holder)

        val rot = holder[724]?.let { runCatching { parseRootOfRootGuard(it) }.getOrNull() }
        val app = holder[727]?.let { runCatching { parseApplicationId(ASN1Sequence.getInstance(it, true)) }.getOrNull() }

        return KeyDescription(
            attestationVersion = attestationVersion,
            attestationSecurityLevel = attestationSecurityLevel,
            keymasterVersion = keymasterVersion,
            attestationChallenge = challenge,
            rootOfTrust = rot,
            osVersion = taggedInt(holder, 725),
            osPatchLevel = taggedInt(holder, 726),
            vendorPatchLevel = taggedInt(holder, 736),
            bootPatchLevel = taggedInt(holder, 737),
            keySize = taggedInt(holder, 703),
            attestationApplicationId = app,
        )
    }

    /**
     * 真实 schema（AOSP）：deviceLocked 为 BOOLEAN；verifiedBootState 为 ENUMERATED。
     * 2026-11-06 Pixel 8/Android 16 实测：旧解析按 ENUMERATED 读 deviceLocked 直接崩溃。
     * 逐字段防御式解析：任一字段形态不符 → 整体返回 null（不崩、不采信），绝不抛出。
     */
    private fun parseRootOfRootGuard(tagged: org.bouncycastle.asn1.ASN1TaggedObject): RootOfTrust? =
        parseRootOfTrust(ASN1Sequence.getInstance(tagged, true))

    private fun parseRootOfTrust(seq: ASN1Sequence): RootOfTrust? = runCatching {
        val lockedObj = seq.getObjectAt(1)
        val deviceLocked = when (lockedObj) {
            is org.bouncycastle.asn1.ASN1Boolean -> lockedObj.isTrue
            else -> ASN1Enumerated.getInstance(lockedObj).intValueExact() != 0 // 兼容旧合成向量
        }
        RootOfTrust(
            verifiedBootKey = ASN1OctetString.getInstance(seq.getObjectAt(0)).octets,
            deviceLocked = deviceLocked,
            verifiedBootState = VerifiedBootState.entries[ASN1Enumerated.getInstance(seq.getObjectAt(2)).intValueExact()],
            verifiedBootHash = ASN1OctetString.getInstance(seq.getObjectAt(3)).octets,
        )
    }.getOrNull()

    /** tagged 字段防御读取：形态不符返回 null（真实厂商链形态多样）。 */
    internal fun taggedInt(holder: Map<Int, org.bouncycastle.asn1.ASN1TaggedObject>, tag: Int): Int? =
        holder[tag]?.let { t -> runCatching { ASN1Integer.getInstance(t, true).value.toInt() }.getOrNull() }

    private fun parseApplicationId(seq: ASN1Sequence): ApplicationId {
        val packageInfos = ASN1Sequence.getInstance(seq.getObjectAt(0))
        val sigDigests = ASN1Sequence.getInstance(seq.getObjectAt(1))
        val names = buildList {
            for (el in packageInfos) {
                val info = ASN1Sequence.getInstance(el)
                add(String(ASN1OctetString.getInstance(info.getObjectAt(0)).octets, Charsets.UTF_8))
            }
        }
        val digests = buildList {
            for (el in sigDigests) add(ASN1OctetString.getInstance(el).octets)
        }
        return ApplicationId(names, digests)
    }

    private fun level(v: Int): SecurityLevel = when (v) {
        0 -> SecurityLevel.SOFTWARE
        1 -> SecurityLevel.TRUSTED_ENVIRONMENT
        2 -> SecurityLevel.STRONGBOX
        else -> throw IllegalArgumentException("unknown security level $v")
    }

    // ---- 测试向量构造（暴露给单测与回放工具） ----

    fun buildTestExtension(
        challenge: ByteArray,
        securityLevel: SecurityLevel,
        deviceLocked: Boolean,
        bootState: VerifiedBootState,
        packageName: String? = null,
        osPatchLevel: Int? = null,
    ): ByteArray {
        val v = ASN1EncodableVector()
        v.add(ASN1Integer(300))          // attestationVersion (Keymaster 3+)
        v.add(ASN1Enumerated(securityLevel.ordinal))
        v.add(ASN1Integer(300))          // keymasterVersion
        v.add(ASN1Enumerated(securityLevel.ordinal)) // keymasterSecurityLevel
        v.add(DEROctetString(challenge))
        v.add(DEROctetString(byteArrayOf()))         // uniqueId
        v.add(DERSequence())             // softwareEnforced: empty

        val tee = ASN1EncodableVector()
        tee.add(DERTaggedObject(703, ASN1Integer(256)))
        val rot = ASN1EncodableVector()
        rot.add(DEROctetString("vbkey".toByteArray()))
        rot.add(if (deviceLocked) org.bouncycastle.asn1.ASN1Boolean.TRUE else org.bouncycastle.asn1.ASN1Boolean.FALSE)
        rot.add(ASN1Enumerated(bootState.ordinal))
        rot.add(DEROctetString("vbhash".toByteArray()))
        tee.add(DERTaggedObject(724, DERSequence(rot)))
        osPatchLevel?.let { tee.add(DERTaggedObject(726, ASN1Integer(it.toLong()))) }
        if (packageName != null) {
            val pkg = ASN1EncodableVector()
            val pkgInfo = ASN1EncodableVector()
            pkgInfo.add(DEROctetString(packageName.toByteArray(Charsets.UTF_8)))
            pkgInfo.add(ASN1Integer(1))
            pkg.add(DERSequence(pkgInfo))
            val sigs = ASN1EncodableVector()
            sigs.add(DEROctetString(byteArrayOf(1, 2, 3)))
            val appId = ASN1EncodableVector()
            appId.add(DERSequence(pkg))
            appId.add(DERSequence(sigs))
            tee.add(DERTaggedObject(727, DERSequence(appId)))
        }
        v.add(DERSequence(tee))
        return DERSequence(v).encoded
    }
}

```

`feature/attestation/src/main/kotlin/com/detectany/feature/attestation/PlayIntegrityClient.kt`:

```kt
package com.detectany.feature.attestation

/**
 * Play Integrity 客户端（Phase 2 服务端通道脚手架）：
 * 客户端只发起 requestIntegrityToken，verdict 解码/验证在服务端完成
 * （specs/attestation-verify：不在被测设备上做最终信任判定）。
 *
 * 装配前提：Google Play Console 项目 + 服务账号密钥 + Play Integrity API 启用。
 */
class PlayIntegrityClient(
    /** Google Play services 的 ApplicationInfo（由装配层注入） */
    private val requestToken: (String) -> String?,
) {
    data class IntegrityResult(
        val token: String?,
        val error: String? = null,
    )

    /**
     * 发起 Play Integrity 请求（Standard，毫秒级）。
     * @param requestHash 服务端生成的防重放哈希（绑定会话）
     * @return token 或 null（GMS 不可用/离线）
     */
    fun requestIntegrity(requestHash: String): IntegrityResult {
        val token = requestToken(requestHash)
            ?: return IntegrityResult(null, "Google Play services unavailable or offline")
        return IntegrityResult(token)
    }
}

/**
 * Play Integrity 服务端解码器（部署在应用后端，不在设备上）。
 * 依赖：Google Play Integrity API 服务账号 + Google API 客户端库。
 *
 * 验证步骤：
 * 1. 用 Play Console 下发的 AES 密钥本地解密 JWE
 * 2. 用 EC 公钥验签 JWS
 * 3. 校验 requestHash 回显、包签名、时间戳
 * 4. 检查 deviceIntegrity 字段（MEETS_BASIC/DEVICE/STRONG_INTEGRITY）
 */
object PlayIntegrityServerDecoder {
    data class DecodedVerdict(
        val appIdRecognitionVerdict: String?,
        val deviceRecognitionVerdict: List<String>,
        val appLicensingVerdict: String?,
        val requestHashMatch: Boolean,
    )

    /**
     * 服务端解码（伪代码骨架——实际需 Google API 客户端库）：
     * decryptIntegrityToken(token, aesKey) → verifyJws(jws, ecPublicKey) → parseVerdict(json)
     */
    fun decodeSkeleton(): String = "服务端解码需部署 Google API 客户端；详见 developer.android.com/google/play/integrity"
}

`feature/attestation/src/test/kotlin/com/detectany/feature/attestation/AttestationServiceTest.kt`:

```kt
package com.detectany.feature.attestation

import java.security.KeyPairGenerator
import java.security.cert.X509Certificate
import java.util.Date
import kotlin.test.Test
import kotlin.test.assertTrue

/** 任务 5.3 验证：first_api_level 降级路径（specs/attestation-verify「设备等级降级」）。 */
class AttestationServiceTest {

    private class FakeSource(val result: AttestationSource.Result?) : AttestationSource {
        override fun attest(): AttestationSource.Result? = result
        override fun cleanup() {}
    }

    private fun cert(): X509Certificate {
        val kp = KeyPairGenerator.getInstance("RSA").apply { initialize(2048) }.genKeyPair()
        val now = Date()
        val b = org.bouncycastle.cert.jcajce.JcaX509v3CertificateBuilder(
            org.bouncycastle.asn1.x500.X500Name("CN=x"), java.math.BigInteger.valueOf(7), now, Date(now.time + 3600_000L),
            org.bouncycastle.asn1.x500.X500Name("CN=x"), kp.public,
        )
        return org.bouncycastle.cert.jcajce.JcaX509CertificateConverter().getCertificate(
            b.build(org.bouncycastle.operator.jcajce.JcaContentSignerBuilder("SHA256withRSA").build(kp.private)),
        )
    }

    private fun crl() = CrlStore(fetcher = { error("unused") })

    @Test
    fun firstApiLevelBelow26IsDegraded() {
        val service = AttestationService(
            client = FakeSource(AttestationSource.Result("n".toByteArray(), listOf(cert()))),
            verifier = AttestationVerifier(emptyList()),
            crl = crl(),
            firstApiLevel = { 24 },
        )
        val out = service.run()
        assertTrue(out is AttestationService.Outcome.Degraded, out.toString())
        assertTrue((out as AttestationService.Outcome.Degraded).reason.contains("first_api_level"))
    }

    @Test
    fun unavailableAttestationIsDegraded() {
        val service = AttestationService(
            client = FakeSource(null),
            verifier = AttestationVerifier(emptyList()),
            crl = crl(),
            firstApiLevel = { 33 },
        )
        val out = service.run()
        assertTrue(out is AttestationService.Outcome.Degraded && out.reason.contains("unavailable"), out.toString())
    }

    @Test
    fun noAnchorsOnModernDeviceIsDegradedNotSpoof() {
        // 无锚配置（真实 Google 根未装配时）：降级为 Rejected-Degraded，绝不误报 Spoof
        val service = AttestationService(
            client = FakeSource(AttestationSource.Result("n".toByteArray(), listOf(cert()))),
            verifier = AttestationVerifier(emptyList()),
            crl = crl(),
            firstApiLevel = { 33 },
        )
        val out = service.run()
        assertTrue(out is AttestationService.Outcome.Degraded, "应降级而非误报: $out")
    }
}

`feature/attestation/src/test/kotlin/com/detectany/feature/attestation/AttestationVerifierTest.kt`:

```kt
package com.detectany.feature.attestation

import org.bouncycastle.asn1.x500.X500Name
import org.bouncycastle.asn1.x509.BasicConstraints
import org.bouncycastle.asn1.x509.Extension
import org.bouncycastle.asn1.x509.GeneralName
import org.bouncycastle.asn1.x509.GeneralNames
import org.bouncycastle.cert.jcajce.JcaX509v3CertificateBuilder
import org.bouncycastle.cert.jcajce.JcaX509CertificateConverter
import org.bouncycastle.operator.jcajce.JcaContentSignerBuilder
import java.math.BigInteger
import java.security.KeyPairGenerator
import java.security.SecureRandom
import java.security.cert.X509Certificate
import java.util.Date
import kotlin.test.Test
import kotlin.test.assertEquals
import kotlin.test.assertTrue

/**
 * 任务 5.1/5.2/5.3 验证：合成链测试向量（合法/吊销/软件链/追加伪叶子/中继/challenge）。
 */
class AttestationVerifierTest {

    private val verifier = AttestationVerifier(listOf(genCa().first))

    // ---- 测试链工厂 ----

    private class Ca(cert: X509Certificate, key: java.security.PrivateKey) {
        val cert = cert
        val key = key
    }

    private fun genCa(): Pair<X509Certificate, java.security.PrivateKey> {
        val kp = KeyPairGenerator.getInstance("RSA").apply { initialize(2048) }.genKeyPair()
        val now = Date()
        val b = JcaX509v3CertificateBuilder(
            X500Name("CN=attest-test-root"), BigInteger(64, SecureRandom()), now, Date(now.time + 3600_000L),
            X500Name("CN=attest-test-root"), kp.public,
        )
        b.addExtension(Extension.basicConstraints, true, BasicConstraints(true))
        return JcaX509CertificateConverter().getCertificate(b.build(JcaContentSignerBuilder("SHA256withRSA").build(kp.private))) to kp.private
    }

    private fun genLeaf(
        issuer: X509Certificate,
        issuerKey: java.security.PrivateKey,
        serial: BigInteger = BigInteger(64, SecureRandom()),
        extension: ByteArray? = null,
        subject: String = "CN=attested-key",
        ca: Boolean = false,
    ): X509Certificate {
        val kp = KeyPairGenerator.getInstance("RSA").apply { initialize(2048) }.genKeyPair()
        val now = Date()
        val b = JcaX509v3CertificateBuilder(
            X500Name(issuer.subjectX500Principal.name), serial, now, Date(now.time + 3600_000L),
            X500Name(subject), kp.public,
        )
        b.addExtension(
            Extension.subjectAlternativeName, false,
            GeneralNames(arrayOf(GeneralName(GeneralName.dNSName, "localhost"))),
        )
        if (ca) b.addExtension(Extension.basicConstraints, true, BasicConstraints(true))
        extension?.let { b.addExtension(org.bouncycastle.asn1.x509.Extension(org.bouncycastle.asn1.ASN1ObjectIdentifier(KeyDescriptionParser.EXTENSION_OID), false, org.bouncycastle.asn1.DEROctetString(it))) }
        return JcaX509CertificateConverter().getCertificate(b.build(JcaContentSignerBuilder("SHA256withRSA").build(issuerKey)))
    }

    private val hwExt = KeyDescriptionParser.buildTestExtension(
        challenge = "nonce-123".toByteArray(),
        securityLevel = KeyDescriptionParser.SecurityLevel.TRUSTED_ENVIRONMENT,
        deviceLocked = true,
        bootState = KeyDescriptionParser.VerifiedBootState.VERIFIED,
        packageName = "com.detectany.app",
        osPatchLevel = 202610,
    )

    // ---- 5.1 解析 ----

    @Test
    fun parsesKeyDescriptionFields() {
        val (root, rootKey) = genCa()
        val v = AttestationVerifier(listOf(root))
        val leaf = genLeaf(root, rootKey, extension = hwExt)
        val r = v.verify(listOf(leaf, root), expectedChallenge = "nonce-123".toByteArray(), expectedPackageName = "com.detectany.app")
        assertTrue(r is AttestationVerifier.Verdict.Trusted, r.toString())
        val d = (r as AttestationVerifier.Verdict.Trusted).description
        assertEquals(KeyDescriptionParser.SecurityLevel.TRUSTED_ENVIRONMENT, d.attestationSecurityLevel)
        assertTrue(d.rootOfTrust!!.deviceLocked)
        assertEquals(KeyDescriptionParser.VerifiedBootState.VERIFIED, d.rootOfTrust.verifiedBootState)
        assertEquals(202610, d.osPatchLevel)
        assertEquals(listOf("com.detectany.app"), d.attestationApplicationId!!.packageNames)
    }

    // ---- 5.2 三类测试向量 ----

    @Test
    fun revokedSerialVector() {
        val (root, rootKey) = genCa()
        val v = AttestationVerifier(listOf(root))
        val serial = BigInteger("aabbccdd", 16)
        val leaf = genLeaf(root, rootKey, serial = serial, extension = hwExt)
        val r = v.verify(listOf(leaf, root), revokedSerials = setOf(serial.toString(16)))
        assertTrue(r is AttestationVerifier.Verdict.Revoked, r.toString())
        assertEquals("aabbccdd", (r as AttestationVerifier.Verdict.Revoked).serialHex)
    }

    @Test
    fun softwareChainVector() {
        val (root, rootKey) = genCa()
        val v = AttestationVerifier(listOf(root))
        val swExt = KeyDescriptionParser.buildTestExtension(
            challenge = "n".toByteArray(), securityLevel = KeyDescriptionParser.SecurityLevel.SOFTWARE,
            deviceLocked = true, bootState = KeyDescriptionParser.VerifiedBootState.VERIFIED,
        )
        val leaf = genLeaf(root, rootKey, extension = swExt)
        val r = v.verify(listOf(leaf, root))
        assertTrue(r is AttestationVerifier.Verdict.SoftwareBacked, r.toString())
    }

    @Test
    fun appendedLeafVector() {
        // 攻击者在合法链尾部追加自签伪叶子（带伪造扩展）：PKIX 锚定必须拒绝
        val (root, rootKey) = genCa()
        val attackerCa = genCa()
        val v = AttestationVerifier(listOf(root))
        val legit = genLeaf(root, rootKey, extension = hwExt)
        val fakeExt = KeyDescriptionParser.buildTestExtension(
            challenge = "evil".toByteArray(), securityLevel = KeyDescriptionParser.SecurityLevel.TRUSTED_ENVIRONMENT,
            deviceLocked = true, bootState = KeyDescriptionParser.VerifiedBootState.VERIFIED,
        )
        val appended = genLeaf(attackerCa.first, attackerCa.second, extension = fakeExt)
        val r = v.verify(listOf(appended, legit, root))
        assertTrue(r is AttestationVerifier.Verdict.Rejected, "追加伪叶子必须被拒: $r")
    }

    @Test
    fun firstExtensionOnlyIsUsed() {
        // 链上多处扩展：leaf（第一个）与中间 CA（第二个）都带扩展 → 只取 leaf 的
        val (root, rootKey) = genCa()
        val v = AttestationVerifier(listOf(root))
        val otherExt = KeyDescriptionParser.buildTestExtension(
            challenge = "other".toByteArray(), securityLevel = KeyDescriptionParser.SecurityLevel.SOFTWARE,
            deviceLocked = false, bootState = KeyDescriptionParser.VerifiedBootState.FAILED,
        )
        // 中间 CA：自带密钥对、由根签发、携带第二个（伪造形态）扩展
        val interKp = KeyPairGenerator.getInstance("RSA").apply { initialize(2048) }.genKeyPair()
        val now = Date()
        val interBuilder = JcaX509v3CertificateBuilder(
            X500Name(root.subjectX500Principal.name), BigInteger(64, SecureRandom()), now, Date(now.time + 3600_000L),
            X500Name("CN=attest-inter"), interKp.public,
        )
        interBuilder.addExtension(Extension.basicConstraints, true, BasicConstraints(true))
        interBuilder.addExtension(
            org.bouncycastle.asn1.x509.Extension(
                org.bouncycastle.asn1.ASN1ObjectIdentifier(KeyDescriptionParser.EXTENSION_OID), false,
                org.bouncycastle.asn1.DEROctetString(otherExt),
            ),
        )
        val inter = JcaX509CertificateConverter().getCertificate(
            interBuilder.build(JcaContentSignerBuilder("SHA256withRSA").build(rootKey)),
        )
        // leaf 由中间 CA 签发、带真实扩展（第一个）
        val leaf = genLeaf(inter, interKp.private, extension = hwExt)
        val r = v.verify(listOf(leaf, inter, root), expectedChallenge = "nonce-123".toByteArray())
        assertTrue(r is AttestationVerifier.Verdict.Trusted, "应以第一个（leaf）扩展为准: $r")
    }

    @Test
    fun challengeMismatchRejected() {
        val (root, rootKey) = genCa()
        val v = AttestationVerifier(listOf(root))
        val leaf = genLeaf(root, rootKey, extension = hwExt)
        val r = v.verify(listOf(leaf, root), expectedChallenge = "wrong".toByteArray())
        assertTrue(r is AttestationVerifier.Verdict.Rejected && r.reason.contains("challenge"), r.toString())
    }

    @Test
    fun relayAppIdMismatchRejected() {
        val (root, rootKey) = genCa()
        val v = AttestationVerifier(listOf(root))
        val leaf = genLeaf(root, rootKey, extension = hwExt)
        val r = v.verify(listOf(leaf, root), expectedChallenge = "nonce-123".toByteArray(), expectedPackageName = "com.other.app")
        assertTrue(r is AttestationVerifier.Verdict.Rejected && r.reason.contains("relay"), r.toString())
    }

    // ---- 5.3 字段交叉 + 降级 ----

    @Test
    fun crossCheckDetectsBootloaderLie() {
        val (root, rootKey) = genCa()
        val v = AttestationVerifier(listOf(root))
        val unlockedExt = KeyDescriptionParser.buildTestExtension(
            challenge = "n".toByteArray(), securityLevel = KeyDescriptionParser.SecurityLevel.TRUSTED_ENVIRONMENT,
            deviceLocked = false, bootState = KeyDescriptionParser.VerifiedBootState.UNVERIFIED,
        )
        val leaf = genLeaf(root, rootKey, extension = unlockedExt)
        val r = v.verify(listOf(leaf, root))
        val d = (r as AttestationVerifier.Verdict.Trusted).description
        val anomalies = v.crossCheck(d, AttestationVerifier.CrossInput(claimedBootloaderLocked = true))
        assertTrue(anomalies.any { it.kind == "bootloader_lock_mismatch" || it.kind == "verified_boot_state" })
    }

    @Test
    fun strongBoxCapabilityMismatch() {
        val (root, rootKey) = genCa()
        val v = AttestationVerifier(listOf(root))
        val sbExt = KeyDescriptionParser.buildTestExtension(
            challenge = "n".toByteArray(), securityLevel = KeyDescriptionParser.SecurityLevel.STRONGBOX,
            deviceLocked = true, bootState = KeyDescriptionParser.VerifiedBootState.VERIFIED,
        )
        val leaf = genLeaf(root, rootKey, extension = sbExt)
        val d = (v.verify(listOf(leaf, root)) as AttestationVerifier.Verdict.Trusted).description
        val anomalies = v.crossCheck(
            d,
            AttestationVerifier.CrossInput(strongBoxCapableModels = mapOf("Pixel 8" to true), claimedModel = "Pixel 4a"),
        )
        assertTrue(anomalies.any { it.kind == "strongbox_capability_mismatch" })
    }

    @Test
    fun noAnchorsIsRejected() {
        val v = AttestationVerifier(emptyList())
        assertTrue(v.verify(listOf(genCa().first)) is AttestationVerifier.Verdict.Rejected)
    }

    // ---- A1: RKP-only 策略（Android 16 首发） ----

    @Test
    fun rkpOnlyDeviceRejectsRsaChain() {
        val (root, rootKey) = genCa()
        val v = AttestationVerifier(listOf(root))
        val leaf = genLeaf(root, rootKey, extension = hwExt)
        val r = v.verify(
            listOf(leaf, root),
            expectedChallenge = "nonce-123".toByteArray(),
            rkpPolicy = AttestationVerifier.RkpPolicy(firstApiLevel = 36),
        )
        assertTrue(r is AttestationVerifier.Verdict.Rejected, "RKP-only 设备上 RSA 链必须被拒: $r")
        assertTrue((r as AttestationVerifier.Verdict.Rejected).reason.contains("RKP"))
    }

    @Test
    fun nonRkpDeviceAcceptsRsaChain() {
        val (root, rootKey) = genCa()
        val v = AttestationVerifier(listOf(root))
        val leaf = genLeaf(root, rootKey, extension = hwExt)
        val r = v.verify(
            listOf(leaf, root),
            expectedChallenge = "nonce-123".toByteArray(),
            rkpPolicy = AttestationVerifier.RkpPolicy(firstApiLevel = 35),
        )
        assertTrue(r is AttestationVerifier.Verdict.Trusted, "非 RKP-only 设备 RSA 链正常通过: $r")
    }

    @Test
    fun nullFirstApiLevelSkipsRkpCheck() {
        val (root, rootKey) = genCa()
        val v = AttestationVerifier(listOf(root))
        val leaf = genLeaf(root, rootKey, extension = hwExt)
        val r = v.verify(
            listOf(leaf, root),
            expectedChallenge = "nonce-123".toByteArray(),
            rkpPolicy = AttestationVerifier.RkpPolicy(firstApiLevel = null),
        )
        assertTrue(r is AttestationVerifier.Verdict.Trusted, "first_api_level 未知时跳过 RKP 检查: $r")
    }
}

`feature/attestation/src/test/kotlin/com/detectany/feature/attestation/CrlStoreTest.kt`:

```kt
package com.detectany.feature.attestation

import kotlin.test.Test
import kotlin.test.assertEquals
import kotlin.test.assertTrue

/** 任务 5.2：CRL 解析/节流/离线快照。 */
class CrlStoreTest {

    private val body = """
        {"entries": {"aabbcc": {"status": "REVOKED", "reason": "KEY_COMPROMISE"}, "112233": {"status": "SUSPENDED"}}, "validUntil": "2026-12-01"}
    """.trimIndent()

    @Test
    fun parsesAndFiltersRevoked() {
        var fetched = 0
        val store = CrlStore(fetcher = { fetched++; body to 60L })
        val revoked = store.revokedSerials(nowMs = 1000L)
        assertEquals(setOf("aabbcc"), revoked)
        assertEquals(1, fetched)
        // 节流：窗口内第二次调用不再拉取
        store.revokedSerials(nowMs = 2000L)
        assertEquals(1, fetched)
        // force 越过节流
        store.revokedSerials(nowMs = 2000L, force = true)
        assertEquals(2, fetched)
    }

    @Test
    fun offlineFallsBackToSnapshot() {
        var online = true
        val snap = object : CrlStore.SnapshotStore {
            var saved: String? = null
            override fun load(): String? = saved
            override fun save(b: String) { saved = b }
        }
        val store = CrlStore(fetcher = { if (online) body to 60L else error("offline") }, snapshotStore = snap)
        // 第一次在线：成功并保存快照
        assertEquals(setOf("aabbcc"), store.revokedSerials(nowMs = 1000L))
        assertTrue(snap.saved != null)
        // 窗口过期后离线：快照兜底
        online = false
        assertEquals(setOf("aabbcc"), store.revokedSerials(nowMs = 999_999_999L))
    }
}

`feature/report/src/main/kotlin/com/detectany/feature/report/ReportPresenter.kt`:

```kt
package com.detectany.feature.report

import com.detectany.core.engine.DetectionEngine
import com.detectany.core.model.CollectorResult
import com.detectany.core.model.Confidence
import com.detectany.core.model.Finding
import kotlinx.serialization.Serializable
import kotlinx.serialization.json.Json

/**
 * 报告展示模型（任务 7.1/7.2）：UI 渲染的唯一数据源——「逐项与引擎输出一致」由
 * Presenter 直译保证（无二次加工），单测断言全量一致性。
 */
data class ReportModel(
    val tier: String,
    val riskScore: Int,
    val conclusive: Boolean,
    val categories: List<String>,
    val frameworkScores: Map<String, Int>,
    val antiDetectPresent: Boolean,
    val findings: List<FindingView>,
    val degradedCollectors: List<String>,
    val elapsedMs: Long,
) {
    data class FindingView(
        val ruleId: String,
        val collector: String,
        /** 强/中/弱 */
        val confidenceLabel: String,
        val category: String,
        val framework: String?,
        val detail: String,
        /** 证据回放：原始观测键值（已脱敏形态） */
        val raw: Map<String, String>,
    )
}

object ReportPresenter {

    fun present(report: DetectionEngine.ScanReport): ReportModel = ReportModel(
        tier = report.tier.name,
        riskScore = report.verdict.riskScore,
        conclusive = report.verdict.conclusive,
        categories = report.verdict.categories.map { it.name },
        frameworkScores = report.verdict.frameworkScores,
        antiDetectPresent = report.verdict.antiDetectPresent,
        findings = report.collectorResults.flatMap { r -> r.findings }.map { it.toView() },
        degradedCollectors = report.degradedCollectors.map { it.name },
        elapsedMs = report.elapsedMs,
    )

    private fun Finding.toView() = ReportModel.FindingView(
        ruleId = ruleId,
        collector = collector.name,
        confidenceLabel = when (confidence) {
            Confidence.A -> "强"
            Confidence.B -> "中"
            Confidence.C -> "弱"
        },
        category = category.name,
        framework = framework,
        detail = detail,
        raw = raw.mapValues { Redactor.redactValue(ruleId, it.key, it.value) },
    )
}

/**
 * 出端脱敏（specs/evidence-report「呈现与出端分离」）：
 * 属性类观测只出哈希；路径/socket 名等特征条目自身可出；其余值截断。
 */
object Redactor {
    private val HASH_KEYS = setOf("valueHash")

    fun redactValue(ruleId: String, key: String, value: String): String = when {
        key in HASH_KEYS -> value // 本就为哈希
        ruleId.startsWith("prop.") && key == "key" -> value.hashCode().toString()
        value.length > 96 -> value.take(96) + "…"
        else -> value
    }

    /** detail 中出现的属性键明文同样以哈希形态替换（specs：出端仅哈希化属性键）。 */
    fun redactDetail(ruleId: String, detail: String, raw: Map<String, String>): String {
        if (!ruleId.startsWith("prop.")) return detail
        val plainKey = raw["key"] ?: return detail
        return detail.replace(plainKey, "#${plainKey.hashCode()}")
    }
}

/**
 * 结构化导出（任务 7.3）：完整证据向量 + 脱敏；JSON 文本由调用方落盘/分享。
 */
object ReportExporter {

    @Serializable
    data class ExportedReport(
        val exportedAtMs: Long,
        val appVersion: String,
        val schemaVersion: Int = 1, // 2026-11-10 审计：导出格式演进保护（消费方可按版本分支）
        val tier: String,
        val riskScore: Int,
        val conclusive: Boolean,
        val categories: List<String>,
        val frameworkScores: Map<String, Int>,
        val evidenceVector: List<String>,
        val findings: List<ExportedFinding>,
        val degradedCollectors: List<String>,
        val elapsedMs: Long,
    ) {
        @Serializable
        data class ExportedFinding(
            val ruleId: String,
            val collector: String,
            val confidence: String,
            val category: String,
            val framework: String?,
            val detail: String,
            val raw: Map<String, String>,
        )
    }

    fun export(
        report: DetectionEngine.ScanReport,
        appVersion: String = "0.1.0",
        nowMs: Long = System.currentTimeMillis(),
    ): String {
        val findings = report.collectorResults.flatMap { r -> r.findings }
        val model = ExportedReport(
            exportedAtMs = nowMs,
            appVersion = appVersion,
            tier = report.tier.name,
            riskScore = report.verdict.riskScore,
            conclusive = report.verdict.conclusive,
            categories = report.verdict.categories.map { it.name },
            frameworkScores = report.verdict.frameworkScores,
            evidenceVector = report.verdict.evidenceVector,
            findings = findings.map {
                ExportedReport.ExportedFinding(
                    ruleId = it.ruleId,
                    collector = it.collector.name,
                    confidence = it.confidence.name,
                    category = it.category.name,
                    framework = it.framework,
                    detail = Redactor.redactDetail(it.ruleId, it.detail, it.raw),
                    raw = it.raw.mapValues { v -> Redactor.redactValue(it.ruleId, v.key, v.value) },
                )
            },
            degradedCollectors = report.degradedCollectors.map { it.name },
            elapsedMs = report.elapsedMs,
        )
        return Json { prettyPrint = true }.encodeToString(ExportedReport.serializer(), model)
    }
}

`feature/report/src/main/kotlin/com/detectany/feature/report/SignatureImport.kt`:

```kt
package com.detectany.feature.report

import com.detectany.data.signature.ApplyResult
import com.detectany.data.signature.BundledSignatureDb
import com.detectany.data.signature.SignatureStore
import com.detectany.data.signature.SignedUpdate
import kotlinx.serialization.json.Json

/**
 * 特征文件手动导入（任务 7.3）：通道无关——同一 SignatureStore.apply 验证/装载/生效路径，
 * 与在线更新完全等价。
 */
class SignatureImport(private val store: SignatureStore) {

    fun import(jsonText: String): Result = runCatching {
        val update = BundledSignatureDb.json.decodeFromString(SignedUpdate.serializer(), jsonText)
        when (val r = store.apply(update)) {
            is ApplyResult.Applied -> Result.Applied(update.version)
            is ApplyResult.Rejected -> Result.Rejected(r.reason)
        }
    }.getOrElse { Result.Rejected("malformed update file: ${it.message ?: it.javaClass.simpleName}") }

    sealed interface Result {
        data class Applied(val version: Long) : Result
        data class Rejected(val reason: String) : Result
    }
}

```

`feature/report/src/test/kotlin/com/detectany/feature/report/ReportTest.kt`:

```kt
package com.detectany.feature.report

import com.detectany.core.model.Collector
import com.detectany.core.model.CollectorId
import com.detectany.core.model.CollectorResult
import com.detectany.core.model.Confidence
import com.detectany.core.model.Finding
import com.detectany.core.model.ScanInput
import com.detectany.core.model.ScanTier
import com.detectany.core.engine.DetectionEngine
import com.detectany.core.rules.BundledRuleSet
import com.detectany.core.rules.RulesEngine
import com.detectany.data.signature.BundledSignatureDb
import com.detectany.data.signature.SignatureStore
import java.security.KeyPairGenerator
import java.security.Signature
import java.util.Base64
import kotlin.test.Test
import kotlin.test.assertEquals
import kotlin.test.assertTrue

/** 任务 7.2/7.3 验证：证据回放与引擎输出一致 + 导出脱敏断言 + 导入等价在线更新。 */
class ReportTest {

    private class FixedCollector : Collector {
        override val id = CollectorId.C8_BEHAVIOR
        override fun collect(input: ScanInput) = CollectorResult(
            id,
            CollectorResult.Status.OK,
            listOf(
                Finding("behavior.ksu_supercall", CollectorId.C8_BEHAVIOR, Confidence.B, Finding.Category.ROOT_KERNEL, "ksu", "reboot 双魔数命中", mapOf("fd" to "anon_inode:[ksu_driver]")),
                Finding("prop.init_svc_magisk", CollectorId.C3_PROPERTY, Confidence.C, Finding.Category.ROOT_USERSPACE, "magisk", "属性命中 init.svc.magisk_p92=running", mapOf("key" to "init.svc.magisk_p92", "valueHash" to "12345")),
            ),
        )
    }

    private fun engineReport(): DetectionEngine.ScanReport =
        DetectionEngine(listOf(FixedCollector()), RulesEngine(BundledRuleSet.ruleSet))
            .scan(ScanInput(ScanTier.L2, 35, 35, "6.1.75", "arm64-v8a"))

    @Test
    fun presenterMatchesEngineOutputExactly() {
        val report = engineReport()
        val model = ReportPresenter.present(report)
        val engineFindings = report.collectorResults.flatMap { it.findings }
        // 逐项一致：数量、ruleId、采集器、置信、原始观测键
        assertEquals(engineFindings.size, model.findings.size)
        engineFindings.zip(model.findings).forEach { (f, v) ->
            assertEquals(f.ruleId, v.ruleId)
            assertEquals(f.collector.name, v.collector)
            assertEquals(f.raw.keys, v.raw.keys)
        }
        assertEquals(report.verdict.riskScore, model.riskScore)
        assertEquals(report.verdict.conclusive, model.conclusive)
        assertEquals(report.verdict.evidenceVector.size, model.findings.map { it.ruleId }.distinct().size)
        // 证据级别标签
        assertTrue(model.findings.any { it.confidenceLabel == "中" && it.ruleId == "behavior.ksu_supercall" })
    }

    @Test
    fun exportIsRedactedAndComplete() {
        val json = ReportExporter.export(engineReport(), appVersion = "test", nowMs = 42L)
        // 完整证据向量 + 结论字段
        assertTrue(json.contains("\"evidenceVector\""))
        assertTrue(json.contains("behavior.ksu_supercall"))
        assertTrue(json.contains("\"riskScore\""))
        // 脱敏断言：属性键不以明文出现（仅哈希），valueHash 本就为哈希可出现
        assertTrue(!json.contains("init.svc.magisk_p92"), "属性键明文不得出现在导出")
        assertTrue(json.contains("12345"), "valueHash 为哈希形态可出现")
        // 解析回读完整
        val back = kotlinx.serialization.json.Json.decodeFromString(
            ReportExporter.ExportedReport.serializer(), json,
        )
        assertEquals(2, back.findings.size)
        assertEquals(42L, back.exportedAtMs)
    }

    @Test
    fun signatureImportEquivalentToOnlineApply() {
        val (pub, priv) = run {
            val kp = KeyPairGenerator.getInstance("Ed25519").genKeyPair()
            Base64.getEncoder().encodeToString(kp.public.encoded) to kp.private
        }
        val store = SignatureStore(verifier = com.detectany.data.signature.SignatureVerifier(pub))
        val delta = BundledSignatureDb.db.copy(version = BundledSignatureDb.db.version + 1)
        val sig = Signature.getInstance("Ed25519").apply {
            initSign(priv)
            update(com.detectany.data.signature.UpdateCodec.payloadBytes(delta))
        }
        val json = com.detectany.data.signature.BundledSignatureDb.json.encodeToString(
            com.detectany.data.signature.SignedUpdate.serializer(),
            com.detectany.data.signature.SignedUpdate(BundledSignatureDb.db.version + 1, null, delta, Base64.getEncoder().encodeToString(sig.sign())),
        )
        // 手动导入
        val imported = SignatureImport(store).import(json)
        assertTrue(imported is SignatureImport.Result.Applied && imported.version == BundledSignatureDb.db.version + 1L, imported.toString())
        // 与在线路径同构：同一 store 状态推进语义（导入即 apply）
        assertEquals(BundledSignatureDb.db.version + 1L, store.current().version)
    }

    @Test
    fun signatureImportRejectsGarbage() {
        val store = SignatureStore(verifier = null)
        val r = SignatureImport(store).import("{ not json")
        assertTrue(r is SignatureImport.Result.Rejected)
    }
}

#!/bin/sh

##############################################################################
#
#   Gradle start up script for POSIX generated by Gradle.
#
#   Important for running:
#
#   (1) You need a POSIX-compliant shell to run this script. If your /bin/sh is
#       noncompliant, but you have some other compliant shell such as ksh or
#       bash, then to run this script, type that shell name before the whole
#       command line, like:
#
#           ksh Gradle
#
#       Busybox and similar reduced shells will NOT work, because this script
#       requires all of these POSIX shell features:
#         * functions;
#         * expansions «$var», «${var}», «${var:-default}», «${var+SET}»,
#           «${var#prefix}», «${var%suffix}», and «$( cmd )»;
#         * compound commands having a testable exit status, especially «case»;
#         * various built-in commands including «command», «set», and «ulimit».
#
#   Important for patching:
#
#   (2) This script targets any POSIX shell, so it avoids extensions provided
#       by Bash, Ksh, etc; in particular arrays are avoided.
#
#       The "traditional" practice of packing multiple parameters into a
#       space-separated string is a well documented source of bugs and security
#       problems, so this is (mostly) avoided, by progressively accumulating
#       options in "$@", and eventually passing that to Java.
#
#       Where the inherited environment variables (DEFAULT_JVM_OPTS, JAVA_OPTS,
#       and GRADLE_OPTS) rely on word-splitting, this is performed explicitly;
#       see the in-line comments for details.
#
#       There are tweaks for specific operating systems such as AIX, CygWin,
#       Darwin, MinGW, and NonStop.
#
#   (3) This script is generated from the Groovy template
#       https://github.com/gradle/gradle/blob/HEAD/platforms/jvm/plugins-application/src/main/resources/org/gradle/api/internal/plugins/unixStartScript.txt
#       within the Gradle project.
#
#       You can find Gradle at https://github.com/gradle/gradle/.
#
##############################################################################

# Attempt to set APP_HOME

# Resolve links: $0 may be a link
app_path=$0

# Need this for daisy-chained symlinks.
while
    APP_HOME=${app_path%"${app_path##*/}"}  # leaves a trailing /; empty if no leading path
    [ -h "$app_path" ]
do
    ls=$( ls -ld "$app_path" )
    link=${ls#*' -> '}
    case $link in             #(
      /*)   app_path=$link ;; #(
      *)    app_path=$APP_HOME$link ;;
    esac
done

# This is normally unused
# shellcheck disable=SC2034
APP_BASE_NAME=${0##*/}
# Discard cd standard output in case $CDPATH is set (https://github.com/gradle/gradle/issues/25036)
APP_HOME=$( cd -P "${APP_HOME:-./}" > /dev/null && printf '%s
' "$PWD" ) || exit

# Use the maximum available, or set MAX_FD != -1 to use that value.
MAX_FD=maximum

warn () {
    echo "$*"
} >&2

die () {
    echo
    echo "$*"
    echo
    exit 1
} >&2

# OS specific support (must be 'true' or 'false').
cygwin=false
msys=false
darwin=false
nonstop=false
case "$( uname )" in                #(
  CYGWIN* )         cygwin=true  ;; #(
  Darwin* )         darwin=true  ;; #(
  MSYS* | MINGW* )  msys=true    ;; #(
  NONSTOP* )        nonstop=true ;;
esac

CLASSPATH=$APP_HOME/gradle/wrapper/gradle-wrapper.jar


# Determine the Java command to use to start the JVM.
if [ -n "$JAVA_HOME" ] ; then
    if [ -x "$JAVA_HOME/jre/sh/java" ] ; then
        # IBM's JDK on AIX uses strange locations for the executables
        JAVACMD=$JAVA_HOME/jre/sh/java
    else
        JAVACMD=$JAVA_HOME/bin/java
    fi
    if [ ! -x "$JAVACMD" ] ; then
        die "ERROR: JAVA_HOME is set to an invalid directory: $JAVA_HOME

Please set the JAVA_HOME variable in your environment to match the
location of your Java installation."
    fi
else
    JAVACMD=java
    if ! command -v java >/dev/null 2>&1
    then
        die "ERROR: JAVA_HOME is not set and no 'java' command could be found in your PATH.

Please set the JAVA_HOME variable in your environment to match the
location of your Java installation."
    fi
fi

# Increase the maximum file descriptors if we can.
if ! "$cygwin" && ! "$darwin" && ! "$nonstop" ; then
    case $MAX_FD in #(
      max*)
        # In POSIX sh, ulimit -H is undefined. That's why the result is checked to see if it worked.
        # shellcheck disable=SC2039,SC3045
        MAX_FD=$( ulimit -H -n ) ||
            warn "Could not query maximum file descriptor limit"
    esac
    case $MAX_FD in  #(
      '' | soft) :;; #(
      *)
        # In POSIX sh, ulimit -n is undefined. That's why the result is checked to see if it worked.
        # shellcheck disable=SC2039,SC3045
        ulimit -n "$MAX_FD" ||
            warn "Could not set maximum file descriptor limit to $MAX_FD"
    esac
fi

# Collect all arguments for the java command, stacking in reverse order:
#   * args from the command line
#   * the main class name
#   * -classpath
#   * -D...appname settings
#   * --module-path (only if needed)
#   * DEFAULT_JVM_OPTS, JAVA_OPTS, and GRADLE_OPTS environment variables.

# For Cygwin or MSYS, switch paths to Windows format before running java
if "$cygwin" || "$msys" ; then
    APP_HOME=$( cygpath --path --mixed "$APP_HOME" )
    CLASSPATH=$( cygpath --path --mixed "$CLASSPATH" )

    JAVACMD=$( cygpath --unix "$JAVACMD" )

    # Now convert the arguments - kludge to limit ourselves to /bin/sh
    for arg do
        if
            case $arg in                                #(
              -*)   false ;;                            # don't mess with options #(
              /?*)  t=${arg#/} t=/${t%%/*}              # looks like a POSIX filepath
                    [ -e "$t" ] ;;                      #(
              *)    false ;;
            esac
        then
            arg=$( cygpath --path --ignore --mixed "$arg" )
        fi
        # Roll the args list around exactly as many times as the number of
        # args, so each arg winds up back in the position where it started, but
        # possibly modified.
        #
        # NB: a `for` loop captures its iteration list before it begins, so
        # changing the positional parameters here affects neither the number of
        # iterations, nor the values presented in `arg`.
        shift                   # remove old arg
        set -- "$@" "$arg"      # push replacement arg
    done
fi


# Add default JVM options here. You can also use JAVA_OPTS and GRADLE_OPTS to pass JVM options to this script.
DEFAULT_JVM_OPTS='-Dfile.encoding=UTF-8 "-Xmx64m" "-Xms64m"'

# Collect all arguments for the java command:
#   * DEFAULT_JVM_OPTS, JAVA_OPTS, JAVA_OPTS, and optsEnvironmentVar are not allowed to contain shell fragments,
#     and any embedded shellness will be escaped.
#   * For example: A user cannot expect ${Hostname} to be expanded, as it is an environment variable and will be
#     treated as '${Hostname}' itself on the command line.

set -- \
        "-Dorg.gradle.appname=$APP_BASE_NAME" \
        -classpath "$CLASSPATH" \
        org.gradle.wrapper.GradleWrapperMain \
        "$@"

# Stop when "xargs" is not available.
if ! command -v xargs >/dev/null 2>&1
then
    die "xargs is not available"
fi

# Use "xargs" to parse quoted args.
#
# With -n1 it outputs one arg per line, with the quotes and backslashes removed.
#
# In Bash we could simply go:
#
#   readarray ARGS < <( xargs -n1 <<<"$var" ) &&
#   set -- "${ARGS[@]}" "$@"
#
# but POSIX shell has neither arrays nor command substitution, so instead we
# post-process each arg (as a line of input to sed) to backslash-escape any
# character that might be a shell metacharacter, then use eval to reverse
# that process (while maintaining the separation between arguments), and wrap
# the whole thing up as a single "set" statement.
#
# This will of course break if any of these variables contains a newline or
# an unmatched quote.
#

eval "set -- $(
        printf '%s\n' "$DEFAULT_JVM_OPTS $JAVA_OPTS $GRADLE_OPTS" |
        xargs -n1 |
        sed ' s~[^-[:alnum:]+,./:=@_]~\\&~g; ' |
        tr '\n' ' '
    )" '"$@"'

exec "$JAVACMD" "$@"

```

`gradlew.bat`:

```bat
@rem
@rem Copyright 2015 the original author or authors.
@rem
